atha navamaṃ maṇḍalam ||
navame' maṇḍale' sasānuvākāḥ |
tatra prathame'nuvāke caturviṃśatisaṃkhyākāni sūktāni |
tatra 'svādiṣṭhayā' iti daśarcaṃ prathamaṃ sūktam |
atrānukramyate—'svādiṣṭhayā daśa madhucchandāḥ' iti |
vaiśvāmitro madhucchandā ṛṣiḥ |
prāvastaprīyaparibhāṣayā gāyatrī chandaḥ |
'navamaṃ maṇḍalaṃ pāvamānaṃ saumyam' (anu.) iti vacanāt pavamānaguṇaviśiṣṭaḥ somo devatā |
grāvastotre'rddhasūktasya prāguttamāyā idamādikaṃ sarvaṃ pāvamānaṃ vikalpenāpanīyam |
sūtritaṃ ca—'prete vadantvityarddhaṃ prāguttamāyā2 ā va ṛñse pra vo prāvāṇa iti sūktayorantaropariṣṭātpurastādvā |
pāvamānīropyā yathārthamā3 vā agraha- ṇāt3' (āśva. śrau. 5. 12) iti |
upālakarmaṇi maṇḍalādigrahaṇa ādyā |
sūtraṃ pūrvamevodāhṛtam ||
svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayā parvasva soma dhārayā | indrāya pātave sutaḥ || 1 || svādiṣṭhayā | madiṣṭhayā | parvasva | soma | dhārayā | indrāya | pātave | sutaḥ || 1 ||
he soma indrāya pātave pātuṃ sutaḥ abhiṣutaḥ tvaṃ svādiṣṭhayā svādutamayā madiṣṭhayā atiśayena mādayityā dhārayā pavasva kṣara ||
rakṣohā viśvacarṣaṇibhira yoniṃmyauhatam | dṛṇā sadhasthamārsadata || 2 || rakṣaḥhā | viśvacarṣaṇiḥ | abhi | yonim | arthaḥ | ahtam | dṛṇā | sadhastham | ā | asadat || 2 ||
rakṣohā rakṣāṃ hantā viśvacarṣaṇiḥ viśvasya draṣṭā somaḥ |
ayohataṃ hiraṇyena hatam |
tathā ca brāhmaṇaṃ-'hiraṇyapāṇirabhiṣuṇoti' iti |
dṛṇā droṇakalaśenādhiṣavaṇaphalakābhyāṃ vā sadhasthaṃ sahasthānaṃ yonim abhiṣavaṇasthānam abhi āsadat abhyāsīdati ||
varivodhārtamo bhava maṃhiṣṭho vṛtrahantamaḥ | parṣi rādho maghonām || 3 || varivaḥ'dhārtamaḥ | bhava | maṃhiṣṭhaḥ | vṛtrahantamaḥ | parṣi | rādhaḥ | maghonām || 3 ||
he soma tvaṃ varivodhārtamaḥ atiśayena dhanānāṃ dātā bhava |
'vedo varivaḥ' iti dhananāmasu pāṭhāt |
maṃhiṣṭhaḥ dātṛttamaśca bhava |
sarvadātṛtvamatrocyate ityapunarūktiḥ |
vṛtrahantamaḥ atiśayena śatrūṇāṃ hantā bhava |
kiṃca maghonāṃ dhanavatāṃ śatrūṇāṃ rādhaḥ dhanaṃ ca parṣi asmabhyaṃ prayaccha ||

tamīmanvīḥ samarya ā gṛbhṇanti yoṣaṇī darśa | svasāraḥ pāyeṃ divi || 7 || tam | īm | anvīḥ | ma'samarya | ā | gṛbhṇanti | yoṣaṇāḥ | darśa | svasāraḥ | pāyeṃ | divi ||7|| samarye samanupye yajñe pāyeṃ divi sauye'hani yoṣaṇāḥ chiyaḥ svasāraḥ svayaṃ saranyo daśasaṃkhyākāḥ anvīḥ aṇvyo'ṅgulayaḥ | 'amvavaḥ anvayaḥ' ityaṅgulināmasu pāṭhāt | tamīṃ tamenaṃ somam ā gṛbhṇanti āgṛhṇanti ||
tamīṃ hinvantyugravo dharmanti bākuraṃ dārtam | tridhātu vāraṇaṃ madhu || 8 || tam | īm | hinvanti | agravaḥ | dharmanti | bākuram | dārtam | tri'dhātu | vāraṇam | madhu ||8|| tamīm enaṃ somam amuvaḥ aṅgulayaḥ hinvanti abhipavadeśaṃ prati prerayanti | prerayitvā ca bākuraṃ bhāsamānaṃ dārta datisadṛśāṃśumenaṃ somaṃ dhamanti abhipuṇvanti | yadyapi dhamatirabhipavakarmā na bhavati tathāpyaucityādatrābhipavaparo bhaviṣyati | tadetat somātmakaṃ madhu vastu tridhātu tristhā- nam | droṇakalaśa ābhavanīyaḥ pūtabhṛditi tridhātavaḥ | vāraṇaṃ śatrūṇāṃ vārakaṃ ca bhavati ||

^viṣṭambhaḥ ^divaḥ svargasya ^dharaṇaḥ dhārakaśca ^asmayuḥ asmatkāmaḥ ^somaḥ ^apsu udakeṣu ^mamṛje mṛjyate ^pavitre abhiṣicyata ityarthaḥ ||

arcikradad vṛṣā harīrmahān mitro na darśataḥ | saṃ sūryeṇa rocate || 6 || arcikradat | vṛṣā | hariḥ | mahān | mitraḥ | na | darśataḥ | sam | sūryeṇa | rocate || 6 ||
^vṛṣā kāmānāṃ varṣakaḥ ^hariḥ haritavarṇaḥ ^mahān sarvottamaḥ ^mitro ^na yathā sakhā tadvat ^darśataḥ darśanīyo yo'yaṃ somaḥ ^acikradat śabdaṃ karoti so'yaṃ somaḥ ^sūryeṇa saha ^rocate divi prakāśate ||
girasta indra ojasā mamṛjyante apsuyavaḥ | yābhirmadāya śumbhase || 7 || giraḥ | te | indra iti | ojasā | mamṛjyante | apsuyavaḥ | yābhiḥ | madāya | śumbhase || 7 ||
he ^indo ^te tava ^ojasā balena ^apsuyavaḥ karmecchāsambandhinyaḥ tāḥ ^giraḥ stutayaḥ ^mamṛjyante śodhyante ^yābhiḥ gīrbhistvaṃ ^madāya kṣaran ^śumbhase alaṃkriyase ||
taṃ tvā madāya ghṛṣvaya u lokakṛtnumīmahe | tatra praśastayo mahīḥ || 8 || tam | tvā | madāya | ghṛṣvaye | ūṃ iti | loka'kṛtnum | īmahe | tatra | pra'śastayaḥ | mahīḥ || 8 ||
he soma yasya ^tava ^praśastayaḥ praśaṃsāḥ ^mahīḥ mahatyaḥ ^ghṛṣvaya ^u tvatprasādācchatrūṇāṃ gharṣaṇaśīlāya yajamānāyaiva ^lokakṛtnum uttamasya lokasya kartāraṃ ^taṃ tvāṃ somaṃ ^madāya ^īmahe vayaṃ yācāmahe ||
asmabhyamindavindrayurmadhvaḥ pavasva dhārayā | parjanyo vṛṣṭimāniva || 9 || asmabhyam | indo iti | indra'yuḥ | madhvaḥ | pavasva | dhārayā | parjanyaḥ | vṛṣṭimān'iva || 9 ||
he ^indo soma ^indrayuḥ indrakāmastvaṃ ^madhvaḥ madakarasyāmṛtasya ^dhārayā ^parjanyo ^vṛṣṭimāniva yathā varṣavān parjanyo meghastathā ^asmabhyaṃ medhātithibhyaḥ ^pavasva kṣara ||
gopā indo nṛṣā asyaśvasā vājasā uta | ātmā yajñasya pūrvyaḥ || 10 || go'pāḥ | indo iti | nṛ'sāḥ | asi | aśva'sāḥ | vāja'sāḥ | uta | ātmā | yajñasya | pūrvyaḥ || 10 ||
he ^indo ^yajñasya ^pūrvyaḥ pratnaḥ ^ātmā ātmabhūtastvaṃ ^gopāḥ asmabhyaṃ gavāṃ dātā ^asi bhavasi |

‘eṣa devaḥ ’ iti daśacaṃ tṛtīyaṃ sūktamājīgartaḥ śunaḥśepasyārṣaṃ gāyatraṃ pavamānasomadevatākam |
anukrāntaṃ ca- ‘eṣaḥ śunaḥśepaḥ ’ iti |
ukto viniyogaḥ ||

2. gha-mu- saṃ rocate |

4. ga-meghaḥ kṣarati tathā |
eṣa devo amartyaḥ parṇavīriva dīyati | ābhi droṇānyāsadam || 1 || eṣaḥ | devaḥ | amartyaḥ | parṇavīḥ'iva | dīyati | ābhi | droṇāni | ā'sadam || 1 || ^devaḥ dyotamānaḥ ^amartyaḥ maraṇarahitaḥ ^eṣaḥ somaḥ ^droṇāni droṇakalaśāni ^abhi abhilakṣya ^āsadam āsādanārtha ^parṇavīriva yathā pakṣī tathā vegena ^dīyati gacchati ||
eṣa devo vipā kṛto'nti hrāṃsī dhāvati | parvamāno adābhyaḥ || 2 || eṣaḥ | devaḥ | vipā | kṛtaḥ | anti | hrāṃsī | dhāvati | parvamānaḥ | adābhyaḥ || 2 || ^vipā aṅgulyā | 'atharvo vipaḥ' ityaṅgulināmasu pāṭhāt | ^kṛtaḥ abhidhutaḥ ^eṣaḥ somaḥ ^devaḥ ^pavamānaḥ kṣaran ^adābhyaḥ kenāpyahiṃsitaśca san ^hrāṃsī śatrūn ^ati ^dhāvati hantumabhigacchati ||
eṣa devo vipanyubhiḥ parvamāna ṛtāyubhiḥ | harivārjāya mṛjyate || 3 || eṣaḥ | devaḥ | vipanyu'bhiḥ | parvamānaḥ | ṛtāyu'bhiḥ | hariḥ | vājāya | mṛjyate || 3 || ^pavamānaḥ kṣaran ^eṣaḥ somaḥ ^devaḥ ^vipanyubhiḥ stotṛbhiḥ ^ṛtāyubhiḥ yajñakāmaiḥ ^hariḥ aśva iva ^vājāya saṃgrāmārtha ^mṛjyate stutibhiralaṃkriyate ||
eṣa viśvāni vāryā śūro yannirva satvabhiḥ | parvamānaḥ sipāsati || 4 || eṣaḥ | viśvāni | vāryā | śūraḥ | yan'iva | satva'bhiḥ | parvamānaḥ | sipāsati || 4 || ^pavamānaḥ kṣaran ^śūraḥ vīraḥ ^eṣaḥ somau devaḥ ^viśvāni sarvāṇi ^vāryā varaṇīyāni dhanāni ^satvabhiḥ balaiḥ ^yacciva gacchacciva ^sipāsati asmadarthaṃ saṃbhaktumicchati ||
eṣa devo rathyati parvamāno daśasyati | āviṣkṛṇoti vagvanum || 5 || eṣaḥ | devaḥ | rathyati | parvamānaḥ | daśasyati | āviḥ | kṛṇoti | vagvanum || 5 || ^pavamānaḥ^3 kṣaran ^eṣaḥ somaḥ^0devaḥ ^rathyati asmadīyaṃ yāgaṃ pratyāgamanāya rathaṃ kāmayate | ^daśasyati āgatya cāsmabhyamabhilaṣitaṃ prayacchati^4 | ^vagvanuṃ śabdam ^āviṣkṛṇoti | abhipya- māṇaḥ prakaṭayati || ||20||
eṣa viprairbhistuto'po devo vi gāhete | dadhadratnāni dāśuṣe || 6 || eṣaḥ | vipraiḥ | abhi'stutaḥ | apaḥ | devaḥ | vi | gāhete | dadhat | ratnāni | dāśuṣe || 6 || ^vipraiḥ medhāvibhiḥ stotṛbhiḥ ^abhistutaḥ paritaḥ stutaḥ ^eṣaḥ somaḥ ^devaḥ ^dāśuṣe haviṣāṃ pradātre yajamānāya ^ratnāni ramaṇīyāni dhanāni ^dadhat dhārayan prayacchan ^apaḥ vasatīvarīḥ ^vi ^gāhete praviśati ||
eṣa divaṃ vi dhāvati tiro rajāṃsi dhārayāṃ | parvamānaḥ kanikradat || 7 || eṣaḥ | divam | vi | dhāvati | tiraḥ | rajāṃsi | dhārayā | parvamānaḥ | kanikradat || 7 ||

ᵛdhārayā ᵛpavamānaḥ kṣaran ᵛeṣaḥ somaḥ ᵛkanikadat abhiṣūyamāṇaḥ śabdaṃ kurvan ᵛrajāṃsi lokān ᵛtiraḥ tiraskurvan yāgāt ᵛdivaṃ svargaṃ ᵛvi ᵛdhāvati gacchati || eṣa divaṃ vyāsarattiro rajāṃsyaspṛṣṭaḥ | parvamānaḥ svaśvvaraḥ || 8 || eṣaḥ | divam | vi | ā | asrat | tiraḥ | rajāṃsi | aspṛṣṭaḥ | parvamānaḥ | su'adhvaraḥ ||8|| ᵛpavamānaḥ kṣaran ᵛeṣaḥ somaḥ ᵛsvadhvaraḥ suyajñaḥ ᵛaspṛṣṭaḥ kenāpyahiṃsitaśca san ᵛrajāṃsi lokān ᵛtiraḥ tiraskurvan yajñāt ᵛdivaṃ prati ᵛvyāsarat visarati gacchati ||
eṣa pratanena janmanā devo devebhyaḥ sutaḥ | hariṃ pavitrai arṣati || 9 || eṣaḥ | pratanena | janmanā | devaḥ | devebhyaḥ | sutaḥ | hariḥ | pavitrai | arṣati || 9 || ᵛhariḥ haritavarṇaḥ ᵛdevaḥ dyotamānaḥ ᵛeṣaḥ somaḥ ᵛpratanena purāṇena ᵛjanmanā jananena ᵛdevebhyaḥ devārthaṃ ᵛsutaḥ abhiṣutaḥ san ᵛpavitrai sthātum ᵛarṣati gacchati ||
eṣa u sya puruvrato jajñāno janayanniṣaḥ | dhārayā pavate sutaḥ || 10 || eṣaḥ | ū~ iti | syaḥ | puruvrataḥ | jajñānaḥ | janayan | iṣaḥ | dhārayā | pavate | sutaḥ || 10 || ᵛsyaḥ ᵛeṣa ᵛu soma eva ᵛpuruvrataḥ bahukarmā ᵛjajñānaḥ jāyamāna eva ᵛiṣaḥ annāni ᵛjanayan utpādayan ᵛsutaḥ abhiṣutaḥ san ᵛdhārayā ᵛpavate kṣarati || ||21||
‘sanā ca’ iti daśarcaṃ caturthaṃ sūktamāṅgirasakulasya hiraṇyastūpasyārṣaṃ gāyatraṃ pavamānasoma-devatākam |
anukrāntaṃ ca- ‘sana hiraṇyastūpaḥ ’ iti |
ukto viniyogaḥ ||
sanā ca soma jerṣi ca parvamāna mahi śravaḥ | arthā no vasyasaskṛdhi || 1 || sane | ca | soma | jerṣi | ca | parvamāna | mahi | śravaḥ | artha | naḥ | vasyasaḥ | kṛdhi || 1 || he ᵛmahi ᵛśravaḥ mahadanna ᵛpavamāna ᵛsoma ᵛsana | asmadāge yajanīyān devān bhaja | ᵛjerṣi ᵛca | yāgavidhvaṃsakāriṇo rākṣasāṃśca jaya | ᵛatha devān prāpya rākṣasāṃśca jitavānantaraṃ ᵛnaḥ asmān ᵛvasyasaḥ śreyasaḥ ᵛkṛdhi kuru | śrayāṃ'smabhyaṃ dehītyarthaḥ ||
sanā jyotiḥ sanā svarvviśvā ca soma saubhagā | arthā no vasyasaskṛdhi ||2|| sane | jyotiḥ | sane | svaḥ | viśvā | ca | soma | saubhagā | artha | naḥ | vasyasaḥ | kṛdhi ||2|| he ᵛsoma tvaṃ ᵛjyotiḥ tejaḥ ᵛsana asmabhyaṃ prayaccha | api ca ᵛsvaḥ ᵛsvargaṃ ᵛsana asmabhyaṃ dehi | ᵛviśvā viśvāni ᵛsaubhagā saubhāgyāni ᵛca ᵛsana | siddhamanyat ||
sanā dakṣamuta kratumapa soma mṛdho jahi | arthā no vasyasaskṛdhi || 3 || sane | dakṣam | uta | kratum | apa | soma | mṛdhaḥ | jahi | artha | naḥ | vasyasaḥ | kṛdhi || 3 || he ᵛsoma tvaṃ ᵛdakṣaṃ balaṃ ᵛsana asmabhyaṃ dehi | ᵛuta api ca ᵛkratuṃ prajñānaṃ sana | ᵛmṛdhaḥ hiṃsakān śatrūṃśca ᵛapa ᵛjahi māraya | siddhamanyat ||
1. ga-ta-bha- ‘tiraḥ’ nāsti | 2. ga-gha-ta-bha- ‘uṃ’ nāsti | 3. ga-sanā | 4. ta-bhara.4-śreyaḥ | 5. ga-sa-ma- ‘svaḥ’ nāsti |

yeṣāṃ yajamānānāṃ madhva: somasya ūrmaya: taraṅgā: mitrāvaruṇā mitrāvaruṇau devau bhagaṃ bhagasthaṃ devaṃ ca prati pavante kṣaranti. te yajamānā: asya somasyemaṃ somaṃ vidānā: jānantaḥ śakmabhi: survai: saṃgacchanta iti śeṣa: ||
asmabhyaṃ rodasī rayiṃ madhvo vājasya sātaye | śravo vastūni saṃ jitam || 9 || asmabhyam | rodasī iti | rayim | madhva: | vājasya | sātaye | śrava: | vastūni | sam | jitam ||9||
he rodasī dyāvāpṛthivyau yuvāṃ madhva: devānāṃ modayitu: vājasya somātmakasyānnasya sātaye lābhāya asmabhyaṃ kāyyapāsitebhya: kāyyapadevalebhyo vā rayiṃ dhanaṃ śrava: annaṃ ca vastūni vāmakānyanyānpyapi paścādīni dhanāni saṃ jitaṃ saṃjayatam |
prayacchatamityartha: ||
29 ||
‘ete somā: ’ iti navarcamaṣṭamaṃ sūktam |
ṛṣyādyā: pūrvavat |
anukrāntaṃ ca- ‘ete somā: ’ iti |
ukta: sūktaviniyoga: |
grāvastotre gāṇagārimatenābhirūpakaraṇe some mṛjyamāne ‘mṛjanti tvā’ ityepā |
sūtritaṃ ca- ‘athāparamabhirūpaṃ kuryāditi gāṇagārirā pyāyasva sametu ta iti tisro mṛjanti tvā daśa kṣipa: ’ ( āśva. śrau. 5.12 ) iti ||
ete somā abhi priyamindrasya kāmamakṣaran | vardhanto asya vīryam || 1 || ete | somā: | abhi | priyam | indrasya | kāmam | akṣaran | vardhantaḥ | asya | vīryam ||1||
ete abhiśutā ime somā: asya indrasya vīryaṃ śaktiṃ vardhantaḥ vardhayantaḥ indrasya kāmaṃ kāmyaṃ priyaṃ prītikaraṃ rasam abhi akṣaran abhipavante abhyavarṣan ||
punānāsaścamūṣado gacchanto vāyumaśvinā | te no dhāntu suvīryam || 2 || punānāsa: | camūṣada: | gacchantaḥ | vāyum | aśvinā | te | na: | dhāntu | suvīryam ||2||
te prasiddhā: somā: punānāsa: punānā abhipūyamāṇā: camūṣada: camaseṣu sīdantaḥ vāyum aśvinā aśvinau ca gacchantaḥ prāpnuvantaḥ na: asmabhyaṃ suvīryaṃ śobhanavīryaṃ dhāntu dhārayantu |
prayacchantvityartha: ||
1. ga-ta-bha- ‘aśvinau’ nāsti |
2. gha-āsagacchaṃta |
3. ga-mādagitu: |
indra̭sya॒ soma̭ rādha̭se punā॒no hārdi̭ codaya | ṛ॒tasya॒ yoni̭māsa॒dam || 3 || indra̭sya | soma̭ | rādha̭se | punā॒naḥ | hārdi̭ | codaya | ṛ॒tasya̭ | yoni̭m | ā॒sa॒dam || 3 || he ᵥsoma ᵥpunānaḥ abhīṣūyamāṇaḥ ᵥhārdi abhilapitasvam ᵥindrasya ᵥrādhase saṃrādhanāya ᵥṛtasya yajñasya ᵥyoni sthānam ᵥāsadaṃ yathendra āsīdati tathendraṃ ᵥcodaya preraya ||
mṛ॒janti॒ tvā॒ daśa̭ kṣi॒po hi॒nvanti॒ sapta̭ dhī॒taya̭ḥ | anu॒ viprā̭ amādiṣuḥ || 4 || mṛ॒janti̭ | tvā॒ | daśa̭ | kṣi॒paḥ | hi॒nvanti̭ | sapta̭ | dhī॒taya̭ḥ | anṷ | viprā̭ḥ | amādiṣuḥ ||4|| he soma ᵥtvā tvāṃ ᵥdaśa daśasaṃkhyākāḥ ᵥkṣipaḥ aṅgulayaḥ | ' triśaḥ' kṣipaḥ ' ityaṅgulināmasu pāṭhāt | ᵥmṛjanti paricaranti | ᵥsapta saptasaṃkhyākāḥ ᵥdhītayaḥ hotrākāśra tvā tvāṃ ᵥhinvanti svasvavyāpāraiḥ prīṇayanti | ᵥviprāḥ medhāvinaśca tvām ᵥanu ᵥamādiṣuḥ anumādayanti ||
de॒vebhya̭stvā ma॒dāya̭ kaṃ sṛ॒jāna̭ma॒ti mḙṣyaḥ | saṃ gobhi̭rvāsayāmasi || 5 || de॒vebhya̭ḥ | tvā॒ | ma॒dāya̭ | kam | sṛ॒jāna̭m | a॒ti | mḙṣyaḥ | sam | gobhi̭ḥ | vā॒sa॒yā॒ma॒si॒ ||5|| he soma ᵥmeṣyaḥ avelairmāni ᵥkam udakaṃ ca ᵥati abhi ᵥsṛjānaṃ ᵥtvā tvāṃ ᵥdevebhyaḥ devānām | vibhaktivyatyayaḥ | ᵥmadāya madārthaṃ ᵥgobhiḥ govikāraiḥ payobhiḥ ᵥsaṃ ᵥvāsayāmasi saṃvāsayāmaḥ || || 30 ||
punā॒naḥ kala̭śeṣvā va॒strāṇya̭ru॒ṣo ha॒riḥ | pari̭ ga॒vyānvya̭nyata || 6 || punā॒naḥ | kala̭śeṣu | ā | va॒strāṇi̭ | a॒ru॒ṣaḥ | ha॒riḥ | pari̭ | ga॒vyāni̭ | a॒nyata || 6 || ᵥpunānaḥ pūyamānaḥ ᵥkalaśeṣu kumbheṣu niṣicyamānaḥ ᵥaruṣaḥ ārocamānaḥ ᵥhariḥ haritavarṇaḥ somaḥ ᵥgavyāni dadhyādīni ᵥvastrāṇīva ᵥpari ᵥavyata paryācchādayati ||
ma॒dhona॒ ā pa̭vasva no॒ jahi॒ viśvā॒ apa̭ dvi॒ṣaḥ | indo॒ sakhā̭yamā vi॒śa || 7 || ma॒dhona̭ḥ | ā | pa॒va॒sva॒ | na॒ḥ | jahi̭ | viśvā̭ | apa̭ | dvi॒ṣaḥ | indo॒ iti̭ | sakhā̭yam | ā | vi॒śa || 7 || he ᵥindo soma ᵥmadhonaḥ dhanavataḥ ᵥnaḥ asmān prati ᵥpavasva kṣara | ᵥviśvaḥ viśvān ᵥdviṣaḥ dveṣan ᵥapa ᵥjahi māraya ca | ᵥsakhāyaṃ priyamindram ᵥā ᵥviśa āpnuhi ca ||
vṛ॒ṣṭiṃ di॒vaḥ pari̭ sra॒va yu॒mnaṃ pṛ̭thi॒vyā a॒dhi | sa॒haḥ na̭ḥ soma॒ pṛtsṷ dhāḥ || 8 || vṛ॒ṣṭim | di॒vaḥ | pari̭ | sra॒va | yu॒mnam | pṛ̭thi॒vyāḥ | a॒dhi | sa॒haḥ | na॒ḥ | soma̭ | pṛtsṷ | dhāḥ || 8 ||
1. ga-ta1.2.4.7-bha4-viśaḥ; ta6-bha1.3-' triśaḥ ' nāsti |
2. ga-ityarthaḥ ati; gha- kamudakaṃ cātyabhi; ta1-kaṃ uvakaṃ cādanyabhi; tara.6-kaṃ uvakaṃ nyabhi; ta4.5- - - - da abhi; ta7-kamudakaṃ tvadanyabhi; bha1.2-da ati; bha3-daśāpavitramati; bha4-abhi; mu-kamudakaṃ cāti |
ṣaṣṭho'ṣṭakaḥ
15
he soma tvaṃ divaḥ dyulokāt vṛṣṭiṃ varṣa pari svava varṣa |
pṛthivyā adhi pṛthivyām |
adhiḥ saptamyarthānuvādaḥ |
śuṣmam annaṃ cotpādayati śeṣaḥ |
naḥ asmākaṃ sahaḥ balaṃ ca pṛtsu saṃgrāmeṣu dhāḥ dhehi ||
nucakṣa̭saṃ tvā va॒yami̭ndrapītaṃ sva॒rvida̭m | bha॒kṣīma̭hi pra॒jāmi̭ṣam || 9 || nu'cakṣa̭sam | tvā | va॒yam | indra̭'pītam | svaḥ'vida̭m | bha॒kṣīma̭hi | pra॒jām | i̭ṣam ||9||
he soma nucakṣasaṃ nṛṇāṃ draṣṭāraṃ svarvidaṃ sarvajñam indrapītaṃ indreṇa pītaṃ tvā tvāṃ pibantaḥ vayaṃ kāśyapāsitāḥ kāśyapadevalā vā prajāṃ putrādikām iṣam annaṃ ca bhakṣīmahi bhajemahi ||
31||
‘pari priyā’ iti navarcaṃ navamaṃ sūktam |
ṛṣyādyāḥ pūrvavat |
‘pari priyā’ ityanukrāntam |
ukto viniyogaḥ ||
pari̭ pri॒yā di॒vaḥ ka॒virva̭yāṃsi na॒ptyohi̭taḥ | su॒vāno̭ yāti ka॒vikra̭tuḥ || 1 || pari̭ | pri॒yā | di॒vaḥ | ka॒viḥ | va॒yāṃsi̭ | na॒ptyoḥ | hi॒taḥ | su॒vāna̭ḥ | yā॒ti | ka॒vi'kra̭tuḥ ||1||
kaviḥ medhāvī kavikratuḥ krāntaprajñaḥ krāntakarmā vā somaḥ naptyoḥ adhiṣavaṇaphalakayoḥ hitaḥ nihitaḥ suvānaḥ abhiṣūyamāṇaḥ divaḥ dyulokasya pari priyā atipriyāṇi vayāṃsi prāṣṇaḥ |
tathā ca mantravarṇaḥ—‘vayāṃsi’ iyenā atiyathaḥ parvatānāṃ kakubhaḥ’ iti |
yāti gacchati ||
prapra kṣa॒yāya̭ pa॒nyase॒ janā̭ya ju॒ṣṭo a̭dru॒he | vī॒tayḙ a॒rdha ca̭ni॒ṣṭhayā̭ || 2 || pra'pra | kṣa॒yāya̭ | pa॒nyasḙ | janā̭ya | ju॒ṣṭaḥ | a̭dru॒he | vī॒tayḙ | a॒rdha | ca॒ni॒ṣṭhayā̭ || 2 ||
he soma prapra atyantaṃ kṣayāya tava nivāsabhūtāya adruhe adroghe ca panyase stotre janāya manuṣyāya vītī vītyai bhakṣaṇāya juṣṭaḥ paryāptastvaṃ caniṣṭhayā ajñavattamayā dhārayā arṣa yāgaṃ prati gaccha ||
sa su॒nurmā॒tarā̭ śu॒cirjo̭to jā॒te a̭rocayat | ma॒hānma॒hī ṛ॒tāvṛ̭dhā || 3 || saḥ | su॒nuḥ | mā॒tarā̭ | śu॒ciḥ | jā॒taḥ | jā॒te | i॒ti̭ | a̭rocayat | ma॒hān | ma॒hī | i॒ti̭ | ṛ॒ta'vṛ̭dhā || 3 ||
jātaḥ utpannaḥ śuciḥ viśuddhaḥ mahān haviruttamaḥ saḥ somākhyaḥ sunuḥ putraḥ mahī mahyau ṛtāvṛdhā yajñasya vardhayitavyau jāte viśvasya janayitryau mātarā ātmano mātarau dyāvā pṛthivyau arocayat rocayati dīpayati ||
sa sa॒pta dhī॒tibhi̭rhi॒to nṛ॒bhyo a̭ji॒nvada̭dru॒haḥ | yā e॒kama̭rṣi vā॒vṛdhṷḥ || 4 || saḥ | sa॒pta | dhī॒ti'bhi̭ḥ | hi॒taḥ | nṛ॒bhyaḥ | a̭ji॒nvat | a̭dru॒haḥ | yāḥ | e॒kam | arṣi | vā॒vṛdhṷḥ ||4||
1. ga-ta-bha- ‘indreṇa pītaṃ tvā’ nāsti |
2. ta-kaśyapa |
3. ga-bha- ‘vayāṃsi’ nāsti |
^yāḥ nadyaḥ yam ^ekaṃ mukhyaṃ somam ^akṣi akṣīṇaṃ ^vaktruḥ vardhayanti ^saḥ somaḥ ^dhītibhiḥ^1 aṅgulibhiḥ |
^raśanā dhītayaḥ' ityaṅgulināmasu pāṭhāt |
^hitaḥ nihitaḥ san ^atuhaḥ drohavarjitāḥ ^sasa saptasaṃkhyākāḥ ^nadyaḥ nadīḥ^2 ^ajinnavat prīṇayati ||
tā abhi santamstṛtaṃ mahe yuvānamā dadhuḥ | indumindra tarva vrate || 5 || tāḥ | abhi | santam | astṛtam | mahe | yuvānam | ā | dadhuḥ | indum | indra | tarva | vrate ||5||
he ^indra ^tava tvadīye ^vrate karmaṇi ^tāḥ aṅgulyaḥ |
pūrvatra dhītibhiḥ ityaṅgulināmapāttatvāt tacchabdena parāmarśaḥ |
^santaṃ vidyamānam ^astṛtam ahiṃsitaṃ ^yuvānaṃ nityataruṇam ^induṃ somaṃ ^mahe mahate'bhipavādilakṣaṇāya karmaṇe ^abhi ^ā ^dadhuḥ ||
32 ||
abhi vahnirmartyaḥ sapta paśyati vārvahaḥ | krivirdevīrtarapayat || 6 || abhi | vahniḥ | amartyaḥ | sapta | paśyati | vārvahaḥ | kriviḥ | devīḥ | atarpayat || 6 ||
yaḥ ^vahniḥ yajñasya dhuro voḍhā ^amartyaḥ maraṇarahitaḥ ^vāvahaḥ devānāṃ tuseratyantaṃ voḍhā ca somaḥ ^sasa nadīḥ ^paśyati so'yaṃ ^kriviḥ kūparūpeṇa^3 pūrṇo'vasthitaḥ san ^devīḥ ^nadīḥ ^atarpayat tarpayati ||
avā kalpeṣu naḥ pumastamā~si soma yodhyā | tārni punāna jaṅghanaḥ || 7 || arva | kalpeṣu | naḥ | pumaḥ | tamā~si | soma | yodhyā | tārni | punāna | jaṅghanaḥ || 7 ||
he ^pumaḥ puman soma ^kalpeṣu kalpanīyeṣvahaḥsu ^naḥ asmān ^ava rakṣa |
api ca ^punāna he pavamāna ^soma tvaṃ ^yodhyā yodhanīyāni ^tamā~si rakṣāṃsi yāni ^tāni ^jaṅghanaḥ nāśaya ||
nū narvyase navīyase sūktārya sādhayā pathaḥ | pratnavadraucayā rucaḥ || 8 || nu | narvyase | navīyase | mu'uktārya | sādhaya | pathaḥ | pratna'vrat | rocaya | rucaḥ || 8 ||
he soma ^navyase navyāya nūtanāya ^narvīyase stutyāyāsmākaṃ ^sūktāya ^pathaḥ mārgān ^nu kṣipraṃ ^sādhaya abhigaccha |
api ca ^pratnavat yathāpūrvaṃ ^rucaḥ svadīptīḥ ^rocaya prakāśaya ||
parvamāna mahi śravo gāmaśvaṃ rāsi vīravat | sanā medhāṃ sanā rvaḥ || 9 || parvamāna | mahi | śravaḥ | gām | aśvam | rāsi | vīra'vat | sanā | medhām | sanā | svaḥ | rvite svaḥḥ || 9 ||
he ^pavamāna soma yastvaṃ ^vīravat putravat ^mahi mahat ^śravaḥ annaṃ ^gāṃ ca ^aśvaṃ ca ^rāsi asmabhyaṃ prayacchasi sa tvaṃ ^medhāṃ ^sana asmabhyaṃ prayaccha |
api ca ^svaḥ yadasmadabhilaṣitaṃ tat sarvaṃ ^sana dehi ||

‘pra svānāsaḥ’ iti navarcaṃ daśamaṃ sūktam |
ṛṣyādyāḥ pūrvavat |
‘pra svānāsaḥ’ ityanukrāntam |
ukto viniyogaḥ ||
1. ga-ta-bha4-dhītayaḥ |
2. ta1.4.5.6.7-bha4--nadībhiḥ; tara-nadīva |
3. ga-kūpavat |
pra svānāso rathā irvanto na śravasyavaḥ | somāso raye akramuḥ || 1 || pra | svānāsaḥ | rathāḥ | iva | arvanataḥ | na | śravasyavaḥ | | somāsaḥ | raye | akramuḥ || 1 || ᵛpra ᵛsvānāsaḥ: abhivavavelāyāmuparaveṣu śabdaṃ kurvantaḥ ᵛsomāsaḥ: somāḥ ᵛrathā iva yathā śabdaṃ kurvanto rathāḥ ᵛarvanato ᵛna yathā śabdaṃ kurvanto'śvāḥ tathā ᵛśravasyavaḥ: śatrubhyo'nnamicchantaḥ ᵛraye yajamānānāṃ dhanāya ᵛakramuḥ: āgacchan ||
hinvānāso rathā iva dadhanvire gabhastyoḥ | bharāsaḥ kāriṇāmiva || 2 || hinvānāsaḥ | rathāḥ | iva | dadhanvire | gabhastyoḥ | | bharāsaḥ | kāriṇām | iva || 2 || somāḥ ᵛrathā iva yathā rathāstathā ᵛhinvānāsaḥ: hinvānāḥ yāgadeśaṃ prati gacchantaḥ ᵛbharāsaḥ: ᵛkāriṇāmiva yathā bhāravāhino bāhradhīyante tathā ᵛgabhastyoḥ: ṛtvijāṃ bāhvoḥ | ᵛgabhastī bāhū ' iti bāhunāmasu pāṭhāt | ᵛdadhanvire dhīyante ||
rājāno na praśastibhiḥ somāso gobhirañjate | yajño na sapta dhātṛbhiḥ || 3 || rājānaḥ | na | praśastibhiḥ | somāsaḥ | gobhiḥ | añjate | | yajñaḥ | na | sapta | dhātṛbhiḥ ||3|| ᵛsomāsaḥ: somāḥ ᵛpraśastibhiḥ praśastābhiḥ2 stutirūpābhirvāgbhiḥ ᵛrājāno ᵛna yathā rājānaḥ ᵛsapta ᵛdhātṛbhiḥ sapta hotrābhiḥ ᵛyajño ᵛna yathā ca yajñastathā ᵛgobhiḥ govikāraiḥ payobhiḥ ᵛañjate ajyante | saṃskriyanta ityarthaḥ ||
pari suvānāsa indavo madāya brahmaṇa girā | sutā arṣanti dhārayā || 4 || pari | suvānāsaḥ | indavaḥ | madāya | brahmaṇa | girā | | sutāḥ | arṣanti | dhārayā || 4 || ᵛsuvānāsaḥ: suvānā abhiṣūyamāṇāḥ ᵛindavaḥ: somāḥ ᵛbrahmaṇa mahatyā ᵛgirā stutirūpayā vācā ᵛsutāḥ: abhiṣutāḥ santaḥ ᵛmadāya madārthaṃ ᵛdhārayā ᵛpari ᵛarṣanti parito gacchanti ||
āpānāso vivasvato janantaṃ upaso bhagam | sūrā aṇvaṃ vi tanvate || 5 || āpānāsaḥ | vivasvataḥ | janantaḥ | upasaḥ | bhagam | | sūrāḥ | aṇvaṃ | vi | tanvate ||5|| ᵛvivasvataḥ: indrasya ᵛāpānāsaḥ: āpānabhūtāḥ ᵛupasaḥ ᵛbhagāṃ ᵛjanantaḥ janayantaḥ ᵛsūrāḥ: sarantaḥ somāḥ ᵛaṇvaṃ ᵛvi ᵛtanvate | abhivavavelāyāmuparaveṣu śabdaṃ kurvanti || || 34 ||
apa dvārā matīnāṃ pratnā ṛṇvanti kāravaḥ | vṛṣṇo harasa āyavaḥ || 6 || apa | dvārā | matīnām | pratnā | ṛṇvanti | kāravaḥ | | vṛṣṇaḥ | harase | āyavaḥ || 6 || ᵛmatīnāṃ stutīnāṃ ᵛkāravaḥ kalatra ṛtvijaḥ ᵛpratnāḥ purāṇāḥ ᵛvṛṣṇaḥ: sevakasya somasya ᵛharasa āharasa āhatāraśca ᵛāyavaḥ: manuṣyāḥ ᵛdvārā yajñasya dvārāṇi ᵛapa ᵛṛṇvanti ||
samīcīnāsa āsate hotāraḥ saptajāmayaḥ | padamekasya piprataḥ || 7 || samīcīnāsaḥ | āsate | hotāraḥ | saptajāmayaḥ | | padam | ekasya | piprataḥ || 7 || 1. ta-bha-mu-bhāravāhanāṃ | 2. ga-bha-'praśastābhiḥ' nāsti; gha-śastābhiḥ | 3. ga-gha-ta-'somasya' nāsti | ṛ. 4-2
^samīcīnāsaḥ samīcīnāḥ ^jāmayaḥ jāmisadṛśāḥ ^ekasya somasya ^padaṃ sthānaṃ ^piprataḥ pūyantaḥ ^sasa ^hotāraḥ sasa hotrakāḥ ^āsate yajña upaviśanti ||
nābhā nābhiṃ na ā dade cakṣuḥśritsūrye sacā| kuverapatyamā duhe || 8 || nābhā | nābhim | naḥ | ā | dade | cakṣuḥ | śrit | sūrye | sacā | kuveḥ | arpatyam | ā | duhe ||8||
^nābhiṃ yajñasya nābhibhūtaṃ somaṃ ^naḥ asmākaṃ ^nābhā nābhau aham ^ā ^dade |
pibāmītyarthaḥ |
pītasomānāsmākaṃ ^cakṣuśrita cakṣurapi ^sūrye ^sacā saṃgataṃ bhavati |
kiṃca ^kaveḥ krāntakarmaṇaḥ somasya ^apatyam aṃśuma ^ā ^duhe āpūrayāmi ||
abhi priyā divaspadamadhvaryubhirguhā hitam| sūraḥ paśyati cakṣasā || 9 || abhi | priyā | divaḥ | padam | adhvaryu'bhiḥ | guhā | hitam | sūraḥ | paśyati | cakṣasā ||9||

ā yo viśvāni vāryā vasūni hastayordadhe | madeṣu sarvadhā asi || 4 || ā | yaḥ | viśvāni | vāryā | vasūni | hastayoḥ | dadhe | madeṣu | sarva'dhāḥ | asi ||4|| ᵥyaḥ somaḥ ᵥviśvāni ᵥvāryā varaṇīyāni ᵥvasūni dhanāni ᵥstotuḥ ᵥhastayoḥ ᵥā ᵥdadhe karoti | prayacchatītyarthaḥ | ᵥmadeṣu ᵥsarvadhā ᵥasi iti | athavā ᵥsa śuṣmī ityuttaratra saṃbandhaḥ ||

1. ga-sarvadhātā dātā asi bhaktasi; ta1.6-sarvadhā sarvadā asi sarvasya dhātā dātā vā bhavati; ta3.4.5-sarvadhātā dātā cā bhavati; ta7-sarvasya dātā dātā vā bhavati; bha4-sarvadhā asi sarvasya dhātā dātā bhavasi; su-sarvadhā sarvadā asi sarvasya dhātā dātā vā bhavasi |

uktarītyā mahān saḥ somaḥ śuṣmī balavān punānaḥ pūyamānaḥ san ā adhikvadat śabdaṃ karoti | atra sarvatra yathocitamuttarapādo neyaḥ || 8 || ‘yat soma’ iti saptamekonaviṃśaṃ sūktam | ṛṣyādyāḥ pūrvavat | ‘yat soma’ ityanukrāntam | gato viniyogaḥ ||
yatsoma citramukthyaṃ divyaṃ pārthivaṃ vasu | tannaḥ punāna ā bhara || 1 || yat | soma | citram | ukthyam | divyam | pārthivam | vasu | tat | naḥ | punānaḥ | ā | bhara ||1||
he soma yat citraṃ cāyanīyam ukthyaṃ stutyaṃ divyaṃ divi bhavaṃ pārthivaṃ pṛthivī- saṃbaddhaṃ ca vasu dhanamasti tat naḥ asmabhyaṃ punānaḥ pūyamānaḥ san ā bhara āhara ||
yuvaṃ hi sthaḥ sva̭rpatī indraśca soma go̭patī | īśānā pipyataṃ dhiyaḥ || 2 || yuvam | hi | sthaḥ | svaḥpatī iti svaḥ'patī | indraḥ | ca | soma | gopatī iti go'patī | īśānā | pipyatam | dhiyaḥ || 2 ||
he soma tvam indraśca yuvam hi yuvāṃ khalu sva̭rpatī sarvasya svāminau sthaḥ bhavataḥ |
tathā gopatī gavāṃ pālakau īśānā īśvarau mantau dhiyaḥ asmadīyāni karmāṇi pipyataṃ pyāyayatam ||
vṛṣā punāna āyṷṣu stanayannadhi barhiṣi | hariḥ sa॒yonimāsadat || 3 || vṛṣā | punānaḥ | āyṷṣu | stanayan | adhi | barhiṣi | hariḥ | sa॒yonim | ā | asadat ||3||
vṛṣā kāmānāṃ varṣakaḥ somaḥ āyṷṣu manuṣyeṣvadhvaryādiṣu punānaḥ pūyamānaḥ san stana- yan śabdaṃ kurvan adhi barhiṣi |

yatsomo̭ vā॒jama̭rṣati śa॒taṃ dhārā̭ a॒psyurva̭: | indra̭sya sa॒khyamāvi̭śan || 2 || yat | somaḥ | vā'jam | arṣati | śatam | dhārāḥ | apsyurvaḥ | indrasya | sakhyam | ā'viśan ||2||
ᵥyat yadā ᵥapsyuvaḥ karmakāmāḥ ᵥśataṃ śatasaṃkhyākāḥ ᵥdhārāḥ somasya dhārāḥ ᵥindrasya ᵥsakhyaṃ sakhitvam ᵥāviśan prāpnuvanti tadā ᵥsomaḥ ᵥvājam annam ᵥarṣati gamayati |
asmabhyaṃ prayacchatītyarthaḥ ||
a॒bhi tvā॒ yoṣaṇo॒ daśa॒ jāra॒ṃ na ka॒nyā̭nūṣata | mṛ॒jyase॒ soma॒ sāta̭ye || 3 || abhi | tvā | yoṣaṇaḥ | daśa | jāram | na | kanyā | anūṣata | mṛjyase | soma | sātaye ||3||
he ᵥsoma ᵥtvā tvāṃ yāḥ ᵥdaśa daśasaṃkhyākāḥ ᵥyoṣaṇaḥ aṅgulayaḥ ᵥkanyā pitṛmatī kanyā ᵥjāraṃ ᵥna yathā priyamabhiśabdayate tadvat ᵥabhi ᵥanūṣata abhiśabdayante tābhiḥ ᵥsātaye asmākaṃ dhanasya lābhāya ᵥmṛjyase indrārthaṃ śodhyase ||
tvamindrā̭ya viṣṇave॒ svādṷrindo॒ pārī̭ sava | nṛ॒nsto॒tṛnpā̭hyaṃhasaḥ || 4 || tvam | indrāya | viṣṇave | svāduḥ | indo iti | pārī | sava | nṛn | stotṛn | pāhi | aṃhasaḥ ||4||
he ᵥindo soma ᵥsvāduḥ priyarasasvam ᵥindrāya indrārthaṃ ᵥviṣṇave viṣṇave ca ᵥpari ᵥsava parikṣara |
ᵥnṛn karmaṇāṃ netṛn ᵥstotṛn tvadviṣayāṇāṃ stutīnāṃ kartṛn ᵥaṃhasaḥ duritāt ᵥpāhi rakṣa ca ||

'pra te dhārāḥ' iti caturṛcaṃ trayastriṃśaṃ sūktam |
ṛṣyādyāḥ pūrvavat |
'pra te dhārāḥ' ityanukrāntam |
ukto viniyogaḥ ||
pra tḙ dhārā॒ asa̭śca॒to di॒vo na ya̭nti vṛ॒ṣṭaya̭: | a॒cchā vā॒jaṃ sa̭hasriṇam || 1 || pra | te | dhārāḥ | asaścataḥ | divaḥ | na | yanti | vṛṣṭayaḥ | acchā | vājam | sahasriṇam ||1||
he soma ᵥte tava ᵥasaścataḥ saṅgarahitāḥ ᵥdhārāḥ ᵥsahasriṇam aparimitasaṃkhyākaṃ ᵥvājam annam ᵥacchā asmadarthaṃ ᵥpra ᵥyanti pragacchanti |
tatra dṛṣṭāntaḥ |
ᵥdivaḥ ᵥna vṛṣṭayaḥ |
yathā dyulokāt varṣadhārā niḥsaṅgāḥ prajānāmaparimitamannaṃ ᵥprayacchanti tadvadityarthaḥ ||
a॒bhi pri॒yāṇi॒ kāvyā॒ viśvā॒ cakṣā̭ṇo arṣati | ha॒riṃ tu॒jñāna ā̭yu॒dhā || 2 || abhi | priyāṇi | kāvyā | viśvā | cakṣāṇaḥ | arpati | harim | tujñānaḥ | āyudhā ||2||
ᵥhariḥ haritavarṇaḥ somaḥ ᵥviśvā viśvāni ᵥpriyāṇi devānāṃ prītikarāṇi ᵥkāvyā kāvyāni karmāṇi ᵥcakṣāṇaḥ paśyan ᵥāyudhā svakīyānyāyudhāni ᵥtujñānaḥ rākṣasān prati prerayanna ᵥabhi ᵥarṣati yāgaṃ prati gacchati ||

sa marma̭jñāna āyṷbhirbho rā॒jaiva su॒vrata̭ḥ | śye॒no na va॒ṃsu sī̭dati || 3 || saḥ | marma̭jñānaḥ | āyṷbhiḥ | irmeḥ | rā॒jā'iva | su॒vrata̭ḥ | śye॒naḥ | na | va॒ṃsu | sī̭dati ||3||

tara̭tsa mandī॒ dhāva̭ti॒ dhārā̭ su॒tasyāndha̭saḥ | tara̭tsa mandī॒ dhāva̭ti || 1 || tara̭t | saḥ | mandī̭ | dhāva̭ti | dhārā̭ | su॒tasya̭ | a॒ndhasa̭ḥ | tara̭t | saḥ | mandī̭ | dhāva̭ti ||1||

ayṷkta॒ sūra॒ eta̭śa॒ṃ parva̭māno॒ manā̭vadhi | a॒nta॒ri॒kṣeṇa॒ yā॒tavḙ || 8 || ayṷkta | sūra̭: | eta̭śam | parva̭mānaḥ | manā̭vadhi | a॒nta॒ri॒kṣeṇa | yā॒tavḙ || 8 || pavamānaḥ pūyamānaḥ somaḥ manāvādhi | manurmanuṣyaḥ | tasmin manuṣya ityarthaḥ | antarikṣeṇa yātave gantuṃ sūraḥ sūryasya etaśam aśvam | etagavaḥ etaśaḥ ityaśvanāmasu pāṭhāt | ayukta yukte ||
u॒ta tyā ha॒rito̭ da॒śa sū॒rau ayṷkta॒ yā॒tavḙ | i॒ndu॒ri॒ndra i̭ti॒ bruva̭n || 9 || u॒ta | tyāḥ | ha॒rita̭ḥ | da॒śa | sū॒rau | ayṷkta | yā॒tavḙ | i॒nduḥ | i॒ndraḥ | iti̭ | bruva̭n || 9 || uta api ca induḥ somaḥ indra iti bruvan tyāḥ tāḥ daśa daśasaṃkhyākāḥ haritaḥ diśaḥ prati yātave gantuṃ sūraḥ sūryasyaitaśam ayukta yunakti ||
pa॒rī॒to vā॒yavḙ su॒taṃ gi॒ra i॒ndrāya̭ ma॒tsara̭m | a॒vyo vā॒reṣṷ siścata || 10 || pa॒rī॒taḥ | vā॒yavḙ | su॒tam | gi॒raḥ | i॒ndrāya̭ | ma॒tsara̭m | a॒vyaḥ | vā॒reṣṷ | siścata || 10 || he giraḥ stotāraḥ yūyaṃ vāyave vāyvartham indrāya indrārthaṃ ca sutam abhiputaṃ matsaraṃ madakaraṃ somam itaḥ abhiṣavadeśādudṛtya anyaḥ aveḥ vāreṣu vāleṣu pari siścata || 31 ||
parva̭māna॒vidā̭ ra॒yima॒smabhya॒ṃ soma̭ du॒stara̭m | yo dū॒ṇāśaṷ va॒nuṣya̭tā || 11 || parva̭māna | vidā̭ | ra॒yim | a॒smabhya̭m | soma̭ | du॒stara̭m | yaḥ | duḥ'naśa̭ḥ | va॒nuṣya̭tā || 11 || he pavamāna soma yaḥ rayiḥ vanuṣyatā hiṃsakena śatruṇā dūṇāśaḥ nāśayitumaśakyaḥ taṃ śatrubhiḥ dustaraṃ rayiṃ dhanam asmabhyaṃ vidā dehi ||
a॒bhya̭rṣa sa॒hasri̭ṇa॒ṃ rayi॒ṃ goma̭nta॒maśvi̭nam | a॒bhi vā॒jamṷta śrava̭ḥ || 12 || a॒bhi | a॒rṣa | sa॒hasri̭ṇam | ra॒yim | go'ma̭ntam | a॒śvi̭nam | a॒bhi | vā॒jam | u॒ta | śrava̭ḥ || 12 || he soma tvaṃ sahasriṇaṃ bahusaṃkhyākaṃ gomantaṃ gobhiryuktam aśvinam aśvavantaṃ rayiṃ dhanam abhyarpa asmānabhigamaya | uta api ca vājaṃ balaṃ śravaḥ annaṃ cāsmān abhi gamaya ||
somo̭ de॒vo na॒ sūryo̭'dri॒bhiḥ pa̭vate॒ suta̭ḥ | da॒dhāna̭ḥ ku॒laśe॒ rasa̭m || 13 || soma̭ḥ | de॒vaḥ | na | sūrya̭ḥ | a॒dri॒bhiḥ | pa॒vatḙ | suta̭ḥ | da॒dhāna̭ḥ | ku॒laśḙ | rasa̭m || 13 || devo na deva iva sūryaḥ rocamānaḥ somaḥ adribhiḥ grāvabhiḥ sutaḥ ābheputaḥ san kalaśe droṇakalaśe rasaṃ dadhānaḥ dhārayan pavate dhāraya, kṣarati ||
e॒te dhā॒mānyā̭ryā॒ śukā̭ ṛ॒tasya̭ dhā॒rayā̭ | vā॒jaṃ goma̭nta॒makṣa̭ran || 14 || e॒te | dhā॒māni̭ | ā॒ryā̭ | śukā̭ | ṛ॒tasya̭ | dhā॒rayā̭ | vā॒jam | go'ma̭ntam | a॒kṣa̭ran || 14 || ete abhiputāḥ ime śukrāḥ dīptāḥ somāḥ āryā āryāṇāṃ śreṣṭhānāṃ yajamānānāṃ dhāmāni gṛhān prati gomantaṃ gobhiryuktaṃ vājam annam ṛtasya udakasya dhārayā akṣaran kṣaranti ||
su॒tā i॒ndrāya̭ va॒jriṇe॒ somā̭so da॒dhyā̭śiraḥ | pa॒vi॒tra॒matya̭kṣaran || 15 || su॒tāḥ | i॒ndrāya̭ | va॒jriṇḙ | somā̭saḥ | da॒dhi'ā̭śiraḥ | pa॒vi॒tram | a॒ti | a॒kṣa̭ran || 15 ||
vvajriṇe vajravate vindrāya indrārthaṃ vsutāḥ abhiṣutāḥ vdadhyāśiraḥ |

pra soma̭ madhumattamo rāye arṣa pavitra ā | mado yo devavītamaḥ || 16 || pra | soma̭ | madhumat'tamaḥ | rāye | arṣa | pavitrai | ā | madaḥ | yaḥ | deva'vītamaḥ || 16 || he vsoma te tava vyaḥ vmadaḥ rasaḥ vmadhumattamaḥ atyantaṃ madhumān vdevavītamaḥ atiśayena devakāmaśca taṃ rasaṃ vrāye asmākaṃ dhanārthaṃ vpavitre vpra vā varṣa dhārayā kṣara ||
tamīṃ mṛjantyāyavo harīṃ nadīṣu vājinam | indumindrāya matsaram || 17 || tam | īmiti | mṛjanti | āyavaḥ | harīm | nadīṣu | vājinam | indum | indrāya | matsaram || 17 || vharīṃ haritavarṇaṃ vvājinaṃ balinaṃ vmatsaraṃ madakaraṃ vtaṃ pavamānam vīm enam vinduṃ somam vāyavaḥ manuṣyā ṛtvijaḥ vnadīṣu vasatīvarīṣu vmṛjanti mārjayanti ||
ā pavasva hiraṇyavadvātsoma vīravat | vājaṃ gomantamā bhara || 18 || ā | pavasva | hiraṇya'vat | aśva'vat | soma | vīra'vat | vājam | go'mantam | ā | bhara || 18 || he vsoma tvaṃ vhiraṇyavat suvarṇopetam vaśvavat aśvayuktaṃ ca vvīravat putrādyupetaṃ ca dhanam vā vpavasva asmān prati kṣara | api ca vgomantaṃ paśubhirupetaṃ vvājam annam vā vbhara asmabhyamāhara ||
pari vāje na vājyumamyo vāreṣu siścata | indrāya madhumattamam || 19 || pari | vāje | na | vāja'yum | avyaḥ | vāreṣu | siścata | indrāya | madhumat'tamam || 19 || vvājayuṃ yuddhakāmaṃ vmadhumattamam atiśayena madhumantaṃ somam vindrāya indrārthaṃ vadhyaḥ aveḥ vvāreṣu vāleṣu daśāpavitre vvāje vna yuddha iva he ṛtvijaḥ vpari vpiñcata ||
kaviṃ mṛjanti maryaṃ dhībhirviprā avasyavaḥ | vṛṣā kanikradarpati || 20 || kavim | mṛjanti | maryam | dhībhiḥ | viprāḥ | avasyavaḥ | vṛṣā | kanikrat | arpati || 20 || vavasyavaḥ rakṣaṇakāmāḥ vviprāḥ medhāvina ṛtvijaḥ vdhībhiḥ aṅgulībhiḥ vmaryaṃ marnīyaṃ vkaviṃ krāntakarmāṇaṃ yaṃ somaṃ vmṛjanti mārjayanti so'yaṃ vvṛṣā secakaḥ somaḥ vkanikrat śabdaṃ kurvan varpati dhārayā kṣarati || || 33 ||
vṛṣaṇaṃ dhībhirappuraṃ sormamṛtasya dhārayā | matī viprāḥ samasvaran || 21 || vṛṣaṇam | dhībhiḥ | ap'puram | somam | ṛtasya | dhārayā | matī | viprāḥ | sam | asvaran || 21 || vviprāḥ medhāvina ṛtvijaḥ vvṛṣaṇaṃ kāmānāṃ varṣakaṃ vappuram apāṃ prerakaṃ vsomaṃ vdhībhiḥ aṅgulībhiḥ vmatī matyā stutyā ca vṛtasya udakasya vdhārayā vsamasvaran prerayanti ||
pavasva devāyupagindraṃ gacchatu te madaḥ | vāyumā rauha dharmaṇā || 22 || pavasva | deva'āyuk | indram | gacchatu | te | madaḥ | vāyum | ā | roha | dharmaṇā || 22 ||

he ᵥdeva dyotamāna soma ᵥpavasva dhārayā kṣara| api ca ᵥte tava ᵥmadaḥ madakaro rasaḥ ᵥāyuṣk anuṣaktam ᵥindraṃ prati ᵥgacchatu| api ca tvaṃ ᵥvāyuṃ ᵥdharmaṇā dhārakena rasena ᵥā ᵥroha prāpnuhi || parvamāna ni tośase rayiṃ soma śravāvyam | 23 || parvamāna | ni | tośase | rayim | soma | śravāvyam | priyaḥ | samudram | ā | viśa || 23 || he ᵥpavamāna ᵥsoma yastvaṃ ᵥśravāryaṃ śravaṇīya ᵥrayiṃ śatrūṇāṃ dhanaṃ ᵥni ᵥtośase nitarāṃ pīḍayasi sa tvaṃ ᵥpriyaḥ san ᵥsamudraṃ droṇakalaśam ᵥā ᵥviśa praviśa ||
apaghnaparvase mṛdhaḥ kratuvitsoma matsaraḥ | 24 || apa'ghnan | pavase | mṛdhaḥ | kratu'vit | soma | matsaraḥ | nudasva | adeva'yum | janam || 24 || he ᵥsoma ᵥmatsaraḥ madakaro yastvaṃ ᵥmṛdhaḥ hiṃsakān śatrūn ᵥapaghnan mārayan ᵥkratuvit asmabhyaṃ prajñāṃ prayacchan ᵥpavase kṣarasi sa tvam ᵥadevayum adevakāmaṃ ᵥjanaṃ rākṣasavargāṃ ᵥnudasva preraya ||
parvamānā asṛkṣata somāḥ śukrāsa indavaḥ | 25 || parvamānāḥ | asṛkṣata | somāḥ | śukrāsaḥ | indavaḥ | abhi | viśvāni | kāvyā || 25 || ᵥśukrāsaḥ ujjvalāḥ ᵥindavaḥ dīptāḥ ᵥpavamānāḥ kṣarantaḥ ᵥsomāḥ ᵥviśvāni ᵥkāvyā kāvyāni stotrāṇi ᵥabhi ᵥasṛkṣata sṛjyanta ṛtvigbhiḥ 1 || || 34 ||
parvamānāsa āśavaḥ śubhā asṛgramindavaḥ | 26 || parvamānāsaḥ | āśavaḥ | śubhāḥ | asṛgram | indavaḥ | ghnanto | viśvā | apa | dviṣaḥ || 26 || ᵥāśavaḥ śīghrāḥ ᵥśubhāḥ śobhamānāḥ ᵥpavamānāsaḥ kṣarantaḥ ᵥindavaḥ dīptāḥ somāḥ ᵥviśvāḥ sarvān ᵥdvipaḥ dveṣṭṛn śatrūn ᵥapa ᵥghnantaḥ mārayantaḥ ᵥasṛgraṃ sṛjyante ||
parvamānā divasparyantarikṣādasṛkṣata | pṛthivyā adhi sānavi || 27 || parvamānāḥ | divaḥ | pari | antarikṣāt | asṛkṣata | pṛthivyāḥ | adhi | sānavi || 27 || ᵥpavamānāḥ somāḥ ᵥdivaḥ dyulokāt ᵥantarikṣāt ca ᵥpṛthivyāḥ bhūmyāḥ ᵥadhi ᵥsānavi samucchrite deśe devayajane ᵥpari ᵥasṛkṣata sṛjyante ||
punānaḥ soma dhārayendo viśvā apa sidhaḥ | jahi rakṣāṃsi sukrato || 28 || punānaḥ | soma | dhārayā | indo iti | viśvāḥ | apa | sidhaḥ | jahi | rakṣāṃsi | sukrato iti su'krato || 28 || he ᵥindo dīpta ᵥsukrato sukarman ᵥsoma ᵥdhārayā ᵥpunānaḥ pūyamānastvaṃ ᵥviśvāḥ sarvān ᵥsidhaḥ dveṣṭṛn śatrūn ᵥrakṣāṃsi rākṣasāṃśca ᵥapa ᵥjahi māraya ||

asme vardhūni dhāraya somaṃ divyāni pārthivā | indo viśvāni vāryā || 30 || asme iti | vardhūni | dhāraya | somaṃ | divyāni | pārthivā | indo iti | viśvāni | vāryā || 30||

‘vṛṣā soma’ iti triṃśadṛcaṃ caturthaṃ sūktaṃ mārīcasya kaśyapasyārṣaṃ gāyatraṃ pavamānasaumyam |
tathā cānukrāntaṃ—‘vṛṣā soma kaśyapaḥ’ iti |
ukto viniyogaḥ ||
vṛṣā soma dyumā~ asi vṛṣā deva vṛṣavrataḥ | vṛṣā dharmāṇi dadhiṣe || 1 || vṛṣā | soma | dyumān | asi | vṛṣā | deva | vṛṣavrataḥ | vṛṣā | dharmāṇi | dadhiṣe || 1 || he soma vṛṣā varṣakastvaṃ dyumān dīptimān asi | api ca he deva dyotamāna soma vṛṣā tvaṃ vṛṣavrataḥ varṣaṇaśīlakarmāsi | kiṃca he soma vṛṣā tvaṃ dharmāṇi devānāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ ca hitāni karmāṇi dadhiṣe dhārayasi ||
vṛṣṇaste vṛṣṇyaṃ śavo vṛṣā vanaṃ vṛṣā madaḥ | satyaṃ vṛṣanvepedasi || 2 || vṛṣṇaḥ | te | vṛṣṇyam | śavaḥ | vṛṣa | vanam | vṛṣā | madaḥ | satyam | vṛṣan | vṛṣā | ita | asi || 2 || he vṛṣan kāmānāṃ varṣaka soma vṛṣṇaḥ varṣituḥ te tava śavaḥ balaṃ vṛṣṇyaṃ varṣaṇaśīlaṃ bhavati | vanaṃ tava bhajanamapi vṛṣā varṣaṇaśīlam | madaḥ tava raso'pi vṛṣā varṣaṇaśīlaḥ | satyaṃ satyameva tvaṃ vṛṣeta varṣaṇaśīla eva asi bhavasi ||
aśvo na cakrado vṛṣā saṃ gā indo samarvata | vi nau rāye duro vṛdhi || 3 || aśvaḥ | na | cakradaḥ | vṛṣā | sam | gāḥ | indo iti | sama | arvataḥ | vi | naḥ | rāye | duraḥ | vṛdhi || 3 || he indo soma vṛṣā tvam aśvo na aśva iva saṃ cakradaḥ saṃkrandase | api ca gāḥ paśūn arvataḥ aśvāṃścāsmabhyaṃ saṃ prayacchasīti śeṣaḥ | kiṃca naḥ asmākaṃ rāye dhanāya duraḥ dvārāṇi vi vṛdhi vivṛtāni kuru ||

punāno varivaskṛdhyūrjaṃ janāya girvaṇaḥ | hare sṛjāna āśirem || 14 || punānaḥ | varivaḥ | kṛdhi | ūrjam | janāya | girvaṇaḥ | hare | sṛjānaḥ | ā'śiram || 14 || he ⱽgirvaṇaḥ gīrbhirvanīya ⱽhare haritavarṇa soma ⱽāśiraṃ kṣīraṃ prati ⱽsṛjānaḥ visṛjyamānaḥ ⱽpunānaḥ pūyamānastvaṃ ⱽjanāya yajamānārthaṃ ⱽvarivaḥ dhanam ⱽūrjam annaṃ ca ⱽkṛdhi kuru ||
punāno devavītaya indrasya yāhi niṣkṛtam | ghṛtāno vājibhiryataḥ || 15 || punānaḥ | deva'vītaye | indrasya | yāhi | niḥ'kṛtam | ghṛtānaḥ | vāji'bhiḥ | yataḥ || 15 || he soma ⱽghṛtānaḥ dīpyamānaḥ ⱽvājibhiḥ balibhiryajamānaiḥ ⱽyataḥ saṃgṛhītaḥ ⱽdevavītaye yajñārthaṃ ⱽpunānaḥ pūyamānastvam ⱽindrasya ⱽniṣkṛtaṃ sthānaṃ ⱽyāhi gaccha || || 38 ||
pra hinvānāsa indavo'cchā samudramāśarvaḥ | dhiyā jūtā asukṣata || 16 || pra | hinvānāsaḥ | indavaḥ | accha | samudram | ā'śarvaḥ | dhiyā | jūtāḥ | asukṣata || 16 || ⱽāśavaḥ vegavantaḥ ⱽindavaḥ somāḥ ⱽsamudram antarikṣam ⱽaccha prati ⱽhinvānāsaḥ hinvānāḥ preryamāṇāḥ ⱽdhiyā aṅgulyā ⱽjūtāḥ kṛṣṭāśca ⱽpra ⱽasukṣata sṛjyante ||
mṛjyamānāsa āyavo vṛthā samudramindavaḥ | agarbhatasya yonimā || 17 || mṛjyamānāsaḥ | āyavaḥ | vṛthā | samudram | indavaḥ | agarbhan | ṛtasya | yonim | ā || 17 || ⱽmṛjyamānāsaḥ mṛjyamānāḥ ⱽāyavaḥ gantāraḥ ⱽindavaḥ somāḥ ⱽvṛthā āyāsaṃ vinaiva ⱽsamudram antarikṣaṃ gacchanti | etadeva darśayati | ⱽṛtasya udakasya ⱽyoniṃ sthānam ⱽagaman ⱽgacchantīti ||
pari ṇe yāhyasmayuviśvā vasūnyorjesā | pāhi naḥ śarma vīravat || 18 || pārī | naḥ | yāhi | asmayuḥ | viśvā | vasūni | ojasā | pāhi | naḥ | śarma | vīra'vat || 18 || he soma ⱽasmayuḥ asmatkāmastvaṃ ⱽnaḥ asmākaṃ ⱽviśvā viśvāni ⱽvasūni dhanāni ⱽojasā balena saha ⱽpari ⱽyāhi rakṣārthaṃ parigaccha | api ca ⱽnaḥ asmākaṃ ⱽvīravat putravat ⱽśarma gṛhaṃ ⱽpāhi rakṣa ||

indrāyendro murutvate parvasva madhumattamaḥ | ṛtasya yonimāsadam || 22 || indrāya | indo iti | murutvate | parvasva | madhumat'tamaḥ | ṛtasya | yonim | ā'sadam || 22 || he indo soma madhumattamaḥ atiśayena madhumāṃsavam ṛtasya yajñasya yoniṃ sthānam āsadam upaveṣṭuṃ marutvate indrāya indrārthaṃ pavasva kṣara ||
taṃ tvā viprā vacovidaḥ pariṣkṛṇvanti vedhasaḥ | saṃ tvā mṛjantyāyavaḥ || 23 || tam | tvā | viprāḥ | vacaḥ'vidaḥ | pari | kṛṇvanti | vedhasaḥ | sam | tvā | mṛjanti | āyavaḥ || 23 || he soma taṃ pavamānaṃ tvā tvāṃ viprāḥ prājñāḥ vedhasaḥ karmaṇāṃ kartāraḥ vacovidaḥ stotāraḥ pariṣkṛṇvanti alaṃkurvanti | api ca tvā tvām āyavaḥ manuṣyāḥ saṃ mṛjanti samyak śodhayanti ||

1 ||

abhyāsasvaraḥ |

4 ||
saḥ | mātarau | vi'caran | vājayan | apaḥ | pra | medhiraḥ | svadhayā | pinvate | padam | aṃśuḥ | yatvena | pipiśe | yataḥ | nṛ'bhiḥ | sam | jāmi'bhiḥ | nasate | rakṣate | śiraḥ ||4||
medhiraḥ |
‘medhārathābhyāmarinaracau’ (pā. sū. 5.2.109.3) hatīranpratyayo matvarthīyaḥ |
medhāvān prājñaḥ yaḥ saḥ somaḥ mātarā mātarau jagato nirmātryau dyāvāpṛthivyau vicran antareṇātyantaṃ caran tathā apaḥ antarikṣasthitānyudakāni vājayan prerayan |
vajeryantasya śatari rūpam |

avayavatvena kṛtaḥ |

nasatirgatikarmā |

tādṛśaṃ tanvam ātmīyaṃ śarīraṃ punānaḥ śodhayan ata eva śuciḥ pūto dīpyamāno vā hariḥ haritavarṇaḥ somaḥ sānavi sānuni samucchrite avye avivālakṛte daśāpavitre nyadhāviṣṭa ṛtvigbhirnidhīyate |
'dhrūj kampane' |
'syasiccasīyudtāsipu' (pā. sū. 6. 4. 62) iti karmaṇi luṅi ciṇvadiṭ |
ṇicvādvṛddhiḥ |
tataḥ mitrāya varuṇāya vāyave etebhyaḥ juṣṭaḥ paryāptaḥ tridhātu triṃdhāno vasatīvarībhirdvedhā payasā saṃsṛṣṭaḥ somaḥ tridhātu tādṛśaṃ madhu madakaraḥ somaḥ sukarmabhiḥ śobhanakarmayuktairṛtvigbhiḥ kriyate mitrādidevārthaṃ pradīyate ||
parvasva soma devavītaye vṛṣendrasya hārdi somadhānamā viśa |
purā nau bādhāhu ritāti pāraya kṣetravidvi diśa āhā vipṛcchate ||
9 ||
parvasva | soma | deva'vītaye | vṛṣā | indrasya | hārdi | soma'dhānam | ā | viśa | purā | naḥ | bādhāt | duḥ'itāt | ati | pāraya | kṣetra'vit | hi | diśaḥ | āha | vi'pṛcchate || 9 ||
he ᵛsoma ᵛvṛṣā kāmānāmudakānāṃ vā secakastvaṃ ᵛdevavītaye devānāṃ pānāya ᵛpavasva kalaśādiṣu kṣara|
atha he soma tvam ᵛindrasya ᵛhārdi hṛdayaṃgamaṃ priyaṃ ᵛsomadhānam|
somo nidhīyate'sminniti somadhānaṃ pātram|
tat ᵛā ᵛviśa praviśa|
kiṃca ᵛnaḥ asmākaṃ ᵛbādhāt bādhanāt pīḍanāt ᵛpurā pūrvameva duritāni durgamanāni rakṣāṃsi ᵛati ᵛpāraya atītya pāraṃ gamaya|
api ca ᵛkṣetravit mārgajñaḥ puruṣaḥ ᵛvipṛcchate vividhaṃ panthānaṃ pṛcchate janāya ᵛdiśaḥ mārgān ᵛāha ᵛhi brūte khalu|
uktvā taṃ yathā rakṣati tathā yajñamārgavettā tvamasmabhyaṃ yajñapatha uktvāsmān pālayeti ||
hito na sarpirābhe vārjamṛbhendrasyendo jaṭharamā pavasva | nāvā na sindhumarti parṣi vidvānchūro na yudhyannava no nidaḥ spaḥ || 10 || hitaḥ | na | sarpiḥ | abhi | vārjam | arbha | indrasya | indo iti | jaṭharam | ā | pavasva | nāvā | na | sindhum | ati | parṣi | vidvān | śūraḥ | na | yudhyan | arbha | naḥ | nidaḥ | spaḥriti spaḥ || 10 ||
he soma ṛtvigbhiḥ preryamāṇaḥ san ᵛvājaṃ somanidhānaṃ droṇakalaśam ᵛabhi ᵛartha abhigaccha |
tatra dṛṣṭāntaḥ |
ᵛhito ᵛna ᵛsasiḥ |
aśvo hitaḥ preryamāṇaḥ san vājaṃ saṃgrāmamabhilakṣya yathā yāti tadvat|
tato he ᵛindo ᵛindrasya ᵛjaṭharaṃ jaṭharabhūtaṃ droṇakalaśaṃ vā ᵛā ᵛpavasva ābhimukhyena praviśa|
kiṃca ᵛvidvān sarvaṃ jānaṃstvamasmān ᵛati ᵛpārpa duritānyatītya pāraya |
tatra dṛṣṭāntaḥ |
ᵛnāvā ᵛna |
yathā nāvikāḥ ᵛsindhuṃ nadīṃ nāvā manuṣyān pārayanti tadvat|
kiṃca he soma tvaṃ ᵛśūro ᵛna śūro vīra iva ᵛyudhyan śatrūn saṃpraharan ᵛnidaḥ nindakācchatroḥ ᵛnaḥ asmān ᵛava ᵛrupaḥ avapāraya |
bādhāṃ vināśaya pālaya |
yadvā |
no'smākaṃ nido nindakānchatrūnava spaḥ avāṅmukhaṃ jahi |
‘spṛ hiṃsāyām’|

tipi guṇe halṅyādinā tipo lopaḥ ||
24 ||
‘ā dakṣiṇā’ iti navacaṃ caturthaṃ sūktaṃ vaiśvāmitrasyarṣyārṣaṃ pavamānasomadevatākam|
navamī triṣṭup śiṣṭā jagatyaḥ |
tathā cānukrāntam- ‘ā dakṣiṇā nava ṛpabhastau vaiśvāmitrau’ iti |
‘tau’ ityanena prakṛto reṇurṛṣabhaśceti parāmṛśyete |
śrūyate hi- ‘atha ha viśvāmitraḥ putrānāmantrayāmāsa madhucchandāḥ śṛṇotana ṛpabho reṇuṣṭakaḥ’ (ai. brā. 7, 17) iti |
gataḥ sūktaviniyogaḥ ||
ā dakṣiṇā sṛjyate śuṣmyā'sadaṃ verti duho rakṣasaḥ pāti jāgṛviḥ | hariropaśaṃ kṛṇute nabhasparya upastirai cumborbrahma nirṇijeṃ || 1 || ā | dakṣiṇā | sṛjyate | śuṣmī | ā'sadam | verti | duhaḥ | rakṣasaḥ | pāti | jāgṛviḥ | hariḥ | opaśam | kṛṇute | nabhaḥ | paryaḥ | upastirai | cumbauḥ | brahma | niḥ'nijeṃ || 1 ||
somasya svabhūte yajñe ṛtvigbhyaḥ ᵛdakṣiṇā ᵛā ᵛsṛjyate asmābhiḥ pradīyate |
ᵛśuṣmī balavān somaḥ ᵛāsadam āsthānaṃ droṇākhyaṃ ᵛveti praviśati |
kiṃca ᵛjāgṛviḥ jāgaraṇaśīlaḥ somaḥ ᵛduhaḥ drohakāriṇaḥ ᵛrakṣasaḥ rākṣasāt ᵛpāti stotṛn rakṣati |
api ca ᵛhariḥ haritavarṇaḥ somaḥ ᵛopaśam |
ā samantādupaśeta ityopaśaḥ |
sarvasya dhārakaṃ ᵛnabhaḥ nabhasa ādityasya svabhūtaṃ ᵛpayaḥ udakaṃ ᵛkṛṇute sarvatra karoti |
‘agnau prāstāhutiḥ’ ityādikrameṇa karotītyarthaḥ |
yadvā |
nabho'ntarikṣaṃ payaḥ payaso
dhārakaṃ karotīti |
kiṃcāyaṃ somaḥ ᵥcamboḥ |
dyāvāpṛthivīnāmaitat |
camanti bhakṣayanti manuṣyadevā atreti |
tayoḥ ᵥupastare upastaraṇāyācchādanāya ᵥbrahma bṛhadyutaṃ tamāṃsi vināśayantaṃ yadvā parivṛḍhaṃ sūrya dyuloke karoti |
tathā tameva sūrya ᵥnirninaje padārthānāṃ ninaṃjanāya pariśodhanāya ca karoti |
ādityo hi dyāvāpṛthivyau svatejasopastṛṇāti |
sa hi svabhāsā sarvaṃ nineṃkti ca ||

ᵥmahiṣāḥ pūjyā mahānto vatvijaḥ ᵥsamyaśvaḥ parasparaṃ saṃgatāḥ santaḥ ᵥsamyak ᵥahepata somaṃ samyak prerayanti abhipuṇvantīti yāvata |

ᵥpavitravantaḥ pavitreṇa śodhakena sāmarthyena yuktāḥ somasya raśmayaḥ ᵥvācaṃ somasthitāṃ mādhya- mikāṃ vācaṃ ᵥpari ᵥāsate paryupaviśanti |

‘śiśurna’ iti navacaṃ saptamaṃ sūktaṃ dairghatamasasya kakṣīvata ārṣaṃ pavamānasomadevatākam |
aṣṭamī triṣṭup śiṣṭā jagatyaḥ |

gataḥ sūktaviniyogaḥ ||
śiśurna jāto'rva cakradadvane svaᵥryadvājarūpaḥ sipāsati |
divo retasā sacate payovṛdhā tamīṃmahe sumatī śarma saprathaḥ ||
1 ||
śiśuḥ | na | jātaḥ | arba | cakradat | vane | svaḥ | yat | vājī | arupaḥ | sipāsati | divaḥ | retasā | sacate | payaḥ'vṛdhā | tam | īmahe | sumatī | śarma | sa'prathaḥ || 1 ||
sa pavamānaḥ somaḥ ᵥvane vananīye vasatīvāryasya udake ᵥjātaḥ utpannaḥ ᵥśiśurna śiśurva ᵥava ᵥcakradat avāṅmukhaṃ krandati |
tadodakamadhye yattacchabdaṃ karoti khalu |
ᵥyat yadā ᵥvājī balavānbhavānvā ᵥarupaḥ ārocamānaḥ |
yadvā |
vājyaśva iva arupaḥ |
ᵥatairupat gamanaśīlaḥ somaḥ ᵥsvaḥ svargalokaṃ ᵥsipāsati saṃbhaktumicchati |
ᵥsanoteḥ sanīdabhāva ātvam |
‘sanoteḥ0’ iti sāṃhitikaṃ plavam |
so'yaṃ ᵥpayovṛdhā gavāmoṣadhīnāṃ ca kṣīrasya vardhakena ᵥretasā udakena saha ᵥdivaḥ dyulokāt ᵥsacate pārthivaṃ lokaṃ samavaiti |
ᵥtaṃ tādṛśaṃ prasiddhaṃ somaṃ ᵥsaprathaḥ sarvataḥ pṛthutaraṃ dhanādinā yuktaṃ ᵥśarma gṛhaṃ tadvayaṃ ᵥsumatī |
‘supāṃ suluk0’ iti tṛtīyāyāḥ pūrvasavarṇadīrghaḥ |

daivādikaḥ |
chāndasaḥ iyano luk ||
divo yaḥ skambho dharuṇaḥ ᵥsvātat āpūrṇo aṃśuḥ paryati viśvataḥ |
seme mahī rodasī yakṣadāvṛtā samīcīne dādhāra samiṣaḥ kaviḥ ||
2 ||
divaḥ | yaḥ | skambhaḥ | dharuṇaḥ | su'ātataḥ | ā'pūrṇaḥ | aṃśuḥ | pari'eti | viśvataḥ | saḥ | ime iti | mahī iti | rodasī iti | yakṣat | ā'vṛtā | samīcīne iti sam'aīcīne | dādhāra | sam | iṣaḥ | kaviḥ || 2 ||
ᵥdivaḥ dyulokasya ᵥskambhaḥ dhikkambhayitā saṃroddha|

yadvā |
pṛthivyā dhārakaḥ |
‘viṣṭambho divo dharuṇaḥ pṛthivyāḥ’ (ṛ. saṃ. 9.89.6) ityāmnānāt |
tādṛśaḥ ᵥsvātataḥ suṣṭhu sarvatra ᵥvitataḥ vistṛtaḥ ata eva ᵥāpūrṇaḥ pātreṣu saṃpūrṇaḥ ᵥyaḥ ᵥaṃśuḥ somātmakaḥ ᵥviśvataḥ sarvataḥ ᵥparyeti parigacchati |
‘tiṅi codāttavati’ iti gaternighātaḥ |
ᵥsaḥ somaḥ ᵥime ᵥrodasī dyāvāpṛthivyau ᵥāvṛtā saṃbhajanīyena karmaṇā ᵥyakṣat yajatu |
svabalena saṃyojayitvityarthaḥ |
tataḥ so'yaṃ ᵥsamīcīne parasparaṃ saṃgate dyāvāpṛthivyau ᵥdādhāra dhārayāṃcakāra |
‘tujādīnām0’ ityabhyāsasya dīrghaḥ |
anantaraṃ ᵥkaviḥ krāntakarmā ᵥiṣaḥ annāni stotṛbhyaḥ ᵥsaṃ prayacchatu |
upasargaśruteryogakriyādhyāhāraḥ ||
1. ta1.2.5.6.7-bha6- ‘vardhakena’ nāsti |
2. ga-ta3-ma-pṛthutamaṃ |
3. bha1.2.7-pūrṇaḥ |
mahi psaraḥ sukṛtaṃ somyaṃ madhvīṃ gavyutirditeṃṛtaṃ yate | īśe yo vṛṣṭirita usiyo vṛṣāpāṃ netā ya itaūtikrgmiryaḥ || 3 || mahiṃ | psaraḥ | sukṛtam | somyam | madhu | urvī | gavyutiḥ | aditeṃḥ | ṛtam | yate | īśe | yaḥ | vṛṣṭeḥ | itaḥ | usiyaḥ | vṛṣā | apām | netā | yaḥ | itaḥ'ūtiḥ | ṛgmiryaḥ ||3||
psaraḥ satyabhūtaṃ yajñaṃ yate gacchate tasmā indrāya sukṛtaṃ suṣṭhu saṃskṛtaṃ somyaṃ somamayaṃ mahi mahat prabhūtaṃ madhu madhurarasaṃ psaraḥ bhakṣaṇaṃ pānīyaṃ bhavati |
'psā bhakṣaṇe' |
kiṃca asmadyajñaṃ pratyāgacchatāmindrādīnām aditeṃḥ pṛthivyāḥ gavyutiḥ mārgaśca urvī vistīrṇo bhavati |
indrasya śatasahasrasaṃkhyakairharibhiḥ saha gantuṃ mārgo vistīrṇo'bhavadityarthaḥ |
yaḥ indraḥ itaḥ imaṃ lokaṃ pratyāpatantyāḥ vṛṣṭeḥ īśe īśvaro bhavati |
tathā usiyaḥ |
usyeti gonāma |
gobhyo hitaḥ apām udakānāṃ vṛṣā varṣakaḥ yajñasya netā nāyakaḥ itaūtiḥ imamasmadīyaṃ yajñaṃ pratyutirgamanaṃ yasya sa tathoktaḥ |
itaḥ gacchan yaḥ indraḥ ṛgmiryaḥ ṛgahaḥ stotavyo bhavati |
tasmā ayaṃ somaḥ pātavyo bhavatīti ||
pravagyeṃ'bhiṣṭave 'ātmanvanbhaḥ' ityeṣā |
sūtritaṃ ca- 'tadu pratyatamasya karmāmanvanbha duhyate ghṛtaṃ payaḥ' (āśva. śrau. 4. 7) iti |
ātmanvanbhau duhyate ghṛtaṃ parya ṛtasya nābhirmṛtaṃ vi jāyate | samīcīnāḥ sudānavaḥ prīṇanti taṃ narau hitamarva mehanti peravaḥ || 4 || ātman'vat | nabhaḥ | duhyate | ghṛtam | paryaḥ | ṛtasya | nābhiḥ | amṛtam | vi | jāyate | samīcīnāḥ | sudānavaḥ | prīṇanti | tam | narau | hitam | arva | mehanti | peravaḥ ||4||
ātmanvat |
'ano yud' iti nuḍāgamaḥ |
ātmavat sāravat ghṛtaṃ payaḥ ca nabhaḥ nabhasa ādityarūpālasomaḥ duhyate |
kiṃca ṛtasya yajñasya nābhiḥ |
'ṇaha bandhane' |
'svamornapuṃsakāt' (pā. sū. 7. 1. 23) iti sālaṃbha bhavati sarvavidhīnāṃ chandasi vikalpitatvāt |

pra | rājā̭ | vāca̭m | ja॒na॒yan | a॒bhi॒sya॒dat | a॒paḥ | vasā̭naḥ | a॒bhi | gāḥ | i॒ya॒kṣati | gṛ॒bhṇāti̭ | ri॒pram | a॒viḥ | a॒sya | tā॒nvā̭ | śu॒ddhaḥ | de॒vānā̭m | u॒pa | yā̭ti | ni॒ḥ'kṛta̭m || 1 ||

samudriyāṃ apsarasau manīṣiṇamāsīnā antarabhi somamakṣaran | tā īṃ hinvanti harmyasya sakṣaṇiṃ yācante sumnaṃ parvamānamakṣitam || 3 || samudriyāṃḥ | apsarasaḥ | manīṣiṇam | āsīnāḥ | antaḥ | abhi | somam | akṣaran | tāḥ | īm | hinvanti | harmyasya | sakṣaṇim | yācante | sumnam | parvamānam | akṣitam ||3||
^samudriyāṃḥ |
samudrasādhanatvāt samudramantarikṣam |
tatsambandhinyaḥ ^apsarasaḥ kāścana ^antaḥ yajñamadhye ^āsīnāḥ pātreṣu vartamānā vasatīvaryaḥ ^manīṣiṇaṃ medhāvinaṃ ^somam ^abhi ^akṣaran abhiṣayamāṇaṃ somamabhikṣaranti |
^tāḥ etā āpaḥ ^īm enaṃ ^harmyasya harmyavatsukhakarasya yāgagṛhasya ^sakṣaṇiṃ sevanaśīlaṃ ^hinvanti vardhayanti poṣayanti |
tā eva stotāro vā ^pavamānaṃ somam ^akṣitam akṣīṇaṃ ^sumnaṃ sukhaṃ ^yācante prārthayante |
tatrāyaṃ bhāvaḥ |
kāścanāpsarasaḥ somaṃ rājānaṃ manīṣiṇaṃ kāmayamānā devānapi parityajya svargādāgatya rājñaḥ samīpe sthitvā tasya ramānādadate |
svarasena vardhayanti taṃ sukhaṃ ca yācanta iti ||
gojitḥ somau rathajiddhiraṇyajitsvarjidābjitaparvate sahasrajit | yaṃ devāsaścakrire pītaye madaṃ svādiṣṭhaṃ drapsamaruṇaṃ mayobhuvam || 4 || go'jit | naḥ | somaḥ | ratha'jit | hiraṇya'jit | svaḥ'jit | ap'jit | pavane | mahaḥ'jit | yam | devāsaḥ | cakrire | pītaye | madam | svādiṣṭham | drapsam | aruṇam | mayaḥ'bhuvam || 4 ||
^naḥ asmākaṃ ^gojit gavāṃ jetā tathā ^rathajit rathasya jetā ^hiraṇyajit hiraṇyasya jetā tathā ^svarjit svargasya sukhasya jetā ^abjit apāṃ jetā ^sahasrajita sahasrasaṃkhyākasya dhanasya jetā ^somaḥ ^pavate pūyate |

5 ||
eva | te | indo iti | su'bhvam | su'peśasam | rasam | tuñjanti | prathamāḥ | abhi'dhriyaḥ | niḥ'danidam | pavamāna | ni | tāripaḥ | āviḥ | te | śuṣmaḥ | bhavatu | priyaḥ | madaḥ ||5||

he indo soma ᵥeva evamidānīṃ- kriyamāṇaprakāreṇa ᵥte tava ᵥsubhvaṃ śobhanabhavanaṃ ᵥsupeśasam |

ᵥprathamāḥ mukhyāḥ |

he ᵥpavamāna ᵥnirdanidam asmacchindakaṃ sarva- mapi śatruṃ ᵥni ᵥtāripaḥ vināśaya |
ᵥte tava ᵥśuṣmaḥ jhalakaraḥ ᵥpriyaḥ priyabhūtaḥ ᵥmadaḥ madakaro rasaḥ ᵥāviḥ ᵥbhavatu ||
4 ||
‘somasya dhārā’ iti pañcarcaṃ trayodaśaṃ sūktaṃ bhāradvājasya ghasunāmna ārṣaṃ jāgataṃ pavamānasoma- devatākam |
tathā cānukrāntaṃ—‘somasya vasurbhāradvājaḥ’ iti |
gato viniyogaḥ ||
somasya dhārā pavate nṛcakṣasa ṛtena devān havate divaspari |
bṛhaspate rvyathenā vi didyute samudrāso na sarvanāni vinyacuḥ ||
1 ||
somasya | dhārā | pavate | nṛ'cakṣasaḥ | ṛtena | devān | havate | divaḥ | pari | bṛhaspateḥ | rvyathena | vi | didyute | samudrāsaḥ | na | sarvanāni | vinyacuḥ || 1 ||
ᵥsomasya abhiṣūyamāṇasya ᵥdhārā ᵥpavate śrotate | kīdṛśasya somasya | ᵥnṛcakṣasaḥ nṛṇāṃ yajamānānāṃ draṣṭuḥ | sa ca ᵥṛtena yajñena ᵥdevān somabhāja indrādīn ᵥhavate | kutra | ᵥdivaspari
dyulokasyopari vartamānān |
vṛhaspateḥ mantrapālakasya stotuḥ ravathena śabdena stotreṇa vi dyute vidyotate |
samudrāso na samudrā iva pṛthivīṃ savanāni yajñasambandhini vivyacuḥ vyāpnuvanti ||
yaṃ tvā vājin‌adhyā abhyanūṣatāyohaṃtaṃ yonimā rauhasi dyumān |
madhonāmāyuḥ pratiran mahi śrava indrāya soma pavase vṛṣā madaḥ ||
2 ||
yam | tvā | vājin | adhyāḥ | abhi | anūpata | aryaḥ | hatam | yonim | ā | rohasi | dyu'mān | madhonām | āyuḥ | pra'tiran | mahi | śravaḥ | indrāya | soma | pavase | vṛṣā | madaḥ || 2 ||
he vājin annavan soma yaṃ tvā tvām adhyāḥ ahanīyā gāvaḥ abhyanūṣata abhiṣṭuvanti |
āśirārthaṃ sthitāḥ śabdāyanta ityarthaḥ |
sa tvam ayohatam aya iti hiraṇyanāma |
tena taddān pāṇilakṣyate |
hiraṇmayena pāṇinā hataṃ saṃskṛtaṃ yoni sthānam ā rohasi dyumān dīptaḥ san |
‘abhi yonimayohatam’ (ṛ. saṃ. 9. 1. 2) iti yuktam |
kiṃca he soma madhonāṃ haviṣmatāṃ yajamānānām āyuḥ āyuṣyaṃ mahi mahat śravaḥ annaṃ yaśo vā pratiran vardhayan indrāya indrārthaṃ pavase pūyase |
vṛṣā varṣako madaḥ madakaraśca tvam ||
endrasya kukṣā parvate madinaṃtapa ūrjaṃ vasānaḥ śravase sumaṃgalaḥ |
pratyaṅ sa viśvā bhuvanābhi paprathe krīḻan haritaryaḥ syandate vṛṣā ||
3 ||
ā | indrasya | kukṣā | pavate | madina'tamaḥ | ūrjam | vasānaḥ | śravase | su'maṃgalaḥ | pratyaṅ | saḥ | viśvā | bhuvanā | abhi | paprathe | krīḻan | hariḥ | atyaḥ | syandate | vṛṣā || 3 ||

madinattamaḥ mādayitṛtamaḥ |
ūrjaṃ vasānaḥ balakaraṃ rasamācchādayan |
‘apāṃ vamānaḥ’ (ṛ. saṃ. 9. 78. 1) iti yuktam |
sumaṃgalaḥ śobhanamaṅgala- pradaḥ |
saḥ somaḥ pratyaṅ viśvā bhuvanā sarvāṇi bhūtajātāni abhi paprathe abhiprathayati |
divi uditaḥ san krīḻan vedyāṃ saṃkrīḍamānaḥ hariḥ haritavarṇaḥ atyaḥ atanakuśalaḥ vṛṣā varṣakaḥ syandate rasarūpeṇa ||
taṃ tvā devebhyo madhumattamaṃ naraḥ sahasradhāraṃ duhate daśa kṣipaḥ |
nṛbhiḥ somaḥ praccuto grāvabhiḥ suto viśvān devā~ ā pavasvā sahasrajit ||
4 ||
tam | tvā | devebhyaḥ | madhumat'tamam | naraḥ | sahasra'dhāram | duhate | daśa | kṣipaḥ | nṛbhiḥ | somaḥ | pra'cyutaḥ | grāvabhiḥ | sutaḥ | viśvān | devān | ā | pavasva | sahasra'jit ||4||
he soma taṃ tādṛśaṃ tvā tvāṃ devebhyaḥ indrāyarthaṃ madhumattamam atiśayena madhumantaṃ sahasra- dhāraṃ bahudhārāyuktaṃ duhate duhanti |
ke |
naraḥ netāra ṛtvijaḥ |
daśa kṣipaḥ teṣāṃ daśasaṃkhyākā aṅgulayaśca |
he soma nṛbhiḥ manuṣyaiḥ pracyutaḥ grāvabhiḥ sutaḥ abhiputastvaṃ sahasrajit sahasrasaṃkhyākadhanasya jetā man viśvān devān ā pavasva ||
taṃ tvā hastino madhumantamadribhirhantyapsu vṛṣabhaṃ daśa kṣipaḥ |
indraṃ soma mādayan daivyaṃ janaṃ sindhorivormiḥ parvamāno arṣasi ||
5 ||
tam | tvā | hastinaḥ | madhu'mantam | adri'bhiḥ | duhanti | ap'su | vṛṣabham | daśa | kṣipaḥ | indram | soma | mādayan | daivyam | janam | sindhoḥ | iva | ūrmiḥ | parvamānaḥ | arpasi ||5||
'taṃ tādṛśaṃ 'madhumantaṃ madhurasaṃ 'vṛṣabhaṃ kāmānāṃ varṣakaṃ 'tvā tvāṃ 'hastinaḥ suhastasya 'daśa 'kṣipaḥ aṅgulayaḥ 'adribhiḥ prāvabhiḥ 'apsu 'duhanti |

pra somasya pavamānasyormaya indrasya yanti jaṭharaṃ supeśasaḥ | dabhā yadīmunītā yaśasā gavāṃ dānāya śūramadandiṣuḥ sutāḥ || 1 || pra | somasya | pavamānasya | ūrmayaḥ | indrasya | yanti | jaṭharam | mu'peśasaḥ | dabhā | yat | īm | uta'nīnāḥ | yaśasā | gavām | dānāya | śūram | ut'armandiṣuḥ | sutāḥ ||1||

'supeśasaḥ surūpā ūrmaya iti sambandhaḥ |
'yat yadā 'īm ete 'sutāḥ abhiputāḥ somāḥ 'gavāṃ 'yaśasā balabhūtena 'dakṣā saha 'ujjītāḥ santaḥ 'dānāya yajamānaviṣayābhimatadānāya 'śūraṃ vikrāntamindram 'udamandiṣuḥ unmādayanti tadā jaṭharaṃ yanti ||

1. ga-sucetunā |

kim |
nirṇijaṃ nirṇejakaṃ pavitram ||
parjanyaḥ pitā mahiṣasya parṇino nābhā pṛthivyā giriśu kṣayaṃ dadhe | svasāra āpo abhi gā utāsarantsa grāvabhirnasate vīte adhvare || 3 || parjanyaḥ | pitā | mahiṣasya | parṇinaḥ | nābhā | pṛthivyāḥ | giriṣu | kṣayam | dadhe | svasāraḥ | āpaḥ | abhi | gāḥ | uta | asaran | sam | grāva'bhiḥ | nasate | vīte | adhvare ||3||
yasya mahiṣasya mahataḥ parṇinaḥ parṇavataḥ patanavato vā somasya parjanyaḥ pitā janakaḥ sa somaḥ pṛthivyāḥ nābhā nābhau nābhisthānīye havirdhāne giriṣu girisaṃbandhigrāvasu kṣayaṃ nivāsaṃ dadhe dhārayatyabhipavasameye |

ataptatarnū tadāmo aśnute śṛtāsa iddhahantastatsamāśata ||
1 ||
pavitram | te | vi'tatam | brahmaṇaḥ | pate | pra'bhu | gātrāṇi | pari | eṣi | viśvataḥ | atapta'tanūḥ | na | tat | āmaḥ | aśnute | śṛtāsaḥ | it | vahantaḥ | tat | sam | āśata || 1 ||
he 'brahmaṇaspate mantrasya svāmin soma 'te 'pavitraṃ śodhakamaṅgaṃ 'vitataṃ sarvatra vistṛtam |
sa 'prabhuḥ prabhavitā tvaṃ 'gātrāṇi pāturaṅgāni 'paryaṣi parigacchasi 'viśvataḥ sarvataḥ |
tava 'tat pavitram 'ataptatanūḥ payovratādinā 'asaṃtaptagātraḥ 'āmaḥ aparipako 'na 'aśnute na vyāpnoti |
'śṛtāsa 'it śratā eva paripakkā eva 'vahantaḥ yāgaṃ nirvahantaḥ 'tat pavitraṃ 'samāśata vyāpnuvanti'||
tapoṣu pavitraṃ vitataṃ divaspade śocanto asya tantavo vyasthiran |
avantyasya pavitāramāśavo divaspṛṣṭhamadhi tiṣṭhanti cetasā ||
2 ||
tapoḥ | pavitram | vi'tatam | divaḥ | pade | śocantaḥ | asya | tantavaḥ | vi | asthiran | avanti | asya | pavitāram | āśavaḥ | divaḥ | pṛṣṭham | adhi | tiṣṭhanti | cetasā ||2||
'tapoḥ śatrūṇāṃ tāpaksya 'somasya 'pavitraṃ śodhakamaṅgaṃ tejo vā 'divaspade dyulokasya ucchrite sthāne 'vitataṃ vistṛtam |
'tṛtīyasyāmito divi soma āsīt ' (tai. brā. 3. 2. 1. 1) iti brāhmaṇam |
'asya 'tantavaḥ aṃśavaḥ 'śocantaḥ dīpyamānāḥ 'vyasthiran vivadhaṃ tiṣṭhanti |
pṛthivyāṃ havirdhāne vā |
'asya somasya 'āśavaḥ śīghragāmano rasāḥ 'avanti rakṣanti |
kam |
'pavitāraṃ pāvayitāram |
yajamānamavanti rakṣanti homadvārā 'paścādbhūtāḥ 'divaḥ dyulokasya 'pṛṣṭhaṃ pṛṣṭhabhāgamunnatadeśaṃ 'cetasā buddhayā devagamanecchāvalyā 'adhi 'tiṣṭhanti āśrayante ||
abhiṣṭave 'arūrucat ' ityeṣāpanīyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—'īle dyāvāpṛthivī iti prāguttamāyā arūrucadupasaḥ pṛbhiramiya ityevāpeta ' (āśva. śrau. 4. 6) iti ||
1. ga-yathā amṛdhaḥ vājaṃ saṃgrāmaṃ |
2. ga-evā evamimeḥ gha-ta-bha-evamevami |
3. ga-ta3.4- sacante sevate |
4. bha6-te tava |
5. bha6-payontyādinā |
6. ga1-ta3-bha1.2.7- 'vahaṃtaḥ ' nāsti |
7. bha6-vyāpnuvati yaḥ śrataḥ sa raudraḥ yaḥ śrataḥ sa sa devaḥ yo vidagdhaḥ sa nairṛtaḥ iti śruteḥ |
8. ta-vyāpakasya |
9. ga-śoṣakasya |
10. ga-somadhārāśāmau hutāḥ; bha6-homadvāreṇa pathādutāḥ |
arūrucadupasaḥ pṛśnirgyi ukṣā bibharti bhuvanāni vājyuḥ |
māyāvino mamire asya māyayā nṛcakṣasaḥ pitaro garbhamā dadhuḥ ||
3 ||

▼upasaḥ saṃbandhī ▼pṛśniḥ ādityaḥ |

▼apriyaḥ mukhyaḥ so'yaṃ somaḥ ▼arūrucat rocayati |

8 ||
‘ pavasva devamādanaḥ ’ iti pañcarcaṃ saptadaśaṃ sūktaṃ vācaḥ putrasya prajāpaterāpaṃ jāgataṃ pavamānasoma- devatākam |
tathā cānukrāntaṃ—‘ pavasva vācyaḥ prajāpatiḥ ’ iti |
gato viniyogaḥ ||
parvasva devamādano vircarṣaṇirpsā indrāya varuṇāya vāyave |
kudhī nau adya varivaḥ svastimudurukṣitau gṛṇīhi daivyaṃ janam ||
1 ||
parvasva | deva'mādanaḥ | vi'carṣaṇiḥ | apsāḥ | indrāya | varuṇāya | vāyave | kudhi | naḥ | adya | varivaḥ | svasti'mat | uru'kṣitau | gṛṇīhi | daivyam | janam || 1 ||
he soma <sup>v</sup>devamādanaḥ devānāṃ mādayitā <sup>v</sup>vircarṣaṇiḥ vidvattā <sup>v</sup>apsāḥ apāṃ dātā <sup>v</sup>tvaṃ <sup>v</sup>pavasva kṣara |
kasmai |
<sup>v</sup>indrāya <sup>v</sup>varuṇāya <sup>v</sup>vāyave ca |
<sup>v</sup>naḥ asmākaṃ <sup>v</sup>varivaḥ dhanaṃ <sup>v</sup>svastimata |
svastītya- vināśanāma |

<sup>v</sup>urukṣitau vistīrṇāyāṃ bhūmau yajñasaṃbandhinyāṃ <sup>v</sup>daivyaṃ <sup>v</sup>janaṃ devasaṃbandhinaṃ saṃgham |
janaśabdaḥ saṃghavācī |
taṃ <sup>v</sup>gṛṇīhi |

athavā daivyaṃ janamityṛṣiḥ svātmānamāha |
devasaṃbandhinaṃ janaṃ māṃ gṛṇīhi sādhusaṃbhaktā iti śabdaya ||
ā yastasthau bhuvanānyamartyo viśvāni somaḥ pari tānyarṣati |
kṛṇvantaṃsaṃcṛtaṃ vicṛtaṃmabhiṣṭaye induḥ siṣaktyupasaṃ na sūryaḥ ||
2 ||
ā | yaḥ | tasthau | bhuvanāni | amartyaḥ | viśvāni | somaḥ | pari | tāni | arṣāti | kṛṇvan | sam'cṛtam | vi'cṛtam | abhiṣṭaye | induḥ | siṣakti | upasam | na | sūryaḥ || 2 ||
<sup>v</sup>yaḥ <sup>v</sup>amartyaḥ davaḥ <sup>v</sup>somaḥ <sup>v</sup>bhuvanāni lokān <sup>v</sup>ā <sup>v</sup>tasthau āsthitavān <sup>v</sup>tāni <sup>v</sup>viśvāni sarvāṇi bhuvanāni <sup>v</sup>pari <sup>v</sup>arṣāti parito gacchati |
parito rakṣatītyarthaḥ |
so'yam <sup>v</sup>induḥ yajñaṃ yajamānaṃ vā <sup>v</sup>saṃcṛtaṃ devaiḥ phalair<sup>2</sup>saṃbaddhaṃ <sup>v</sup>kṛṇvan kurvan <sup>v</sup>vicṛtam asurādibhirduḥkhairvā vimuktaṃ<sup>3</sup> <sup>v</sup>kṛṇvan abhito yāgāya <sup>v</sup>siṣakti sevate yajñam |
<sup>v</sup>upasaṃ <sup>v</sup>na <sup>v</sup>sūryaḥ |
sūrya ugasamiva yathā <sup>v</sup>abhiṣṭaye abhito gamanāya prāṇināṃ saṃcṛtaṃ prakāśaiḥ saṃyuktaṃ vicṛtaṃ tamobhirvimuktaṃ ca lokaṃ kurvanupasaṃ sevate tadvat ||
ā yo gobhiḥ sṛjat oṣadhīṣvā devānāṃ sumna iṣayannupāvasuḥ |
ā vidyutā pavate dhārayā suta induḥ somau mādyandaivyaṃ janam ||
3 ||
1. ga-sātā |
2. ga-sukhairvā |
3. ga-viyukte |
ā | yaḥ | gobhiḥ | sṛjyate | oṣadhīṣu | ā | devānām | sunne | iṣayan | upa'vasuḥ | ā | vi'dyutā | pavate | dhārayā | sutaḥ | indram | somaḥ | mādayan | daivyam | janam ||3||
yaḥ somaḥ gobhiḥ raśmibhiḥ ā sṛjyate oṣadhīṣu | yaṃ somaṃ sthāpayantītyarthaḥ | kimartham | devānāṃ sunne sukhe nimitte sati | kīdṛśo'yam | iṣayan devān prāptumicchan | ‘iṣa gatau’ | dhanamicchan vā | tathā upavāsuḥ śatrubhyaḥ sakāśāt prāptadhanaḥ | saḥ somaḥ vidyutā vidyotamānayā dhārayā ā pavate sutaḥ san | kiṃ kurvan | daivyaṃ devasvāminam indraṃ mādayan ||
eṣa sya somaḥ pavate sahasrajitnvāno vācamiṣirāṃyurbudham |
induḥ samudramudiyarti vāyubhirendrasya hārdi kalaśeṣu sīdati ||
4 ||

eṣa syaḥ saḥ somaḥ pavate pūyate |
kīdṛśa eṣaḥ |
sahasrajit sahasrasya jetā |
kiṃ kurvan |

kīdṛśīṃ vācam |
iṣirāṃ gamanaśīlāṃ stutyaṃ prati gantrīm uparbudham upasi prabuddhām |
so'yam induḥ somaḥ samudraṃ samudriyaṃ rasam udiyarti udgamayati vāyubhiḥ gantubhiradhvaryuvādibhiḥ vāyubhireva vā prīyamāṇaḥ san |
tathā indrasya hārdi |
hārdaṃ priyam |

abhi tyaṃ gāvaḥ paryasā payovṛdhaṃ somaṃ śrīṇanti matibhiḥ svivirdam |
dhanuṃjyaḥ pavate kṛtvyo raso vipraḥ kaviḥ kāvyenā svarcasāḥ ||
5 ||
abhi | tyam | gāvaḥ | paryasā | payaḥ'vṛdham | somam | śrīṇanti | mati'bhiḥ | svaḥ'vidam | dhanam'jayaḥ | pavate | kṛtvyaḥ | rasaḥ | vipraḥ | kaviḥ | kāvyena | svaḥ'rcasāḥ || 5 ||
tyaṃ taṃ payovṛdhaṃ payaso vardhakaṃ somaṃ gāvaḥ paryasā svakīyena kṣīreṇa śrīṇanti śrayaṇaṃ kurvanti |
yaḥ matibhiḥ stutibhiḥ sarvaṃ prayacchati taṃ svarvividaṃ somaṃ śrīṇanti |
saḥ dhanaṃjayaḥ śatrū- dhanānāṃ jetā somaḥ kāvyena karmaṇā pavate pūyate |
kīdṛśaḥ saḥ |
kṛtvyaḥ karmaṇyaḥ rasaḥ rasarūpaḥ vipraḥ medhāvī kaviḥ krāntaprajñaḥ svarcasāḥ sarvāṅgaḥ |
cana ityananāma ||
9 ||
‘indrāya’ iti dvādaśarcamaṣṭādaśaṃ sūktaṃ bhṛgugotrasya venasyārṣe pavamānasomadevatākam |
ekādaśī- dvādaśyau triṣṭubhau śiṣṭā jagatyaḥ |
tathā cānukrāntam—‘indrāya dvādaśa veno bhāgavo dvitriṣubantam’ iti |
gato viniyogaḥ ||
indrāya soma suṣṭutaḥ pari sravāpāmīvā bhavatu rakṣasā saha |
mā te rasasya matsata dvāvino draviṇasvanta iha santudevaḥ ||
1 ||
1. bhara-daivyaṃ janaṃ |
2. ga-tyaṃ taṃ somaṃ gāvaḥ payasā abhīśrīṇanti |
kīdṛśaṃ |
payovṛdhaṃ payovardhakaṃ matibhiḥ stutibhiḥ svarvividaṃ sarvasya lambhayitāraṃ |
dhanaṃjayaṃ jetā kṛtvyaḥ karmaṇyaḥ rasarūpaḥ vipraḥ medhāvī kaviḥ kāvyena kavikarmaṇā svarcasāḥ sarvāṅgaḥ |
ya evaṃbhūtastaṃ somamabhīśrīṇantītyanvayaḥ |
indrāya | soma | su'sutaḥ | pari | srava | arpa | abhīvā | bhavatu | rakṣasā | saha | mā | te | rasasya | matsata | dvayāvinaḥ | draviṇasvantaḥ | iha | santu | indavaḥ || 1 ||
he soma tvaṃ suputaḥ suṣṭhu abhiputaḥ san indrārtha pari srava paritaḥ srava | gaccha | rasaṃ muñca | amīcā rogaḥ rakṣasā saha apa bhavatu apagato viyukto bhavatu | te tava rasasya svāṃśaṃ rasaṃ pītvā mā matsata mā mādyantu | ke | dvayāvinaḥ | dvayaṃ sadhyānṛtam | tena yuktāḥ | pāpina ityarthaḥ | kiṃca indavaḥ te rasāḥ iha asmin yajñe draviṇasvantaḥ dhanavantaḥ santu bhavantu ||
asmānsamarye pavamāna codaya dakṣo devānāmsi hi priyo madaḥ |
jahi śatrūbhyā bhandanāyataḥ pibeṃndra somamarva no mṛdho jahi ||
2 ||
asmān | sa'marye | patramāna | codaya | dakṣaḥ | devānām | asi | hi | priyaḥ | madaḥ | jahi | śatrūn | abhi | ā | bhandanāyataḥ | piba | indra | somam | arva | naḥ | mṛdhaḥ | jahi ||2||
he pavamāna soma asmān samarye saṃgrāme codaya preraya | dakṣaḥ hi asi samarthaḥ khalu bhavasi devānāṃ madhye | yadvā | devānāṃ prīṇanāya dakṣo'si | yadvā | dakṣastvaṃ devānāṃ priyaḥ priyakaro madaḥ mādayitāsi hi | jahi ca śatrūn asmākam | abhyā gaccha cāsmān prati bhandanāyataḥ | bhandanā | stutayaḥ | bhandate stutikarmaṇaḥ | tā icchataḥ | piba ca indra somam ava jahi ca naḥ asmākaṃ mṛdhaḥ saṃgrāmāt ||

3 ||
ardabdhaḥ | indo iti | pavase | madina'tamaḥ | ātmā | indrasya | bhavasi | dhāsiḥ | ut'tamaḥ | abhi | svaranti | bahavaḥ | manīṣiṇaḥ | rājānam | asya | bhuvanasya | niṃsate || 3 ||

4 ||
sahasra'nīthaḥ | śata'dhāraḥ | adbhuta | indrāya | induḥ | pavate | kāmyam | madhu | jayan | kṣetram | abhi | arṣa | jayan | apaḥ | urum | naḥ | gātum | kṛṇu | soma | mīḍhvaḥ || 4 ||
sahasranīthaḥ bahuprakāranayanaḥ śatadhāraḥ aparimitadhāropetaḥ adbhutaḥ āścaryakaro mahān induḥ indrāya kāmyaṃ madhu pavate |

1. ga-ma5-saṃgacchate na; tā1.2.4.5.6-mu-gacchati vā; tā3-saṃgacchate ca; bhā1.7-gacchate ca |
karnikadat‌kulaśe gobhirjayase vya1vyayaṃ sumāyā vāramarṣasi | mṛjyamāno atyo na sānusirindrasya soma jaṭhare samakṣara: || 5 || karnikadat | kulaśe | gobhi: | ajayase | vi | avyayam | sumāyā | vāram | arhasi | mṛjyamāna: | atya: | na | sānusi: | indrasya | soma | jaṭhare | sam | akṣara: || 5 || he soma karnikadat śabdaṃ kurvan kalaśe vartamāna: gobhi: payobhi: ajayase sikto bhavasi | avyayam aviyamaṃ vāraṃ vālaṃ daśāpavitraṃ samayā tatsamīpe vi arhasi vividhaṃ gacchasi | mṛjya- māna: śodhyamāna: atyo na aśva iva sānusi: saṃbhajanaśīlastvaṃ he soma indrasya jaṭhare samakṣara: samyak kṣarasi ||
svādu: parvasva divyāya janmane svādurindrāya suhavītunāmne | svādurmitrāya varuṇāya vāyave bṛhaspataye madhumā~ adābhya: || 6 || svādu: | parvasva | divyāya | janmane | svādu: | indrāya | suhavītunāmne | svādu: | mitrāya | varuṇāya | vāyave | bṛhaspataye | madhumān | adābhya: || 6 || he soma svādu: tvaṃ divyāya janmane devagaṇāya parvasva | tathā suhavītunāmne śobhanā- hnānamabhreyāya indrāya svādu: tvaṃ parvasva | mitrāya varuṇāya vāyave bṛhaspataye ca parvasva madhumān madhurarasa: adābhya: anyairahiṃsyastvam || || 10 ||
atyaṃ mṛjanti kalaśe daśa kṣipa: pra viprāṇāṃ matayo vācaṃ īrate | parvamānā abhyarṣanti suṣṭutimendraṃ viśanti madirāsa indava: || 7 || atyam | mṛjanti | kalaśe | daśa | kṣipa: | pra | viprāṇām | mataya: | vācam | īrate | parvamānā: | abhi | arṣanti | mṛḍastutim | ā | indram | viśanti | madirāsa: | indava: ||7|| atyam atanavantamaśvasthānīyaṃ vā somaṃ kalaśe daśa kṣipa: daśāṅgulayo'dhvaryu- saṃbandhinya: mṛjanti śodhayanti | tathā viprāṇāṃ madhye mataya: stotāra: vāca: stutī: īrate prerayanti | pavamānā: somā: abhyarṣanti abhigacchanti suṣṭutiṃ śobhanastutim | indraṃ madi- rāsa: madakarā: indava: somā: ā viśanti praviśanti ||
pavamāno abhyarṣā suvīryamurvī gavyūrti mahi śarma sapratha: | mārkino asya pariṣpūtirīśatendo jayaima tvayā dhanaṃdhanam || 8 || pavamāna: | abhi | arṣā | suvīryam | urvīm | gavyūrtim | mahi | śarma | sapratha: | mārki: | na: | asya | pariṣpūti: | īśata | indo iti | jayaima | tvayā | dhanam dhanam ||8|| he soma pavamāna: pūyamānastvaṃ suvīryamurvī mahatīṃ gavyūrtiṃ gomārgaṃ ca mahi mahat sapratha: sarvata: pṛthu śarma gṛhaṃ sukhaṃ vā abhyarṣa abhigamaya | na: asmākam asya karmaṇa:

^paribhūti: hiṃsāyā: pariprerako dveṣī ^māki: ^īśata medharo bhavatu |

adhi ghārmasthādṛṣabho vicakṣaṇo'rūrucad divo raucanā kavi: |
rājā pavitramatyeti roruvad diva: pīyūṣaṃ duhate nṛcakṣasa: ||
9 ||
adhi | ghām | asthāt | vṛṣabha: | vi'lakṣaṇa: | arūrucat | vi | diva: | rocanā | kavi: | rājā | pavitram | ati | eti | roruvat | diva: | pīyūṣam | duhate | nu'cakṣasa: || 9 ||
^adhi ^asthāt ^dyāṃ dhulokaṃ ^vṛṣabha: varṣitā ^vicakṣaṇa: vidraṣṭā ayaṃ soma: |
tathā kṛtvā ^diva: dhulokasambandhīni ^rocanā rocamānāni nakṣatrādīni ^vi ^arūrucat vividhaṃ rocayati |
^kavi: krāntaprajña: san ^rājā soma: ^pavitraṃ daśāpavitram ^atyeti atikramya gacchati |
^roruvat śabdaṃ kurvan |
^diva: dhulokasya ^pīyūṣaṃ sāraṃ rasaṃ ^nṛcakṣasa: nṛṇāṃ draṣṭāra: somā: ^duhate sravanti |
svakīyaṃ rasamāntarikṣamudakaṃ vā duhantītyartha: ||
divo nāke madhujihva: asaśratau venā duhantyukṣaṇaṃ giriṣṭhām |
apsu drapsaṃ vāvṛdhānaṃ samudre ā sindhorūrmā madhumantaṃ pavitra ā ||
10 ||
diva: | nāke | madhu'jihva: | asaśrata: | venā: | duhanti | ukṣaṇam | giri'sthām | apsu | drapsam | vavṛdhānam | samudre | ā | sindho: | ūrmā | madhu'mantam | pavitra | ā || 10 ||
^diva: dyotamānasya yajñasya sambandhini ^nāke du:kharahite havirdhānākhye sthāne ^madhujihva: madhuravāca: ^asaśrata: asaṃsaktā: |
pṛthakpṛthaggatyartha: |
yadvā ciramakṛtvā1 śīghramabhipuṇvantaḥ ^venā: etannāmakā maharṣaya: ^duhanti abhipuṇvanti |
yadvā dhuloka eva venā: kāntā devā duhanti |
taṃ somam ^ukṣaṇaṃ settāraṃ ^giriṣṭhāṃ girāvṛjate deśe vartamānam ^apsu udakeṣu vasatīvarīvantaḥ2 ^vavṛdhānaṃ2 vardhamānaṃ ^drapsaṃ rasarūpaṃ ^samudre samudravat pravṛddhe droṇakalaśe ^sindho: udakasya ^ūrmā ūrmau pūre ^ā siñcantīti śeṣa: |
tadartha ^madhumantaṃ mādhuyepitaṃ rasaṃ ^pavitre daśāpavitre ^ā siñcantīti śeṣa: ||
nāke suparṇamupapattivāṃsaṃ giro venānāmakṛpanta pūrvī: |
śiśuṃ rihanti mataya: parnitamataṃ hiraṇyayaṃ śakunaṃ kṣārmāṇa sthā ||
11 ||
nāke | su'parṇam | upa'patti'vāṃsam | gira: | venānām | akṛpanta | pūrvī: | śiśum | rihanti | mataya: | parnitamatam | hiraṇyayam | śakunam | kṣārmāṇa | sthā || 11 ||
^nāke dhuloke vartamānaṃ paścādutpatantaṃ somaṃ ^venānām asmākaṃ sambandhinya: ^pūrvī: bahvya: ^gira: stutaya: ^upa ^akṛpanta upakalpante |
abhiṣṭuvantītyartha: |
^taṃ ^śiśuṃ śiśuvat saṃskṛtavyaṃ somaṃ ^mataya: stutaya: ^rihanti3 lihanti saṃstṛśanti |
prāpnuvantītyartha: |
kīdṛśaṃ śiśum |
^panimataṃ śabdāyantam |
panateryaṅ‌lukantāta śatari abhyāsasya nigāma upadhālopaśca |
^hiraṇyayaṃ hiraṇmayaṃ ^śakunaṃ pakṣiṇaṃ ^kṣārmāṇa kṣamāyāṃ ^sthāṃ havirdhāne sthāṃ vartamānam ||
1. ga-cirapravṛttā:; ta-bha1.2.6-ciramabhṛtvā; bha4-cirasadhṛtvā |
2. ga-vasatīvarīṣu vatru°; ta1. 2.4.5-bha7-vasatīvarīṣu tervatru°; ta3-bha1.2.4-vasatīvarīṣu tairvatru°; ta7-bha6-vasatīvarīṣu te vātru° |
3. ta-parihanti |
ūrdhvo gandharvo adhi nāke asthādviśvā rūpā praticakṣāṇo asya |
bhānuḥ śukreṇa śociṣā vyadyautprārūrucadrodasī mātarā śuciḥ ||
12 ||
ūrdhvaḥ | gandharvaḥ | adhi | nāke | asthāt | viśvā | rūpā | prati'cakṣāṇaḥ | asya | bhānuḥ | śukreṇa | śociṣā | vi | adyaut | pra | arūrucat | rodasī iti | mātarā | śuciḥ ||12||
^ūrdhvaḥ unnata: ^gandharvaḥ raśmīnāṃ dhāraka: soma: ^nāke āditye ^adhi ^asthāt adhitiṣṭhati |
kiṃ kurvan |
^asya ādityasya ^viśvā viśvāni rūpāṇi ^praticakṣāṇaḥ pratipaśyan |
^bhānuḥ ādityaḥ somādhiṣṭhita: san ^śukreṇa dīptena ^śociṣā tejasā ^vyadyaut viyotate |
na kevalaṃ svayameva api tu ^mātarā nirmātryau ^rodasī dyāvāpṛthivyau ^prārūrucat prarocayati |
^śuciḥ dīpta: sūryaḥ ||
11||
4||
atha pañcame'nuvāka ekādaśa sūktāni |
tatra 'pra te' ityaṣṭācatvāriṃśadacaṃ prathamaṃ sūktam |
prathamadaśarcasya ākṛṣṭā^2 iti māpā iti ca dvināmāna ṛṣigaṇā^1 draṣṭāraḥ |
dvitīyasya daśarcasya sikatā iti nīvāvarī iti dvināmāna ṛṣigaṇāḥ |
tṛtīyasya daśarcasya pṛṣva ityajā iti ca nāmadvayopetā ṛṣigaṇāḥ |
aṣṭārthameṣāṃ dvināmatvamavagantavyam |
caturthasya daśarcasya ākṛṣṭā māpā ityādidvināmānakhyo gaṇā draṣṭāraḥ |
evaṃ catvāriṃśadṛtaḥ |
atha pañcānāṃ bhaumo'trirṛṣiḥ |
natasimaṇāṃ gṛtsamadaḥ |
jagatī chandaḥ |
pavamānaḥ somo devatā |
tathā cānukrāntaṃ---'pra te'ṣṭācatvāriṃśa- daṛṣigaṇā daśarcā ākṛṣṭā māpāḥ prathame sikatā nīvāvarī dvitīye pṛṣvo'jāstṛtīye trayaścaturthe'triḥ pañcānyāstistro gṛtsamadaḥ' iti |
gato viniyogaḥ ||
pra te āśarvaḥ pavamāna dhījavo madā arṣanti raghujāīva tmanā |
divyāḥ suparṇā madhumanta indavo madantamāsaḥ pari kośamāsate ||
1 ||
pra | te | āśarvaḥ | pavamāna | dhī'jarvaḥ | madāḥ | arṣanti | raghujāḥ'iva | tmanā | divyāḥ | su'parṇāḥ | madhu'mantaḥ | indavaḥ | madantamāsaḥ | pari | kośam | āsate ||1||
he ^pavamāna pūyamāna soma ^te tava ^āśarvaḥ vyāptāḥ ^dhījatrayaḥ manovegāḥ |
^madāḥ madakarā rasāḥ ^arṣanti gacchanti |
^tmanā ātmanaiva |
anāyāsenetyarthaḥ |
ka 'iva |
^raghujāiva |
raghuḥ śīghragā vaḍā^3 |
tatra jātā raghujāḥ |
te iva nirgatāste ^divyāḥ divi bhavāḥ |
antarikṣe daśā- pavitre dhāryamāṇā ityarthaḥ |
^suparṇāḥ supatanāḥ ^madhumantaḥ mādhuryopetāḥ ^madantamāsaḥ atiśayena mādayitṛtamāḥ ^indavaḥ dīptā rasāḥ ^kośaṃ droṇakalaśaṃ ^pari ^āsate paryupaviśanti ||
pra te madāso madirāsa āśarvo'sṛkṣata rathyāso yathā pṛthak |
dhenurna vatsaṃ paryasābhi vajriṇamindramindavo madhumanta ūrmayaḥ ||
2 ||
pra | te | madāḥ | madirāsaḥ | āśarvaḥ | asṛkṣata | rathyāḥ'saḥ | yathā | pṛthak | dhenuḥ | na | vatsam | paryasābhi | vajriṇam | indram | indavaḥ | madhu'mantaḥ | ūrmayaḥ ||2||
1. ga-prādīpayaddhi |
2. ga-bha5.6-pṛśva iti yājā (bha6-yajā) iti ca nāmagaṇāḥ |
3. ga-raghavaḥ śīghravegā athāḥ; ta6-raghuḥ śīghravāḍavā |
kra. 4-11
pra asukṣata prasṛjyante te tava madirāṃsaḥ madakarāḥ āśavaḥ vyāptāḥ madāsaḥ madā rasāḥ ka iva |
rathyāsaḥ rathā aśvāḥ te yathā tathā pṛthak pramṛjyante |
te madhumantaḥ mādhuryapetāḥ ūrmayaḥ patrarūpāḥ indravaḥ somāḥ dhenuḥ payasā na yamam iva svarasena vajrimindram abhi gacchanti ||
atyo na hiyāno abhi vājarmapa svarvitkośaṃ divo adrimātaram |
vṛṣā pavitre adhi sānau avyaye somaḥ punāna indriyāya dhāryase ||
3 ||
atyaḥ | na | hiyānaḥ | abhi | vājam | arpa | svaḥ | vit | kośam | divaḥ | adri'mātaram | vṛṣā | pavitre | adhi | sānau | avyaye | somaḥ | punānaḥ | indriyāya | dhāryase || 3 ||
atyo na aśva iva hiyānaḥ preryamāṇaḥ vājaṃ saṃgrāmam abhi arpa abhigaccha |
tathā svarvit sarvavit kośaṃ droṇakalaśaṃ divaḥ dyulokāt adrimātaram |
adrimeghaḥ |
tena tajjanya-mudakaṃ lakṣyate |
tasya nirmotāramabhyarpa |
atha pratyakṣakṛtaḥ |
vṛṣā varṣakaḥ somaḥ avyaye avisaye adhi sānau |
ardhīti saptamyarthānuvādī |
samuccite tasmin punānaḥ pūyamāno bhavati |
kimartham |
dhāryase dhārakāya indriyāya tadartham ||
pra tu āśvinīḥ pavamāna dhījuvo divyā asṛgran paryasā dharīmaṇi |
prāntarpayaḥ sthāvirīrasṛkṣata ye tvā mujantyṛpiṣāṇa vedhasaḥ ||
4 ||
pra | te | āśvinīḥ | pavamāna | dhī'juvaḥ | divyāḥ | asṛgran | paryasā | dharīmaṇi | pra | antaḥ | ṛṣayaḥ | sthāvirīḥ | amṛkṣata | ye | tvā | mujanti | ṛpi'sāna | vedhasaḥ ||4||
he pavamāna soma te tava āśvinīḥ vyāptāḥ |
aśvā vyāptāḥ ityasmādauṇādiko viniḥ |
tato'ṇ |
vyanyeyenānudāttaḥ |
dhījuvaḥ manovegāḥ divyāḥ divi bhavā divaḥ patantyo dhārāḥ payasā yuktāḥ dharīmaṇi dhārake droṇakalaśe pra asṛgran gacchanti |
ye vedhasaḥ vidhātāraḥ ṛṣayaḥ he soma ṛpiṣāṇa ṛpibhiḥ saṃbhakta tvā tvāṃ mṛjanti abhipṛṇvanti te ṛṣayaḥ sthāvirīḥ sthavirā dhārāḥ antaḥ madhye pātrasyāntaḥ pra amṛkṣata pramṛjanti ||
viśvā dhāmāni viśvacakṣa ṛbhvaṃsaḥ prabhostai sutaḥ pari yanti ketave |
vyānuśiḥ pavase sobha dharmabhiḥ patirviśvasya bhuvanasya rājasi ||
5 ||
viśvā | dhāmāni | viśva'cakṣaḥ | ṛbhvaṃsaḥ | prabhoḥ | te | sutaḥ | pari | yanti | ketavaḥ | vi'ānuśiḥ | pavame | soma | dharmabhiḥ | patiḥ | viśvasya | bhuvanasya | rājasi || 5 ||
he viśvacakṣaḥ sarvasya draṣṭaḥ soma prabhoḥ parivṛḍhasya yataḥ te tava ṛbhvasaḥ ṛbhvā iti mahannāma |
mahantaḥ ketavaḥ raśmayaḥ viśvā sarvāṇi dhāmāni tejaḥsthānāni devaśarīrāṇi pari yanti parigacchanti |
prakāśayantītyarthaḥ |
he soma vyānuśiḥ vyāpanaśīlaśca tvaṃ dharmabhiḥ dhārakaiḥ rasinyandaiḥ pavase pūyase |
viśvasya bhuvanasya ca patiḥ svāmī tvaṃ rājasi īśvaro bhavasi ||
12||
ubhayataḥ parvamānasya raśmayau dhruvasya sutaḥ pari yanti ketave |
yadī pavitre adhi mṛjyate hariḥ sattā ni yonā kalaśeṣu sīdati ||
6 ||
1. ga-pavitre sānau; ta1.7-pavitre eṣāpavitre |
2. ta1.2.4.7-bhara-'divaḥ' nāsti |
ubhayataḥ | parvamānasya | raśmayaḥ | dhruvasya | sutaḥ | pari | yanti | ketavaḥ | yadi | pavitrai | adhi | mṛjyate | hāriḥ | sattā | ni | yonā | kalaśeṣu | sīdati || 6||
pavamānasya pūyamānasya dhruvasya svayamavicalitasya sata: vidyamānasya somasya ketavaḥ prajñāpakāḥ raśmayaḥ ubhayataḥ itaścāmutaśca pari yanti parigacchanti |
abhipavasamaya evaṃ bhavati |
yadi yadā pavitre daśāpavitre hariḥ haritavarṇo'yam adhi mṛjyate tadānīṃ sattā sadana- śīla'yaṃ yonau svīye sthāne kalaśeṣu droṇakalaśādiṣu ni pīdati ||
yajñasya ketuḥ parvate svadhvaraḥ somo devānāmupa yāti niṣkṛtam |
sahasradhāraḥ pari kośamarṣati vṛṣā pavitramatyeti roravat ||
7 ||
yajñasya | ketuḥ | pavate | su'adhvaraḥ | somaḥ | devānām | upa | yāti | niḥ'kṛtam | sahasra'dhāraḥ | pari | kośam | arpati | vṛṣā | pavitram | atyeti | roravat || 7 ||
yajñasya ketuḥ prajñāpakaḥ svadhvaraḥ śobhanayāgaḥ somaḥ pavate pūyate abhipūyate |
tādṛśaḥ somaḥ devānāṃ niṣkṛtaṃ saṃskṛtaṃ sthānam upa yāti uprācchati |
tadarthaṃ sahasradhāraḥ kośaṃ droṇakalaśaṃ pari arpati |
natastadarthaṃ vṛṣā sekāya roravat śabdayan pavitram atikramya gacchatyadhodeśam ||
rājā samudraṃ nadyo vi gāhete'pāmūrmiṃ saṃcate sindhuṣu śritaḥ |
adhyasthātsānu parvamāno avyayaṃ nābhā pṛthivyā dharuṇo maho divaḥ ||
8 ||
rājā | samudram | nadyaḥ | vi | gāhete | apām | ūrmim | saṃcate | sindhuṣu | śritaḥ | adhi | asthāt | sānu | parvamānaḥ | avyayam | nābhā | pṛthivyāḥ | dharuṇaḥ | mahaḥ | divaḥ ||8||
ayaṃ somaḥ rājā samudram antarikṣaṃ nadyaḥ tatratyā apaśca vi gāhete |
vigāhya ca apāmūrmiṃ saṃcate udakapūraṃ sravati |
sindhuṣu udakeṣu graheṣu vā śritaḥ san |
hūyamānaḥ san raśmidvārā sūrya prāpya annarikṣe meghepu vartata ityarthaḥ |
asyaiva vṛṣṭisādhanatvāt |
yadvā |
lusopamametat |
samudraṃ nadya iva sindhuṣu vasatīvarīṣu śrita āśritaḥ 'man samudram abhipūtasomarasādhārapātraṃ vi gāhete abhipavātpūrvam |
paścāt pavamānaḥ pūyamānaḥ avyayaṃ sānu samucchritaṃ daśāpavitram adhyasthāt adhitiṣṭhati |
kutra deśe |
nābhā pṛthivyāḥ nābhisthānīye yajñe |
'tatsaṃbandhidhani havirdhāna ityarthaḥ |
'yajñamāhurbhuvanasya nābhim' ( tai. saṃ. 7.4.18.2 ) iti śruteḥ |
kīdṛśaḥ |
maho divaḥ gulokasya dharuṇaḥ dhartā ||
divo na sānuṃ stanayannacikradadyauśca yasya pṛthivī ca dharmabhiḥ |
indrasya sūryyaṃ pavate vivevidatsomaḥ punānaḥ kalaśeṣu sīdati ||
9 ||
divaḥ | na | sānu | stanayan | acikradat | dyauḥ | ca | yasya | pṛthivī | ca | dharmabhiḥ | indrasya | sūryyam | pavate | vi'vevidat | somaḥ | punānaḥ | kalaśeṣu | sīdati || 9 ||
1. gata-bha- 'niṣkṛta' nāsti |
2. bha1(2)-atikramya eti |
3. ga-tasyaiva |
atha ṛgvedasaṃhitā
maṇḍala 7, sūkta 3, mantra 14
'divo 'na 'sānu 'stanayan 'dyulokasya 'samucchritaṃ 'sthānaṃ 'śabdayanniva 'avikradat 'krandate |
'yasya 'somasya 'dharmabhiḥ 'dhāraṇeḥ 'cauśca 'pṛthivī 'ca 'dhṛte 'syātām |
'vṛṣṭipradānadvārāpyadhyāyutpādanena devānāṃ 'havirbhāvena 'ubhayadhārakatvaṃ 'prasiddham |
'tādṛśaḥ 'somaḥ 'mahānubhāvaḥ 'somaḥ 'punānaḥ 'pūyamānaḥ 'indrasya 'sakhyaṃ 'vivididat 'pavate 'pyūyate 'pavitre |
'saḥ 'punānaḥ 'somaḥ 'karuśeṣu 'sīdati 'nivasati ||
jyotiryajñasya pavate madhu priyaṃ pitā devānāṃ janitā vibhūrvasuḥ |
dadhāti ratnaṃ svadhayorapīcyaṃ maditamo manmura indriyo rasaḥ ||
10 ||
jyotiḥ | yajñasya | pavate | madhu | priyam | pitā | devānām | janitā | vibhūrvasuḥ | dadhāti | ratnam | svadhayoḥ | apīcyam | mada
tathā ᵛvājasya annasya balasya vā lābhāya ᵛmahādhanaṃ saṃgrāmaṃ ᵛbhajate |
saḥ ᵛsvāyudhaḥ ᵛvṛṣā varṣakaḥ momaḥ ᵛsotṛbhiḥ abhipavakartṛbhiḥ ᵛpūyate ||
ayaṃ ̭ma॒tavā̭ñchaku॒no ya॒thā hi॒to'nyai̭ sa॒sāra̭ pa॒rvamāna॒ ūrmi̭ṇā | tava॒ kratvā॒ roda̭sī a॒ntarā̭ ka॒ve śu॒cirdhi॒yā pa̭vate॒ soma̭ indra te || 13 || ayam | ma॒ta'vān | śaku॒naḥ | ya॒thā | hi॒taḥ | a॒nyai | sa॒sāra̭ | pa॒rvamānaḥ | ūrmi̭ṇā | tava̭ | kratvā̭ | roda̭sī iti̭ | a॒ntarā̭ | ka॒ve | śu॒ciḥ | dhi॒yā | pa॒vate | soma̭: | indra̭ | te ||13|| ᵛayaṃ somaḥ ᵛmatavān | manaṃ saṃmataṃ priyaṃ ratnetram | tadvān ᵛpavamānaḥ pūyamānaḥ śodhayamānaśca san ᵛhitaḥ preritaḥ ᵛśakunaḥ pakṣī ᵛyathā śīghraṃ gacchati tathā ᵛagne pavitra ᵛūrmiṇā rasena ᵛmasāra gacchati | he ᵛkave krāntaprajña anucāna vā | 'ye vā anucānāste kavayaḥ' (ai. brā. 2. 38) iti śruteḥ | tādṛśa yajamāna yadvā uktalakṣaṇa ᵛindra ᵛte tava ᵛkratvā karmaṇā ᵛdhiyā prajñayā ca | yadvā | viśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāvaḥ | dhiyā dhārakeṇa kratvā karmaṇā ᵛrodasī ᵛantarā rodasyorvāvyāptidhryāntantarā ᵛśuciḥ ᵛsomaḥ ᵛpavate ṣyate ||
drā॒piṃ va̭sāno॒ yajña̭to di॒vispṛ̭śa॒mantari̭kṣaprā॒ bhuva̭ne॒ṣvarpi̭taḥ | sva॒rja̭no nabha̭gā॒bhya̭kramī॒tprata̭masya pi॒tara̭mā vi॒vāsa̭ti || 14 || drā॒pim | va॒sāna̭ḥ | ya॒jñata̭ḥ | di॒vi'spṛ̭śam | a॒ntari̭kṣa'prāḥ | bhuva̭neṣu | arpi̭taḥ | svaḥ | ja॒jñāna̭ḥ | nabha̭gā | a॒bhi | a॒kramī̭t | pra॒tam | a॒sya | pi॒tara̭m | ā | vi॒vāsa̭ti ||14|| ᵛdivispṛśaṃ divispṛṣṭāraṃ ᵛdrāpiṃ kavacaṃta tejorūpaṃ ᵛvasānaḥ ācchādayan ᵛyajataḥ yaṣṭavyaḥ ᵛanta- rikṣaprāḥ antarikṣasya pūraka udakena tādṛśaḥ somaḥ ᵛbhuvaneṣu udakeṣu ᵛarpitaḥ ᵛsvaḥ sarva svena pāyayi- tavyaṃ devasaṃghaṃ svargaṃ vā ᵛjajñānaḥ janayan | athavā | svarudakam | tajjanayan | ᵛnabhamā udakena ᵛabhya- kramīt abhikrāmati | ᵛasya udakasya ᵛpitaraṃ pālakaṃ ᵛpraśnaṃ purāṇamindram ᵛā ᵛvivāsati paricarati ||
so a̭sya॒ viśḙ ma॒hi śarmma̭ yacchati॒ yo a̭sya॒ dhāma̭ pra॒thama॒ṃ vyā̭na॒śe | pu॒daṃ yada̭sya pa॒rame॒ vyo̭ma॒nyato॒ viśvā̭ a॒bhi saṃ yā̭ti॒ saṃya̭taḥ || 15 || saḥ | a॒sya | viśḙ | ma॒hi | śarmma̭ | ya॒cchati̭ | yaḥ | a॒sya | dhāma̭ | pra॒thamam | vi'ā̭na॒śe | padam | yat | a॒sya | pa॒ramḙ | vi'o̭mani | a॒taḥ | viśvā̭ | a॒bhi | mam | yā॒ti | sa॒m'yata̭ḥ ||15|| ᵛsaḥ somaḥ ᵛasya indrasya ᵛviśe praveśanāya ᵛmahi mahat ᵛśarma sukhaṃ ᵛyacchati | ᵛyaḥ somaḥ ᵛasya indrasya ᵛdhāma tejoyuktaṃ śarīraṃ ᵛprathamam itaradevaprāpte pūrvaṃ ᵛvyānaśe prāptavān | ᵛyat yasya ᵛasya somasya ᵛparame utkṛṣṭe ᵛvyoman viśeṣeṇa rakṣaka ke dhuloke vayāṃ vā ᵛpadaṃ bhavati | ᵛyataḥ yasmāt somāt nṛsa indraḥ somaḥ svayaṃ vā ᵛviśvāḥ ᵛsaṃyataḥ sarvān saṃgrāmān ᵛabhi ᵛyāti samyagabhigacchati | sa somo mahi śarma yacchatīti saṃbandhaḥ || || 14 ||

^induḥ somaḥ ^indrasya ^niṣkṛtaṃ sthānamudaraṃ ^pro ^ayāsīt ^praiva gacchati |

tave | imāḥ | pra'jāḥ | divyasya | retasaḥ | tvam | viśvasya | bhuvanasya | rājasi | atha | idam | viśvam | pavamāna | te | vaśe | tvam | indo | iti | prathamaḥ | dhāma'dhāḥ | asi || 28 ||
tava divyasya retasaḥ sakāśāt imāḥ prajāḥ utpadyamānāḥ prāṇina utpannāḥ |
'somo vai retodhāḥ' (tai. saṃ. 2.1.1.6) iti śruteḥ |

pasaḥ somaḥ | paviśvāyuḥ sarvasya gantā somaḥ | prajāvatīḥ prajārūpaphaladātrīḥ | pasubharāḥ suṣṭhu hiyamāṇāḥ | paviśvāḥ sarvāḥ | pabhandanāḥ stutīḥ | udiyarti utkṛṣṭaṃ prerayati | kasmin kāle ucyate | paahardivi ahorātrayośca | atha pratyakṣakṛtaḥ | pabrahma parivṛḍhaṃ karma prajāvat prajābhiryuktam | prajāphalaka- mityarthaḥ | yadvā | atra tacca prajāvat prajāyupetam | tathā rayiṃ dhanam | kīdṛśam | paaśvapastyaṃ vyāsa- gṛm | tadubhayaṃ he indo dīsa soma pītaḥ indreṇa pīnasvaṃ tameva indramasmabhyaṃ yācatāt yācasva ||

'pra tu drava' iti navarcaṃ dvitīyaṃ sūktaṃ kāśyasyośanasa ārṣamanādeśaparibhāṣayā traiṣṭubhaṃ pavamānasomadevatākam |

1 ||
pra | tu | drava | pari | kośam | ni | sīda | nṛbhiḥ | punānaḥ | abhi | vājam | arṣa | aśvam | na | tvā | vājinam | marjyantaḥ | acchā | barhiḥ | raśanābhiḥ | nayanti || 1 ||
