yadvā ^karmaṇāṃ pravartayitāsi he ^soma ||
1. ga-manuṣyasaṃbandhīni|
indro na yo muhā karmāṇi cakrirhantā vṛtrāṇāmasi soma pūrbhit | paidro na hi tvamahināmnāṃ hantā viśvasyāsi soma dasyoḥ || 4 || indraḥ | na | yaḥ | mahā | karmāṇi | cakriḥ | hantā | vṛtrāṇām | ami | soma | puḥ'bhit | paidraḥ | na | hi | tvam | ahi'nāmnām | hantā | viśvasya | ami | soma | dasyoḥ || 4 || vindro vna indra iva vyaḥ svaṃ vmahā mahānta vkarmāṇi vcakriḥ tācchīlyena karopi sa svaṃ he vsoma vvṛtrāṇāṃ śatrūṇāṃ vhantā vami bhavasi | tathā vpūrbhit purāṃ bhettāsi | kiṃca vpaidro vna aśva iva yattu vahināmnāṃ vhantā bhavasi | āgamya hantāsyahiḥ | | tannāmakānāmityarthaḥ | | na kevalaṃ teṣāmeva api tu vviśvasya api sarvasyāpi vdasyoḥ upakṣapayituḥ śatrorhantā vasi ||
agnirna yo vana ā sṛjyamāno vṛthā pājāṃsi kṛṇute nadīṣu | jano na yudhvā mahata upabdiriyarti somaḥ parvamāna ūrmim || 5 || agniḥ | na | yaḥ | vaneṃ | ā | sṛjyamānaḥ | vṛthā | pājāṃsi | kṛṇute | nadīṣu | janaḥ | na | yudhvā | mahataḥ | upabdaḥ | iyarti | somaḥ | parvamānaḥ | ūrmim || 5 || vagnirna agniriva vyaḥ somaḥ vvane araṇye vā vsṛjyamānaḥ vanasaṃbandhyapriyathā balāni? kurute' evaṃ yaḥ somaḥ vvṛthā anāyāmena vnadīṣu āntarikṣarāṇi vpājāṃsi vkṛṇute kurute | kiṃca vyudhvā yuddhasya kartā vjano vna ayo manuṣya iva vmahataḥ śatroḥ vupabdaḥ | vāṅnāmainat | śabdayitā san vpavamānaḥ pūyamānaḥ vsomaḥ vūrmi pravavṛddhaṃ rasam viyarti prerayati ||
ete somā ati vārānyavyā divyā na kośaso abhravarṣāḥ | vṛthā samudraṃ sindhavo na nīcīḥ sutāso abhi kalaśā~ asṛgran || 6 || ete | somāḥ | ati | vāryāṇi | avyā | divyāḥ | na | kośasaḥ | abhra'varṣāḥ | vṛthā | samudram | sindhavaḥ | na | nīcīḥ | sutāsaḥ | abhi | kalaśān | asṛgran || 6 || vete pūyamānāḥ vsomāḥ vavyā avisamayāni vvārāṇi vālāni vati gacchantīti śeṣaḥ | tatra dṛṣṭāntaḥ | vdivyā vna kośāmaḥ divi bhavāḥ kośā āpa iva | tā viśeṣyante | vabhravarṣāḥ abhravṛṣṭayamānāḥ | kiṃca vvṛthā anāyāsena vsamudraṃ vsindhavo vna nadya iva vnīcīḥ nīcīnāgrāḥ vsutāsaḥ abhiputāḥ somāḥ vkalaśān vabhi vasṛgran abhigacchanti ||
śuṣmī śardho na mārutaṃ patrasvānbhiśastā divyā yathā viṭ | āpo na makṣu sumatirbhava naḥ sahasrāpsāḥ pratanāpāṇa yajñaḥ || 7 || śuṣmī | śardhaḥ | na | mārutam | patrasva | anabhi'śastā | divyā | yathā | viṭ | āpaḥ | na | makṣu | su'matiḥ | bhava | naḥ | sahasra'apsāḥ | pratanāpāṭ | na | yajñaḥ || 7 || he soma vśuṣmī | śuṣmaṃ balaṃ śoṣaṇāt | balavāṃstvaṃ vmārutaṃ vśardho vna marutāṃ balamiva vpatrasva | tatra dṛṣṭāntameva spaṣṭayati | vyathā vdivyā vviṭ prajā vanabhiśastā | abhiśāso nindā |
1. ga-jvālā vikṛdate |
aninditā pavate |
'maruto vai devānāṃ viśaḥ' (tai. saṃ. 2. 2. 5. 7) iti hi brāhmaṇam |
kiṃca 'āpo 'na udakānīva 'makṣu kṣipaṃ pacamānastvaṃ 'sumatiḥ 'bhava 'naḥ asmākam |
kiṃca 'sahasrāpsāḥ |
apsa iti rūpanāma |

śucirdhamasi priyo na mitro dakṣādyau aryamevāsi soma ||
8 ||
rāīḥ | nu | te | varuṇasya | vratāni | bṛhat | gabhīram | tarva | soma | dhāma | śuciḥ | tvam | asi | priyaḥ | na | mitraḥ | dakṣāyyaḥ | aryamā'iva | asi | soma || 8 ||
he 'soma 'varuṇasya vārakasya 'te tava 'vratāni karmāṇa 'nu kṣipraṃ karomīti śeṣaḥ |
he 'soma 'tava 'dhāma tejaḥsthānaṃ 'bṛhat mahat 'gabhīraṃ ca |

24 ||
'pro sya vahniḥ' iti saptacaṃ caturthaṃ sūktam |
ṛṣyādyāḥ pūrvavat |
'pro sya sasa' ityanukrāntam |
gato viniyogaḥ ||
pro sya vahniḥ pṛthyābhirasyān divo na vṛṣṭiḥ parvamāno akṣāḥ |
sahasradhāro asadat 'sme mātṛrupasthe vana ā ca somaḥ ||
1 ||
pro iti | syaḥ | vahniḥ | pṛthyābhiḥ | asyān | divaḥ | na | vṛṣṭiḥ | parvamānaḥ | akṣāriti | sahasra'dhāraḥ | asadat | 'na | amme iti | mātuḥ | upasthe | vanai | ā | ca | somaḥ || 1 ||
pra ū iti nipātadvayasamudāya eko nipātitaḥ |
'pro 'asyān prasandate 'syaḥ saḥ 'vahniḥ voḍhā 'padhyābhiḥ yajñamārgaiḥ |
'divo 'na 'vṛṣṭiḥ divaḥ sakāśāt vṛṣṭiriva 'pavamānaḥ pūyamānaḥ san 'akṣāḥ vyāpnopi |
sā'yaṃ 'somaḥ 'sahasradhāraḥ bahudhāraḥ san 'asme asmāsu 'ni 'asadat niṣīdati ||
rājā sindhūnāmavasiṣṭa vāṃsaṃ ṛtasya nāvamāruhadrajiṣṭhām |
apsu dṛpso vāvṛdhe śyenajūto duha ī pitā duha ī piturjām ||
2 ||
rājā | sindhūnām | avasiṣṭa | vāsaḥ | ṛtasya | nāvam | ā | aruhat | rajiṣṭhām | ap'su | dṛpsaḥ | vavṛdhe | śyena'jūtaḥ | duhe | īm | pitā | duhe | īm | pituḥ | jām ||2||
ayaṃ 'rājā somaḥ 'sindhūnām udakānāṃ kṣīrādisyandinīnāṃ gavāṃ vā 'vāsaḥ vasanasthānīyaṃ kṣīrādyaṃ śrayaṇadravyam 'avasiṣṭa ācchādayati |
tathā kṛtvā 'rajiṣṭhām ṛjutarām 'ṛtasya yajñasya 'nāvamāruhat ārohati |
saḥ 'dṛpsaḥ sāmarasaḥ 'śyenajūtaḥ śyenapreritāṃ ramaḥ 'apsu vasatī- varīṣu 'vavṛdhe vardhate |
'īṃ tamimaṃ 'pituḥ pālakāt pitṛsthānīyāt dyulokāna 'jāṃ jātamapaśyaṃ somam 'īm ayaṃ 'pitā pālako lokaḥ 'duhe dogdhi rasam |
tathādhvaryurpi 'duhe dogdhi |

1. ga-yaḥ |
siṃhaṃ na̭santa ma॒dhvo̭ a॒yāsa̭ṃ ha॒rima̭ru॒ṣaṃ di॒vo a॒sya pa॒rtim | śūro̭ yu॒tsu pra॒thama̭ḥ pṛ॒cchate॒ gā a॒sya ca̭kṣa॒sā pa॒ri pā॒tyukṣā̭ || 3 || siṃham | na॒santa | ma॒dhvaḥ | a॒yāsa̭m | ha॒rim | a॒ru॒ṣam | di॒vaḥ | a॒sya | pa॒rtim | śūraḥ | yu॒tsu | pra॒thama̭ḥ | pṛ॒cchatḙ | gāḥ | a॒sya | ca̭kṣa॒sā | pa॒ri | pā॒ti | u॒kṣā || 3 || ^siṃhaṃ śatrūṇāṃ hiṃsakaṃ siṃhasadṛśaṃ ^madhvaḥ udakasya ^ayāsaṃ prerakaṃ ^hariṃ haritavarṇam ^asya ^divaḥ dyulokasya ^patiṃ pālakaṃ somaṃ ^nasanta vyāpnuvanti yajamānāḥ | ^yutsu saṃgrāmeṣu ^śūraḥ ^prathamaḥ devānāṃ madhye mukhyaḥ somaḥ ^gāḥ paṇibhirapahṛtāḥ ^pṛcchate mārgajñān | paṇīn hatvā gā labdhuṃ gomārgaṃ pṛcchatītyarthaḥ | kiṃca ^asya ^cakṣasā sāmarthyena ^ukṣā sektā devendraḥ ^pari ^pāti viśvam ||
ma॒dhu॒pṛṣṭha̭ṃ gho॒ramā̭sa॒maśva॒ṃ rathḙ yu॒jantyṷru॒cakra̭ ṛ॒ṣvam | svasā̭ra īṃ jā॒maya̭ ma॒rjaya̭nti sa॒nābha̭yo vā॒jina̭mūrja॒yanti || 4 || ma॒dhu॒'pṛṣṭham | gho॒ram | a॒yāsa̭m | a॒śvam | rathḙ | yu॒janti | u॒ru॒'cakrḙ | ṛ॒ṣvam | svasā̭raḥ | īm | jā॒maya̭ḥ | ma॒rjaya̭nti | sa॒nābha̭yaḥ | vā॒jina̭m | ū॒rja॒yanti || 4 || ^madhupṛṣṭhaṃ madhurapṛṣṭhabhāgaṃ ^ghoraṃ bhayānakam ^ayāsaṃ gantāram ^ṛṣvaṃ darśanīyamuktalakṣaṇāśvasthānīyaṃ vyāsaṃ somam ^urucakre prabhūtacakre ^rathe yathāśvaṃ rathe ^yujanti radhikāstaddhat prabhūtacaraṇaṃ rathe raṃhaṇasya sādhane yajñārūye rathe yujanti | yojayantyadhvarvādyaḥ | kiṃca ^īm enaṃ somaṃ ^svasāraḥ svayaṃ sāriṇyaḥ parasparaṃ svasṛbhūtā vā ^jāmayaḥ bandhubhūtā aṅgulayaḥ | ekahastaniṣpannatvāt svasṛtvaṃ jāmitvaṃ ca | evaṃbhūtāḥ ^mārjayanti śodhayanti | tadevā'ha | ^sanābhayaḥ samānabandhanāḥ ^vājinaṃ balavantaṃ somam ^ūrjayanti balinaṃ kurvanti ||
cata̭sra īṃ ghṛ॒tadṷhaḥ sa॒cante॒ samā̭ne a॒ntardhru॒ṇe ni॒rdhattā̭ḥ | tā ī̭ma॒rdhanti॒ nama̭sā pu॒nānā॒stā ī̭ vi॒śvata॒ḥ pari̭ ṣanti pu॒rvīḥ || 5 || cata̭sraḥ | īm | ghṛ॒ta'dṷhaḥ | sa॒cantḙ | samā̭ne | a॒ntaḥ | dhru॒ṇe | ni॒'dhattā̭ḥ | tāḥ | īm | a॒rdhanti̭ | nama̭sā | pu॒nānā̭ḥ | tāḥ | īm | vi॒śvata̭ḥ | pa॒ri | ṣa॒nti | pu॒rvīḥ ||5|| ^catasraḥ ^ghṛtaduhaḥ ghṛtadohyo gāvaḥ ^īm enaṃ somaṃ ^sacante sevante | kīdṛyastāḥ | ^samāne ekasmin ^dhruṇe sarveṣāṃ dhārake'ntarikṣe ^nidhattāḥ nidhattāḥ | ^tāḥ ghṛtaduhaḥ ^īm enam ^ardhanti prāpnuvanti ^namasā annena ^punānāḥ pūyamānāḥ satyaḥ | ^tāḥ ^pūrvīḥ bahvyaḥ prabhūtā gāvaḥ ^ ^viśvataḥ sarvataḥ ^pari ^ṣanti paribhavanti ||

1 ||

^tri'pṛṣṭham |
trīṇi pṛṣṭhāni stotrāṇi droṇakalaśādisthānāni vā savanāni vā yasya sa tathoktaḥ |
taṃ vṛṣaṇaṃ varṣakaṃ vayodhāma annasya dātāraṃ somam āhūpāṇām āghoṣavatāṃ stotrāṇāṃ ^vāṇīḥ vācaḥ |
abhi avāvaśanta śabdayante |
vanā vanānyudakāni vasānaḥ ācchādayan varuṇo ^na varuṇa iva ^sindhūn ācchādayati tadvat |
^ratnadhāḥ ratnānāṃ dātā somaḥ |
^vāryāṇi dhanāni ^dayate prayacchati stotṛbhyaḥ ||
śūragrāmaḥ sarvavīraḥ sahāvāñjetā pavasva sarnitā dhanāni |
tigmāyudhaḥ kṣipradhanvā sumatsvapāḻhaḥ sāhrānpṛtanāsu śatrūn ||
3 ||
śūra'grāmaḥ | sarva'vīraḥ | sahāvān | jetā | pavasva | sarnitā | dhanāni | tigma'āyudhaḥ | kṣipra'dhanvā | sumat'su | apāḻhaḥ | sahrān | pṛtanāsu | śatrūn || 3 ||
he soma ^pavasva tvam | krīḍasvatvam | ^śūragrāmaḥ śūraṇāṃ grāmaḥ saṃgho yasya saḥ | ^sarvavīraḥ | sarve vīrā yasya sa tathoktaḥ | ^mahāvān sahanavān ^jetā jayaśīlaḥ | ^sarnitā saṃbhaktā ^dhanāni ^tigmāyudhaḥ tīkṣṇapraharaṇasādhanaḥ ^kṣipradhanvā kṣiprasahanaśīladhanvā ^sumatsu saṃgrāmeṣu ^apāḻhaḥ asoḍhaḥ ^sāhrān abhibhavan | kutra | ^pṛtanāsu śatrusenāsu | kān | ^śatrūn ||
urugavyūtirbharayāni kṛṇvantsarmīcīne ā pavasvā puraṃdhī | apaḥ | sipāsanupasaḥ | svaḥ'rgāḥ | saṃ citrado maho asmabhyaṃ vājān || 4 ||
uru'gavyūtiḥ | abhayāni | kṛṇvan | samīcīne iti sum'īcīne | ā | pavasva | puraṃdhī iti puram'dhī | apaḥ | sipāsan | upamaḥ | svaḥ | gāḥ | sam | citradaḥ | mahaḥ | asmabhyam | vājān ||4||

idaṃ dyāvāpṛthivyornāma |
taiḥ ^sasīcīne saṃgate kurvan ^ā ^pavasva aśvara |
^apaḥ ^upasaḥ ^svaḥ ādityaṃ ^gā raśmīṃśca ^sipāsan puṣṭyarthaṃ saṃbhaktumicchan ^saṃ ^citradaḥ saṃkrandase |
^mahaḥ mahato mahānta ^vājān annāni ^asmabhyaṃ dātumiti śeṣaḥ ||

^kavīyan kavirivācaran | yadvā | kavayaḥ stotāraḥ | tānicchan | tatra dṛṣṭāntaḥ | ^vrajaṃ ^na ^mamva mananīyaṃ' gavāṃ goṣṭhaṃ ^paśuvardhanārtha paśūnāṃ vardhanāya gopālaḥ parigacchati | tathā devāmāṃ prīṇamāya pātrāṇi prati pavata iti ||
dvitā vyūrṇvanmṛtasya dharma svarvvide bhuvanāni prathanta |
dhiyaḥ pinvānāḥ svasare na gāvaṃ ṛtāyantīrābhi vāvaśra indum ||
2 ||
dvitā | vi'ūrṇvan | amṛtasya | dharma | svaḥ'vide | bhuvanāni | prathanta | dhiyaḥ | pinvānāḥ | svasare | na | gāvaṃḥ | ṛta'yantīḥ | abhi | vāvaśra | indum || 2 ||
^amṛtasya udakasya ^dhāma dhārakaṃ sthānamantarikṣaṃ somaḥ ^dvitā dvidhā ^vyūrṇvan ubhayataḥ svatejasācchādayan madhyena gacchati |

tasya raśmīnāṃ saṃcaraṇārthaṃ prathanta ityarthaḥ |
atha ^pinvānāḥ prīṇayiṣyaḥ ^dhiyaḥ stutilakṣaṇā vācaḥ ^ṛtāyantīḥ yajñamicchantyaḥ mathyastamimam ^induṃ somamabhilakṣya svasare yāgāhana ^vāvaśra śabdāyante |
yadvā |
somaṃ kāmayante |
tatra dṛṣṭāntaḥ |
^gāvo ^na yathā pinmānāḥ payaḥ kṣaranyo gātraḥ ^svasare |
suṣṭhu asyante preryante gāvo'treti svasaro goṣṭham |
tasmin‌cchāmilakṣya śabdaṃ kurvanti tadvat ||
pari yatkaviḥ kāvyā bharate śūro na ratho bhuvanāni viśvā |
deveṣu yaśo martīya bhūndakṣāya rāyaḥ purubhūṣu navyaḥ ||
3 ||
pari | yat | kaviḥ | kāvyā | bharate | śūraḥ | na | rathaḥ | bhuvanāni | viśvā | deveṣu | yaśaḥ | martīya | bhūn | dakṣāya | rāyaḥ | puru'bhūṣu | navyaḥ || 3 ||
^kaviḥ krāntaprajñaḥ somaḥ ^kāvyā kāvyāni kaveḥ ' karmāṇi stotrāṇi ^yat yadvā ^pari ^bharate parito bibharti |
parigacchatīti yāvat |
kathamiva |
^śūro ^na yathā śūraḥ śatrūṇāṃ bādhakaḥ ^rathaḥ |

stotavyo bhavati |

1 ||
kanikranti | hariḥ | ā | mujyamānaḥ | sīdan | vanasya | jaṭhare | punānaḥ | nṛbhiḥ | yataḥ | kṛṇute | nirṇijam | gāḥ | ataḥ | matīḥ | janayataḥ | svadhābhiḥ || 1 ||
vmujyamānaḥ vā samantānmujyamāno'bhibhūyamānaḥ vhariḥ haritavarṇaḥ somaḥ vkanikranti punaḥpunaḥ śabdāyate|
krandateryaṅluki rmiḍāve 'dardhati dardhati' ityādinā nipātanādabhyā- sasya nigāmaḥ|
vabhyastasthiraḥ|

kiṃca vnṛbhiḥ karmanetṛbhirdhvigbhiḥ vyataḥ saṃyataḥ somaḥ vgāḥ govikārān kṣīrādīnyācchādayan vnirṇijam ātmano rūpaṃ vkṛṇute grahādiṣu karoti|

ṛ. 4-12
hāreṃḥ | sṛjānaḥ | pṛthyām | ṛtasya̭ | irya̭ti | vāca̭m | aritā'i̭va | nāva̭ṃm | devaḥ | devānā̭m | guhyā̭ni | nāma̭ | ā॒viḥ | kṛṇoti | barhiṣi | pra'vācai̭ || 2 ||
ᵛsṛjānaḥ sṛjyamānaḥ ᵛhariḥ haritavarṇaḥ somaḥ ᵛṛtasya satyabhūtasya ᵛpadhyāṃ pathi bhavāṃ ᵛvācaṃ daivīm ᵛiryati prerayati |
tatra dṛṣṭāntaḥ |
ᵛaritava janāstīraṃ prāpayannāvikaḥ ᵛnāvaṃ yathā prerayati tadvat |
‘ṛ gatau’ |
jauhotyādikaḥ |
‘artipiparyoḥ’ ityabhyāsasyetvam |
tataḥ ᵛdevaḥ dīpyamānaḥ somaḥ ᵛdevānām indrādīnāṃ ᵛguhyāni antarhitāni ᵛnāma nāmāni śarīrāṇi ᵛpravāce prakarṣeṇa vācayitre stotre ᵛbarhiṣi yajñe ᵛāviṣkṛṇoti stotumāviṣkaroti ||
apāmivedūrmayastatṝrāṇāḥ pra ma̭nīṣā ī̭rate soma̭maccha | nama̭syantīrupa̭ ca yanti saṃ cā ca̭ viśa̭ntyuśa̭tīruśa̭ntam || 3 || apāma'i̭va | it | ūrma̭yaḥ | tatṝrāṇāḥ | pra | manī̭ṣāḥ | ī̭rate | soma̭m | accha̭ | nama̭syantīḥ | upa̭ | ca | yanti | sam | ca | ā | ca̭ | viśa̭nti | uśa̭tīḥ | uśa̭ntam ||3||
ᵛapāmiva yathodakānām ᵛūrmayaḥ tvarante |
ᵛit iti pūraṇaḥ |
tadvat ᵛtatṝrāṇāḥ karmaṇi devān stotuṃ tvaramāṇāḥ |
‘tura tvaraṇe’ |
jauhotyādikaḥ |
yaṅlugantasya śānaci rūpam |
abhyāsasyā- varṇaśca rephādeśaśchāndasaḥ |
abhyastasvaraḥ |
tādṛśā ṛtvijaḥ ᵛmanīṣāḥ manasa īṃśitrīḥ stutīḥ ᵛsomamaccha somaṃ prati ᵛpra ᵛīrate prerayanti |
ᵛnamasyantīḥ namasyantyaḥ somaṃ pūjayantyaḥ satyaḥ tam ᵛupa ᵛyanti ca upagacchanti tameva ᵛsaṃ yanti ca |
‘cavāyoge prathamā’ iti na nighātaḥ |
tataḥ ᵛuśatīḥ kāmayamānāḥ stutayaḥ ᵛuśantaṃ kāmayamānaṃ somam ᵛā ᵛviśanti ᵛca ||
taṃ ma̭rmṛjānaṃ ma̭hiṣaṃ na sānaṷhaṃśuṃ dṷhantyukṣa̭ṇaṃ gi̭ri'sthām | taṃ vā̭vaśānaṃ ma॒taya̭ḥ sa॒cante॒ trito̭ bibharti varṷṇaṃ sa॒mudrḙ || 4 || tam | ma̭rmṛjānam | ma̭hiṣam | na | sānaṷ | a̭śum | dṷhanti | ukṣa̭ṇam | gi̭ri'sthām | tam | vā̭vaśānam | ma॒taya̭ḥ | sa॒cante॒ | trita̭ḥ | bibharti̭ | varṷṇam | sa॒mudrḙ || 4 ||
ᵛmarmṛjānaṃ yatnaiḥ paricaryyamāṇaṃ ᵛmahiṣaṃ ᵛna mahiṣākhyaṃ mṛgamiva ᵛsānau samucchrite deśe vartamānam ᵛukṣaṇaṃ kāmānāṃ settāraṃ ᵛgiristhām abhiṣavārthaṃ grāvasu niṣṭhitaṃ ᵛtaṃ ᵛtādṛśaṃ prasiddham ᵛaṃśuṃ somaṃ ᵛduhanti ṛtvijo duhanti |
‘grāvāṇo vatsā ṛtvijo duhanti’ (tai. saṃ. 6.2.11.4) iti taittirīyabrāhmaṇam |
ᵛtaṃ tādṛśaṃ ᵛvāvaśānaṃ kāmayamānaṃ somaṃ ᵛmatayaḥ mantavyāḥ stutayaḥ ᵛsacante samavayanti |
sevanta iti yāvat |
tataḥ ᵛtritaḥ triṣu sthāneṣu vartamāna indraḥ ᵛvaruṇaṃ śatrūṇāṃ nivārakamenaṃ somaṃ ᵛsamudre antarikṣe ᵛbibharti śatruvadhārthaṃ dhārayati |
yadvā |
tritastriṣu sthāneṣu droṇāvanīyapūtabhṛdākhyeya kalaśeṣu tato6 vitataḥ6 somaḥ śatrūṇāṃ nivārakamindraṃ dyuloke bibharti poṣayati ||

iṣyanvācamupavakteva hotṛḥ punāna indo vi pyā manīṣām | indraśca yatkṣayathaḥ saubhagāya suvīryasya patayaḥ syāma || 5 || iṣyan | vācam | upavaktā'iva | hotṛḥ | punānaḥ | indra iti | vi | sya | manīṣām | indraḥ | ca | yat | kṣayathaḥ | saubhagāya | suvīryasya | patayaḥ | syāma || 5 ||
he ॰indo soma ॰vācaṃ stutim ॰iṣyan prerayan ॰hotuḥ ॰upavakteva yathādhvaryuḥ pratigaraṃ kurvan protsāhayati tadvat stotṛṇāṃ śaṃsanāya protsāhaṃ kurvan ॰punānaḥ pūyamānastvaṃ ॰manīṣāṃ buddhiṃ ॰vi ॰pya vimuñca |
kiṃtu buddhiṃ dhanapradānābhimukhāṃ kuru |
kiṃca ॰yat yadā ॰indraśca tvaṃ ca saha yajñe ॰kṣayathaḥ nivasathaḥ tadā stotāro vayaṃ ॰saubhagāya saubhāgyāya syāma |
kiṃca ॰suvīryasya śobhanavīryopetasya dhanasya
^tiṣṭhati āmīdati|
anantaram ^indrasya ^sakhā sakhibhūtaḥ ^vidvān prājñaḥ somaḥ ^eṇā etena rathena pavitraṇa ^sumaṃtiṃ śobhanastutikaṃ stotāram ^accha ^yāti abhigacchati|
tasmin^ kāle tena kriyamāṇāṃ stutimabhigacchatītyarthaḥ ||

saḥ | naḥ | deva | devatātai | pavasva | mahe | soma | pyarase | indrapānaḥ | kṛṇvan | apaḥ | varṣayan | dyām | uta | imām | uroḥ | ā | naḥ | varivasya | punānaḥ ||3||

somo hi devānāpyāyayatīti ||

devasuvāṃ haviḥpu somasya vanaspateḥ ‘brahmā devānām’ iti yājyā |
sūyate ca- ‘tvaṃ ca soma no vaśo brahmā devānāṃ padavīḥ kavīnām’ (āśva. śrau. 4. 11) iti ||
brahmā devānāṃ padavīḥ kavīnāmṛṣirviprāṇāṃ mahiṣo mṛgāṇām | śyeno gṛdhrāṇāṃ svadhitirvanānāṃ somaḥ pavitramatyeti rebhan || 6 || brahmā | devānām | padavīḥ | kavīnām | ṛṣiḥ | viprāṇām | mahiṣaḥ | mṛgāṇām | śyenaḥ | gṛdhrāṇām | svadhitiḥ | vanānām | somaḥ | pavitram | ati | eti | rebhan ||6||
soma evaṃrūpo bhavati |

yadvā |

tathā ᵥkavīnāṃ krāntaprajñānāṃ ᵥpadavīḥ |

ᵥyā gatyādiṣviṣyetasmāt kripa rūpam |

ᵥvanānām |
vanatirhiṃsākarmā |
hiṃsakānāṃ chedakānāṃ madhye ᵥsvadhitiḥ etannāmakaścedako'si |
ᵥevaṃprabhāvaḥ ᵥsomaḥ ᵥrebhan śabdayamānaḥ san ᵥpavitram ūrṇāstukrena kṛtam ᵥatyeti atigacchati ||
prāvīvipadvā ūrmiṃ na sindhurgiraḥ somaḥ parvamāno manīṣāḥ | antaḥ paśyanvrajanemārvarāṇyā tiṣṭhati vṛṣabho goṣu jānan || 7 || pra | avīvipat | vācaḥ | ūrmim | na | sindhuḥ | giraḥ | somaḥ | parvamānaḥ | manīṣāḥ | antarhita | paśyan | vrajanā | imā | arvaraṇi | ā | tiṣṭhati | vṛṣabhaḥ | goṣu | jānan ||7||
ᵥpavamānaḥ ᵥsomaḥ ᵥmanīṣāḥ manasa īśitā ᵥhṛdayaṃgamāḥ ᵥgiraḥ stutāḥ ᵥprāvīvipat prakarṣeṇa vapayati prerayati |
kathamiva |
ᵥsindhuḥ syandamānā nadīva ᵥvācaḥ śabdasya ᵥūrmiṃ ᵥna saṃdhaṃ yathā prerayati tadvat |
kiṃca ᵥvṛṣabhaḥ kāmānāmudakānāṃ vā varṣakaḥ somaḥ ᵥantaḥ antarhitaṃ vastujātaṃ ᵥpaśyan ᵥavaraṇi durbalairvārayitumaśakyāni ᵥimā ᵥvrajanā imāni balāni ᵥā ᵥtiṣṭhati āsī- dati |
kiṃ kurvan |

sa matsuraḥ pṛtsu vṛnvanarvātaḥ sahasraretā ābhi vājarmarṣa | indrāyendo parvamāno manīṣyaṃ śorurmimīrya gā iṣuṇyan || 8 || saḥ | matsaraḥ | pṛtsu | vṛnvan | arvātaḥ | sahasraretāḥ | abhi | vājam | arṣa | indrāya | indo iti | parvamānaḥ | manīṣī | aṃśoḥ | ūrmim | īraya | gāḥ | iṣuṇyan ||8||

ṛgveda:
vmatsara: madakara: vpṛtsu saṃgrāmeṣu vvanvan śatrūn hiṃsan ata eva vavāta: anyairgantumaśakya: vsahasretā: sahasrodhadhāropeta: vsa: soma: vvājaṃ śatrūṇāṃ balam vabhi vartha abhigaccha|

2. ta-asya |
devāvyo naḥ pariṣicyamānāḥ kṣayaṃ suvīraṃ dhanvantu somāḥ | āyujyavaḥ sumatiṃ viśvavārā hotāro na divijo mandratamāḥ || 26 || deva'avyaḥ | naḥ | pari'sicyamānāḥ | kṣayam | su'vīram | dhanvantu | somāḥ | ā'yujyavaḥ | su'matim | viśva'vārāḥ | hotāraḥ | na | divi'jaḥ | mandratamāḥ ||26||
^devāvyaḥ |
avatestarpaṇārthasya 'avitṛstṛnantribhya īḥ' itipratyayaḥ |
'udāttasvaritayoryaṇaḥ' iti jasaḥ svaritatvam |
devānāṃ tarpayitāraḥ ^pariṣicyamānāḥ paritaḥ pātreṣu sicyamānāḥ ^somāḥ ^naḥ asmākaṃ ^suvīraṃ śobhanaputraṃ ^kṣayaṃ gṛhaṃ ^dhanvantu preyantu |
tathā ^āyujyavaḥ ābhimukhyena yaṣṭavyā ābhimukhyena prayacchanto vā ata eva ^viśvavārāḥ sarvairvarṇanīyāḥ ^sumatim anugrahabuddhiṃ ca preyantu |
kīdṛśāḥ |
^holāro ^na hotāro yathā devānindrādīn stuvanti evaṃ ^divijaḥ divi dyuloke sthitānnindrādīn devān yajantaḥ |

^mandratamāḥ atyantaṃ madakarāḥ ||
evā deva devatātai pavasva mahe soma prasarse devapānaḥ | mahaściddhi ṣmasi hitāḥ samarya kṛdhi susthāne rodasī punānaḥ || 27 || eva | deva | deva'tāte | pavasva | mahe | soma | prasarse | deva'pānaḥ | mahaḥ | cit | hi | smasi | hitāḥ | sa'marya | kṛdhi | su'sthāne | rodasī | punānaḥ || 27 ||

tato vayaṃ ^hitāḥ tvayā preritāḥ santaḥ ^samarya maraṇadharmasahite saṃgrāme ^mahaścit mahato balādhikānapi śatrūn ^smasi hi abhibhavema khalu |

1. na-bha6-mu-dyulokaḥ |

di॒vo na sa॒rgā asṷgramhā॒ rājā̭ na mi॒traṃ pra mi̭nāti dhīra̭ḥ | pi॒turna pu॒traḥ ka॒rtubhi̭rya॒tāna ā pa̭vasva vi॒śe a॒syā a॒jīti̭m || 30 || di॒vaḥ | na | sa॒rgāḥ | asṷgram | a॒hām | rājā̭ | na | mi॒tram | pra | mi॒nāti̭ | dhīra̭ḥ | pi॒tuḥ | na | pu॒traḥ | ka॒rtu'bhi̭ḥ | ya॒tān | ā | pa॒vasva̭ | vi॒śe | a॒syai | a॒jīti̭m ||30|| ᵥdivo ᵥna yathā divo dyotamānasyādityasya ᵥahnāṃ saṃbandhanaḥ ᵥsargāḥ raśmayaḥ ᵥasugraṃ visṛjyante tadvat somasya sargāḥ | sṛjyanta iti sargā dhārāḥ | visṛjyante | sṛjerbhāve lyuṭ laṅi 'bahulaṃ chandasi' iti śasya bhluḥ | ᵥśeramādeśaśchāndasaḥ | ᵥdhīraḥ prājñaḥ ᵥrājā ayaṃ somaḥ ᵥmitraṃ sakhāyaṃ ᵥna ᵥpra ᵥmināti na hinasti | ᵥkratubhiḥ karmabhiḥ ᵥyatāna yatamānaḥ ᵥputraḥ ᵥpiturna pituryathāparāvabhavaṃ kārati tadvat karmabhiryatamānasravam ᵥasyai ᵥviśe prajāyai ᵥajītim aparābhavam ᵥā ᵥpavasva āprāpaya | yathā na parājitā bhavanti tathā kurvityarthaḥ || || 16 ||

‘ adhvare ‘ ūrdhvaḥ samucchritaḥ sarveṣāṃ mukhyaḥ ‘ yaḥ somaḥ ‘ gavyayuḥ gokāmaḥ yadvā kṣīrādikaṃ kāmayamānaḥ san ‘ dhārā dhārayā saha ‘ eti gacchati |
‘ājā ’ na ājamānayā dīptyā yathāntarikṣe gacchati tadvat ||
sa hi tvaṃ deva śaśvate vasuṃ martīya dāśuṣe | indau sahasriṇaṃ rayiṃ śatātmānaṃ vivāsāsi || 4 || saḥ | hi | tvam | deva | śaśvate | vasu | martīya | dāśuṣe | indo iti | sahasriṇam | rayim | śata'ātmānam | vivāsāsi || 4 ||
he ‘deva soma ‘saḥ ‘tvaṃ ‘śaśvate putrādimattvena bahave ‘martyāya manuṣyāya ‘dāśuṣe havirdattavate yajamānāya ‘vasu dhanaṃ mahaṃ ca ‘vivāsasi prerayasi |
ekavākyatāpakṣe hi tiṣṭhantasya hiyogādanighātaḥ syāt ||
vyaṃ tai asya vṛtrahanvaso vasvaḥ puruspṛhaḥ |
ni nediṣṭhatamā iṣaḥ syāma sumnasyādhigo ||

tato he 'vaso vāsayitaḥ 'puruspṛhaḥ bahubhiḥ spṛhaṇīyasya 'vasvaḥ basunaḥ dhanasya tvayā dīyamānasya vayaṃ 'ni nitarāṃ 'nediṣṭhatamāḥ atyantamantikatamāḥ syāma |

diyaṃ pañca svaryaśasaṃ svasāro adrisaṃhatam | priyāmindrasya kāmyaṃ prasnāpayantyurmiṇam || 6 || dviḥ | yam | pañca | svaryaśasam | svasāraḥ | adri'saṃhatam | priyam | indrasya | kāmyam | pra'snāpayanti | ūrmiṇam || 6 ||
'dviḥ 'pañca daśasaṃkhyākāḥ 'svasāraḥ karmakaraṇārthamitastato gacchantyo'ṅgulayaḥ 'svayaśasaṃ svabhūtayaśaskam 'adrisaṃhataṃ grāvabhirabhiputam 'indrasya 'priyaṃ 'kāmyaṃ sarvaiḥ kāmyamānam 'ūrmiṇaṃ dhārābhistadantaṃ 'yaṃ taṃ somaṃ 'prasnāpayanti vasatīvarībhiḥ prakarṣeṇa secayanti yajamānāḥ taṃ punantītyuttaratra saṃbandhaḥ ||

pari tyaṃ haryataṃ hariṃ bradhaṃ punanti vāreṇa | yo devānviśvā~ itapārī madaina saha gacchati || 7 || pari | tyam | haryatam | hāram | bradham | punanti | vāreṇa | yaḥ | devān | viśvān | ita | pārī | madaina | saha | gacchati || 7 ||
'haryataṃ sarvaiḥ spṛhaṇīyaṃ 'hariṃ haritavarṇaṃ 'bradhaṃ babhruvarṇaṃ ca 'yaṃ taṃ somaṃ 'vāreṇa vālena pavitreṇa 'pari 'punanti pariśodhayanti |

1. ga-ṛtavyā |
he ᵥpavamāna soma ᵥvidharmaṇi vividhaṃ haviṣāṃ dhārake yajñe ᵥadruhaḥ drohavarjitāḥ ᵥmātaraḥ mātṛbhūtā vasatīvaryaḥ ᵥhariṃ haritavarṇaṃ ᵥtvāṃ ᵥpavitre sthitaṃ ᵥrihanti lihanti āsvādayanti | kathamiva | yathā ᵥdhenavaḥ ᵥjātaṃ ᵥvatsaṃ lihanti tathā || pavamāna mahi śravaścitrairbhāyasi raśmibhiḥ | śardhan tamāṃsi jighnase viśvāni dāśuṣo gṛhe || 8 || pavamāna | mahi | śravaḥ | citraiḥ | yāsi | raśmibhiḥ | śardhan | tamāṃsi | jighnase | viśvāni | dāśuṣaḥ | gṛhe || 8 || he ᵥpavamāna ᵥmahi mahat ᵥśravaḥ śravaṇīyamantarikṣaṃ ᵥcitraiḥ citrairnānāvidhaiścāyanīyairvā ᵥraśmibhiḥ pari ᵥyāsi parigacchasi | tathā ᵥśardhan vegaṃ kurvastvaṃ ᵥdāśuṣaḥ havirdattavato yajamānasya ᵥgṛhe sthitvā ᵥviśvāni sarvāṇi ᵥtamāṃsi tamarūpāṇi rakṣāṃsi ᵥjighnase haṃsi | evaṃ cāvāpṛthivyoḥ vartasa ityarthaḥ || tvaṃ dyāṃ ca mahivrata pṛthivīṃ cāti jabhriṣe ! prati drāpiṃmumugdhyāḥ pavamāna mahitvanā || 9 || tvam | dyām | ca | mahi'vrata | pṛthivīm | ca | ati | jabhriṣe | prati | drāpim | amugdhyāḥ | pavamāna | mahi'tvanā || 9 || he ᵥmahivrata mahākarman bahuvidhakarman soma ᵥtvaṃ ᵥdyāṃ dyulokaṃ ᵥca ᵥpṛthivīṃ ᵥca ᵥati ᵥjabhriṣe atyantaṃ bibharṣi | ‘ḍubhṛñ dhāraṇapoṣaṇayoḥ |’ | tasya chāndase liṭi sarvavidhīnāṃ chandasi vikalpita- tvādatredāgamaḥ | antarikṣe somātmaneti pṛthivyāṃ latārūpetyevaṃ lokadvayavartitvam | he ᵥpavamāna kṣaran soma tvaṃ ᵥmahitvanā mahatvena yuktaḥ san ᵥdrāpiṃ kavacaṃ ᵥprati ᵥamugdhyāḥ pratimuñcasi saṃvṛṇoṣi || || 28 || vedārthasya prakāśena tamo hārdaṃ nivārayan | pumarthāṃścaturo deyādvidyātīrthamaheśvaraḥ || iti śrīmadrājādhirājaparameśvaravaidikamārgapravartakaśrīvīrabukkabhūpāla- sāmrājyadhuraṃdhareṇa sāyaṇācāryeṇa viracite mādhavīye vedārthaprakāśa ṛksaṃhitābhāṣye saptamāṣṭake caturtho'dhyāyaḥ samāptaḥ ||
1. ta1.2.5.7-bha6-daśāpavitre
śrīgaṇeśāya namaḥ ||
yasya niḥśvasitaṃ vedā yo vedebhyo'khilaṃ jagat|
nirmame tamahaṃ vande vidyātīrthamaheśvaram||
vijñātavedagāmbhīryastatha vyākhyāya sasame|
sāyaṇāryastato'dhyāyaṃ pañcamaṃ vyācikīrṣati||
tatra ‘purojitī vaḥ’ iti ṣoḍaśarcamanuvākāpekṣayā pañcamaṃ sūktam|
ādyasya tṛcasya iyāvāśva- putro'ndhīgurnāmarṣiḥ|
dvitīyasya nahupasya rājñaḥ putro yathātirnāma|
tṛtīyasya manoḥ putro nahupo nāma rājarṣiḥ|
caturthasya saṃvaraṇasya rājñaḥ putro manuḥ|
evaṃ dvādaśa gatāḥ|
śiṣṭasya caturtharcasya vācaḥ putro vaiśvāmitro vā prajāpatirṛṣiḥ|
dvitīyātṛtīye gāyatryau śiṣṭāścaturdaśānuṣṭubhaḥ|
tathā cānukramyate- ‘purojitī ṣoḍaśa iyāvāśvirandhīguryathātirnahupo’ mānavo manuḥ sāṃvaraṇa iti tṛcāḥ|
śeṣe prajāpatirupādye gāyatryau’ iti|
gataḥ sūktaviniyogaḥ||
purojitī vo andhasaḥ sutāya mādayitvanai|
apa śvānaṃ śrathiṣṭana sakhāyo dīrghajihvyam ||
1 ||
puraḥ| jitī| vaḥ| andhasaḥ| sutāya| mādayitvanai| apa| śvānam| śrathiṣṭana| sakhāyaḥ| dīrgha| jihvyam|| 1||
he sakhāyaḥ sakhibhūtāḥ samānasthānā vā he stotāraḥ vaḥ yūyaṃ purojitī|
paṣṭhyāḥ pūrva- savarṇadīrghaḥ|
puraḥsthitajayasya andhasaḥ adanīyasya somasya svabhūtaya sutāya abhiputāya mādayitvanai atyantaṃ madakarāya rasāya dīrghajihvyam|
dīrghajihvā yasya saḥ|
‘dīrghajihvī ca chandasi’ (pā. sū. 4. 1. 59) iti ṅīpantatvena nipātitaḥ|
tādṛśaṃ śvānam apa śrathiṣṭana apakṣayayata apabādhadhvam|
yathā śvā rākṣasā vā sutaṃ somaṃ na lihanti tathā kurutetyarthaḥ||
pavitreṣṭāṃ dvitīyasyājyabhāgasya ‘yo dhārayā’ ityetānuvākyā|
sūtritaṃ ca—‘pāvakavantā- vājyabhāgavamrī rakṣāṃsi sedhati yo dhārayā pāvakayā’ (āśva. śrau. 2. 12) iti||
yo dhārayā pāvakayā pariprasyandate sutaḥ|
induraśvo na kṛtvyaḥ ||
2 ||
yaḥ| dhārayā| pāvakayā| pari| prasyandate| sutaḥ| induḥ| na| kṛtvyaḥ|| 2||
sutaḥ abhiputaḥ| kṛtdhyaḥ| kṛtvīti karmanāma| karmaṇi sādhuḥ yaḥ induḥ somaḥ pāvakayā pāpānāṃ śodhayitvā dhārayā pariprasyandate paritaḥ prakṣarati| kathamiva| aśvo na aśvo yathā vegena pracchati tadvat||
taṃ duroṣamabhī naraḥ somaṃ viśvācyā dhiyā|
yajñaṃ hinvantyadribhiḥ ||
3 ||
tam| duroṣam| abhi| naraḥ| somam| viśvācyā| dhiyā| yajñam| hinvanti| adribhiḥ||3||
naraḥ karmanetāra ṛtvijaḥ duroṣam|
roṣaterhiṃsārthasya rephalope dīrghabhāva oṣaterdāhārthasya vā khali rūpamiti sandehādanavagrahaḥ|
taṃ durvahaṃ durvadhaṃ vā yajñaṃ yajñyaṃ taṃ somaṃ viśvācyā

tatra dṛṣṭāntadvayam |

‘ krāṇā śiśuḥ’ ityetat ṣaṣṭhaṃ sūktādhyāsya tritasyārpam |

krāṇā śiśurmahīnāṃ hinvanṛtasya dīdhitim | viśvā pari priyā bhuvadartha dvitā || 1 || krāṇā | śiśuḥ | mahīnām | hinvan | ṛtasya | dīdhitim | viśvā | pari | priyā | bhuvat | artha | dvitā || 1 ||
krāṇā |
karoteḥ śānaci ‘bahulaṃ chandasi’ iti vikaraṇasya luk |
‘supāṃ suluk’ iti supa ākārādeśaḥ |
yajñaṃ kurvāṇaḥ mahīnāṃ mahatīnāṃ mahanīyānāṃ vā apāṃ śiśuḥ putrasthānīyaḥ somaḥ ṛtasya yajñasya dīdhitiṃ prakāśakaṃ dhārakaṃ vā svīyaṃ rasaṃ hinvan prerayan viśvā sarvāṇi priyā priyāṇi havīṃṣi pari bhuvat paribhavati vyāpnoti |
adha api ca dvitā dvidhā bhavati |
divi ca pṛthivyāṃ ca vartata ityarthaḥ ||
upa tritasya pāṣyo'raṃkta yadguhā padam | yajñasya suta dharmabhirartha priyam || 2 || upa | tritasya | pāṣyoḥ | aṃkta | yat | guhā | padam | yajñasya | suta | dharmabhiḥ | artha | priyam || 2 ||
vtritasya mama yajñe vguhā guhāyāṃ havirdhāne vartamānayoḥ vpāpyoḥ pāṣāṇavad‌dṛḍhayoradhiṣavaṇa- phalakayoḥ vpadaṃ sthānaṃ somaḥ vyat yadā vupa vabhanka samabhajata vadha anantaraṃ vyajñasya vdhāmabhiḥ dhārakaḥ vsasa saptabhiścandobhirgāṃyādibhiḥ vpriyaṃ prīṇayitāraṃ somamabhiṣṭuvanti ṛtvijaḥ|

kṣipraṃ pajātāsaḥ jātāḥ paindavaḥ pātreṣu kṣarantaḥ pasvarvadaḥ sarvajñāḥ paharayaḥ haritavarṇāḥ pasutāḥ abhiṣutāḥ paime somāḥ pavṛṣaṇaṃ kāmānāṃ settāram paindramaccha payantu abhigacchantu ||

ayaṃ bharāya sānusirindrāya pavate sutaḥ| somo jaitrasya cetati yathā vide ||2|| ayam | bharāya | sānusiḥ | indrāya | pavate | sutaḥ | somaḥ | jaitrasya | cetati | yathā | vide ||2|| vbharāya saṃgrāmāya tadarthaṃ vsānasiḥ bhajanīyaḥ vsutaḥ abhiṣutaḥ vayaṃ vsomaḥ vindrāya indrārthaṃ vpavate grahādiṣu kṣarati | tataḥ vsomaḥ vjaitrasya || ‘kriyāgrahaṇaṃ kartavyam’ iti karmaṇaḥ saṃpradānasaṃjñā | ‘caturthyarthe bahulam’ iti ṣaṣṭhī || jayanīlamindraṃ vcetati jānāti | vyathā indraḥ vvide lokairjñāyate tathā jānāti ||

āsīda ||
tarva drupsā 'udapruta indraṃ madāya vāvṛdhuḥ | tvāṃ 'devāso 'amṛtāya 'kaṃ 'papuḥ || 8 || tarva | drupsāḥ | 'uda'prutaḥ | 'indram | madāya | vavṛdhuḥ | 'tvām | 'devāsaḥ | 'amṛtāya | kam | 'papuḥ || 8 ||

tataḥ 'devāsaḥ devā indrādayaḥ 'kaṃ sukhakaraṃ 'tvām 'amṛtāya amaraṇārthaṃ 'papuḥ pibanti ||
ā naḥ sutāsa indavaḥ punānā dhāvatā rayim | vṛṣṭidyāvo rītayāpaḥ 'svarvidaḥ || 9 || ā | naḥ | sutāsaḥ | 'indavaḥ | 'punānāḥ | 'dhāvata | rayim | vṛṣṭi'dyāvaḥ | 'rīti'āpaḥ | 'svaḥ'vidaḥ || 9 ||
he 'sutāsaḥ abhiṣūyamāṇā he 'indavaḥ dīptāḥ pātreṣu kṣaranto vā he 'rītyāpaḥ yeḥ pṛthivīṃ prati śravaṇaśīlā āpaḥ kṛtāḥ tādṛśā he somāḥ 'punānāḥ pūyamānā yūyaṃ 'naḥ asmabhyaṃ 'rayim 'ā 'dhāvata āgamayata |

'vṛṣṭidyāvaḥ |

‘ parītaḥ ’ iti ṣaḍviṃśatyṛcaṃ caturthaṃ sūktam |

ādyā bṛhatī dvitīyā satobṛhatī |

gataḥ sūktaviniyogaḥ ||
parīto piṃścatā sutaṃ somo ya uttamaṃ haviḥ |
dadhanvā~ yo nayau apsu ntarā supāv somadribhiḥ ||

he ṛtvijaḥ ᵥsutam abhiṣutaṃ somam ᵥitaḥ asmāt karmaṇa ūrdhvamathavāsmātpraveśādūrdhvaṃ ᵥpari ᵥpinvata vasatīvarībhiḥ || 'ito pinvata' ityatra saṃhitāyāṃ 'chandasi' 'iti' rohvam | 'ādeśa- pratyayayoḥ' iti paravam || ᵥyaḥ ᵥsomaḥ devānām ᵥuttamaṃ praśasyaṃ ᵥhaviḥ bhavati ᵥā api ca ᵥnayaḥ manuṣyahitaḥ ᵥyaḥ ca somaḥ ᵥapsu vasatīvarīṣvantarikṣe vā ᵥantaḥ ᵥdadhanvān gacchan bhavati taṃ ᵥsomamadribhiḥ ᵥsurabhitaraḥ atyantaṃ sugandhastvaṃ ᵥnunam idānīṃ ᵥpunānaḥ pūyamānaḥ san ᵥavibhiḥ avicālakṛtaiḥ pavitraistebhyaḥ ᵥpari ᵥsrava paritaḥ kṣara | ᵥsute ᵥcit abhiṣute sati ᵥandhasā saktulakṣaṇenānnena ᵥgobhiḥ govikāraiḥ kṣaraiḥ kṣīrādibhiḥ ᵥśrīṇantaḥ miśrayanto vayam ᵥuttaram unnatataram ᵥapsu vasatīvarīṣu saṃsthitaṃ tvāṃ ᵥmadāmaḥ mandāmahe stumaḥ || pariṃ suvānaścakṣe devaṃmādanaḥ kratuṃrindvicakṣaṇaḥ || 3 || pariṃ | suvānaḥ | cakṣe | devaṃmādanaḥ | kratuḥ | indviḥ | tri'cakṣaṇaḥ || 3 || ᵥsuvānaḥ abhiṣūyamāṇaḥ māṃmaḥ ᵥcakṣe sarveṣāṃ darśanāya ᵥpari sravati | kīdṛśaḥ | ᵥdevamādanaḥ devānāṃ tarpayitā ᵥkratuḥ kartā ᵥinduḥ pātreṣu kṣaraṇāllo dīpto vā ᵥvicakṣaṇaḥ sarvamya vidraṣṭā || puṃānāḥ somaṃ dhārayāpo varsāno arṣāsi | ā raṃtnadhā yoniṃmṛtasya sīdasyutsau deva ▿gomān goyuktaḥ somaḥ ▿anupe nimne deśe kalaśe ▿gobhiḥ govikāraiḥ kṣīrādibhiḥ saha ▿akṣāḥ kṣarati |

ā sauma suvāno adribhistiro vārāṇyavyayā | jano na puri cumboṃrviśaddhariḥ sado vaneṣu dadhiṣe || 10 || ā | soma | suvānaḥ | adribhiḥ | tiraḥ | vārāṇi | avyayā | janaḥ | na | puri | cumboṃḥ | viśat | hariḥ | sadaḥ | vaneṣu | dadhiṣe || 10 ||
he ▿soma ▿adribhiḥ grāvabhiḥ ▿suvānaḥ abhiṣūyamāṇastvam ▿avyayā avimayāni ▿vārāṇi vālāni pavitrāṇi ▿tiraḥ kurvan vyavadhāyakāni kurvāṇaḥ san ▿ā pavasa iti śeṣaḥ |

'naḥ asmākam 'ṛṇayāḥ ṛgānāṃ yāpayitā vināśayitā tvaṃ 'dviṣaḥ śatrūn 'taradhyai tarītuṃ hantum 'īyase parigacchasi' ||
anu hi tvā sutaṃ somaṃ madāmasi mahe samaryarājyai | vājā~ abhi pavamāna pra gāhase || 2 || anu | hi | tvā | sutam | somam | madāmasi | mahe | samarya'rājyai | vājān | abhi | pavamāna | pra | gāhase || 2 || he 'soma 'sutam abhiputaṃ 'tvā tvāṃ vayaṃ 'anu 'madāmasi 'hi anumadāmaḥ | anukrameṇa- bhiṣṭumaḥ khalu | he 'pavamāna soma sa tvaṃ 'mahe mahati 'samaryarājye mahat samanuṣyaṃ tvadīyaṃ rājya- manupālayituṃ 'vājān śatrubalānyabhilakṣya 'pra 'gāhase pragacchasi ||
ajījano hi pavamāna sūryyaṃ vidhāre śakarmanā paryaḥ | gojīryā raṃhamāṇaḥ puraṃdhyā || 3 || ajījanaḥ | hi | pavamāna | sūryyam | vi'dhāri | śakarmanā | paryaḥ | go'jīryā | raṃhamāṇaḥ | puram'dhyā || 3 || he 'pavamāna soma tvaṃ 'payaḥ payasaḥ udakasya 'vidhāre vidhāre'ntarikṣe 'śakamanā samarthena balena 'sūryyam 'ajījano 'hi utpāditavān bhavasi khalu | kīdṛśaḥ | 'gojīryā stotṛbhyo gavāṃ prerakeṇa | stotṛṇāṃ preritapaśukenetyarthaḥ | tādṛśena 'puraṃdhyā bahuvidhaprajñānena yuktaḥ 'raṃhamāṇaḥ vegaṃ kurvāṇastvaṃ sūryyamajījanaḥ ||
ajījano amṛta martyeṣvṛtasya dharmannamṛtasya cāruṇaḥ | sadāso vājamacchā saniṣyadat || 4 || ajījanaḥ | amana | martyeṣu | ā | ṛtasya | dharman | amṛtasya | cāruṇaḥ | sadā | asaḥ | vājam | accha | saniṣyadat || 4 || he 'amṛta maraṇadharmarahita soma tvam 'ṛtasya satyabhūtasya 'cāruṇaḥ kalyāṇasya 'amṛtasya udakasya 'dharman dhārake'ntarikṣe sūryyam 'ajījanaḥ | kimartham | 'martyeṣvā manuṣyeṣvabhimukhībhavanāya | kiṃca 'saniṣyadat saṃbhajan sa tvaṃ 'vājamaccha saṃgrāmamabhilakṣya 'sadā 'asaraḥ sarasi gacchasi ||

‘ayā rucā’ iti tṛcamaṣṭamaṃ sūktaṃ parucchepaputrasyānānatāsyasyāryamatyaṣṭicchandaskaṃ pavamāna- somadevatākam|

ayā| rucā| hariṇyā| punānaḥ| viśvā| dveṣāṃsi| tarati| svayugvabhiḥ| sūro| na| svayugvabhiḥ| dhārā| sutasya| rocate| punānaḥ| aruṣaḥ| harīḥ| viśvā| yat| rūpā| pariyāti| arkabhiḥ| sutasyaibhiḥ| arkabhiḥ|| 1 || 'punānaḥ pūyamānaḥ somaḥ 'hariṇyā haritavarṇayā 'ayā anayā 'rucā rocamānayā dhārayā 'viśvā sarvāṇi 'dveṣāṃsi dveṣāṃsi rakṣāṃsi 'tarati vināśayati| tatra dṛṣṭāntaḥ| 'sūro 'na yathā sūryaḥ 'svayugvabhiḥ svayaṃ yuktai raśmibhistanmāṃsi hinasti tadvat| 'svayugvabhiḥ iti dviruktirādarārthā| yadvā| dhārayā yuktaḥ somaḥ svīyairyuktaistejobhī rakṣāṃsi tarati| tasya 'sutasya̭ abhiputasya somasya 'dhārā2 'rocate dīpyate| 'punānaḥ pūyamānaḥ 'hariḥ haritavarṇaḥ somaḥ| 'arupaḥ ārocamāno bhavati| 'yat3 yaḥ somaḥ 'sasāsyebhiḥ rasaharaṇaśīlasyaiḥ 'arkabhiḥ stutimadbhiḥ 'arkabhiḥ tejobhiḥ 'viśvā viśvāni vyāsāni 'rūpā rūpāṇi nakṣatrāṇi 'pariyāti gacchati vyāpnoti ||

tvam| tyat| paṇīnām| vidaḥ| vasu| sam| mātṛbhiḥ| marjayāṃsi| sve| ā| dame| ṛtasya| dhītibhiḥ| dame| parā'dhṛtaḥ| na| sāma| tat| yatra| raṇanti| dhīrtayaḥ| tridhātubhiḥ| aruṣībhiḥ| vayeḥ| dadhe| rocamānaḥ| vayeḥ| dadhe || 2 || he soma 'tvaṃ 'tyat 'paṇīnāṃ 'vasu paṇibhirapahṛtaṃ gavātmakaṃ dhanaṃ 'vidaḥ alabhathāḥ| 'ā api ca 'ṛtasya yajñasya 'dhītibhiḥ dhātrībhiḥ 'mātṛbhiḥ vasatīvarībhiḥ 'sve ātmīye 'dame gṛhabhūte 'dame yajñe 'saṃ 'marjayasi samyak śuddho bhavasi| 'parāvato 'na dūrasthāddeśādyathā 'sāma sāmadhvaniḥ śrūyate tathā tava 'tat sāmadhvaniḥ sarvaiḥ śrūyate| asau somābhiśavābhiprāyeṇoktaḥ| 'yatra yasmin śabde 'dhītayaḥ karmaṇo dhartāro yajamānāḥ 'raṇanti ramante| 'rocamānaḥ somaḥ 'tridhātubhiḥ trayāṇāṃ lokānāṃ dhārayitrībhirmātṛbhirvasatīvarībhiḥ 'aruṣībhiḥ ārocamānābhirdīptibhiḥ 'vayaḥ annaṃ 'dadhe stotṛbhyaḥ prayacchati| punaḥ 'vayāṃ 'dadhe ityādiśartham ||
1. ta4.5-tayutasya; bha6-tatsutasya |
2. ta5-bha2.6-dhārā parvati |
3. ta1.2.10.7-yatayaḥ; bha6-yataḥ yaḥ |
pūrvāmanu pradiśaṃ yāti cekitatsa raśmibhiryatate darśato ratho daivyo darśato rathaḥ |
agman‌ukthāni pausyendraṃ jaitrāya harṣayan |
vajreśca yadvatho anapacyutā samatsvarnapacyutā ||
3 ||
pūrvām | anu | pra'diśam | yāti | cekitat | sam | raśmibhiḥ | yatate | darśanaḥ | rathaḥ | daivyaḥ | darśanaḥ | rathaḥ | agman | ukthāni | pausyā | indram | jaitrāya | harṣayan | vajraḥ | ca | yat | rathaḥ | anapacyutā | samatsu | anapacyutā || 3 ||
cekitat jānānaḥ somaḥ pūrvā prācīṃ pradiśaṃ prakṛṣṭāṃ diśam anu yāti anugacchati |
kiṃca darśanaḥ sarvairdarśanīyaḥ daivyaḥ deveṣu bhavastava rathaḥ sūryaraśmibhiḥ saṃ yatate saṃgacchate |
punaḥ darśanaḥ rathaḥ ityādarārtham |
tataḥ pausyā puṃsyāvagamanāni ukthāni stotrāṇi agman indraṃ gacchanti |
tathā jaitrāya jayārthaṃ tāni stotrāṇi indraṃ harṣayan harṣayanti |
tathā vajraśca tamindraṃ gacchati |
yat yadā samatsu saṃgrāmeṣu anapacyutā anapacyutau śatrubhirarājitau soma tvaṃ cendraśca yuvāṃ saha abhavatha |
tadā stotrāgamanādīni bhavanti |
punaḥ anapacyutā ityādarārtham ||
24 ||
‘nānām’ iti caturṛcaṃ navamaṃ sūktāṅgirasasya śiśunāmna ārṣam |
pañcapadā paṅktiśchandaḥ |
pavamānaḥ somo devatā |
tathā cānukrāntaṃ—‘nānāṃ catuṣkaṃ śiśuḥ pāṅkaṃ hi’ iti |
gato viniyogaḥ ||
nānāṃ vā u no dhiyo vi vratāni janānām |
takṣo riṣṭaṃ rutaṃ bhiṣagbrahmā sunvantamicchatīndrondo pari srava ||
1 ||
nānām | vai | ūṃ iti | naḥ | dhiyaḥ | vi | vratāni | janānām | takṣā | riṣṭam | rutam | bhiṣak | brahmā | sunvantam | icchati | indrāya | indo iti | pari | srava || 1 ||
pariretadādibhiḥ sūktaiḥ aparisravataḥ somasyājāmitvāya manaso vinodaṃ kurvannāha |
he soma naḥ asmākaṃ dhiyaḥ karmāṇi nānāṃ nānājātīyākāni bahūni bhavanti |
vaiśabdaḥ prasiddhyarthadyotanārthaḥ |
u iti pūraṇaḥ |
tānyeṣāṃ janānāṃ vratāni karmāṇi vividhāni bhavanti |
takṣā svasā riṣṭaṃ dārutakṣaṇam icchati |
tathā bhiṣak vaidyaścikitsakaḥ rutaṃ roga- micchati |
brahmā brāhmaṇaḥ sunvantaṃ somābhiṣavaṃ kurvantaṃ yajamānamicchati |
tāhaṃ tvasparisravaṇa- micchāmi |
tasmāddhe indo soma indrāya indrārthaṃ pari svaya paritaḥ kṣara ||
jaratībhiroṣadhībhiḥ pūrṇabhiḥ śakunānām |
kāmaro aśmabhirdhubhirhiraṇyavantamicchatīndrondo pari srava ||
2 ||

1. ga-ta-bha-yuvāṃ samatsu saṃgrāmeṣu |
2. ga-ta3-paristravataḥ; bhara-āparistravataḥ |
^jaratībhiḥ jīrṇābhiḥ^1 ^oṣadhībhiḥ^ ^iṣavaḥ kriyante|
tathā ^śakunānāṃ ^parṇaibhiḥ iṣūṇāṃ pakṣa- bhūtaiḥ parṇaiśca kriyante|
tathā ^śubhiḥ dīptābhirpūṇāṃ tejanārthābhiḥ^2 ^asmabhiḥ^2 śilābhiśca kriyante|
etaiḥ ^kāmārḥ ayaskāraḥ ^hiraṇyavantam ādyaṃ puruṣam ^icchati|
tathā'haṃ svatparistavaṇamicchāmi|
tasmāt ^indo ^indrāya ^pari ^srava ||
kāruhaṃ tato bhiṣagupalaprakṣiṇīṃ nunā |
nānādhiyo vasuyavo'nu gāīva tasthimendrāyendro pariṃ srava ||
3 ||
kāruḥ | aham | tataḥ | bhiṣak | upala'prakṣiṇī | nunā | nānā'dhiyaḥ | vasu'yatraḥ | anu | gāḥ'iva | tasthima | indrāya | indo iti | pariṃ | srava ||3||
ṛṣiḥ pravṛttasomayāgaḥ san daśāpavitrāt some kṣarati pratibandhakapāpāpanuttaye puraścaritānu- kīrtanapuraḥsaramaṣyeṣaṇāṃ cakāra|
tāvat ^ahaṃ ^kāruḥ stomānāṃ kartāsmi |
^tataḥ iti saṃtānanāma |
tanyate'smāditi tataḥ pitā |
tanyate'sāviti vā tataḥ putraḥ |
^bhiṣak bheṣajakṛt |
yajñasya bahvityarthaḥ |
‘sarvaṃ^3 trayyā vidyayā bhidyayati’ iti śruteḥ|
tathā ^upalaprakṣiṇī |
upalepu bālukāsu^ prakṣiṇoti yavān hinasti bhṛjatīti |
yadvā |
dṛṣadādipūpaleṣu bhṛṣṭān yavān hinasti cūrṇayatīti |
athavā dhānāsaktakarambhādīnāṃ kārikā vā|
asau^4 nanā mātā duhitā vā|
namanakriyāyogatvāt |
mātā khalvapatyaṃ prati stanapānādinā namanaśīlā bhavati |
duhitā vā śuśrūṣārtham |
evaṃ sarveṣāṃ paricaraṇena ^nānādhiyaḥ nānākarmāṇaḥ ^vasūyavaḥ dhanakāmā vayam ^anu ^tasthima lokamanvāsthitāḥ smaḥ |
tatra dṛṣṭāntaḥ |
^gāiva |
chāndaso jasaḥ śasādeśaḥ |
gāvo yathā goṣṭhamanutiṣṭhanti svapayaḥpradānena pari- caranti vā evaṃ vayamapi paricarāmaḥ |
etajjñātvā he ^indo soma ^indrāya ^pari ^srava daśā- pavitrāt kṣara |
evamepādhyepaṇā satkārapūrvikā vyāpārā|
atra niruktam— ‘upalaprakṣaṇītyupalepu prakṣiṇātyupalaprakṣepiṇī^5 vā |
kāruhamasmi kartā stomānāṃ tato bhiṣak |
tata iti saṃtānanāma piturvā putrasya vā |
upalaprakṣaṇī^6 satkukārikā nanā namatārmitā vā^ duhitā vā |
nānādhiyo nānākarmāṇo vasūyavo vasukāmā anvāsthitāḥ smo gāṃva iva lokam’ (niru. 6. 5-6) iti ||
aśvo voḻahā sukhaṃ rathaṃ hasanāmupamantraṇaḥ |

2. tara-bha6-iva dhāraṇe |

vyasmin ca vloke vsvaḥ ādityākhyaṃ jyotiḥ vhitaṃ nihitamasti |

yatra rājā vaivasvato yatrāvarodhanaṃ divaḥ | yatrāmūryahatīrāpastatra māmamṛtaṃ kṛdhīndrondo pari srava || 8 || yatra | rājā | vaivasvataḥ | yatra | avarodhanam | divaḥ | yatra | amūḥ | yahatīḥ | āpaḥ | tatra | mām | amṛtam | kṛdhi | indrāya | indo iti | pari | srava ||8||
vyatra yasmiṃlloke vvaivasvataḥ vivasvataputraḥ vrājā bhavati |
vyatra ca loke vdivaḥ ādityasya vavarodhanaṃ bhūtānāṃ praveśanām |
kiṃca vyatra loke vyahatīḥ mahatyaḥ vamūḥ imā gaṅgādyāḥ vāpaḥ tiṣṭhanti |
vtatra tādṛśe loke vmāmamṛtaṃ maraṇadharmarahitaṃ vkṛdhi kuru |
mama devatvaṃ prāpayetyarthaḥ |
tataḥ kāraṇāt vpari vsrava ||
yatrānukamaṃ caraṇaṃ trināke tridive divaḥ | lokā yatra jyotiṣmantastatra māmamṛtaṃ kṛdhīndrondo pari srava || 9 || yatra | anu'kāmam | caraṇam | tri'nāke | tri'dive | divaḥ | lokāḥ | yatra | jyotiṣmantaḥ | tatra | mām | amṛtam | kṛdhi | indrāya | indo iti | pari | srava ||9||

vtatra tādṛśe loke vmāmamṛtaṃ vkṛdhi |
yathā mamottamaloko bhavati tathā tvam vindrāya vpari vsrava ||
yatra kāmā nikāmāśca yatra bradhnasya viṣṭapam | svadhā ca yatra tṛptiśca tatra māmamṛtaṃ kṛdhīndrondo pari srava || 10 || yatra | kāmāḥ | ni'kāmāḥ | ca | yatra | bradhnasya | viṣṭapam | svadhā | ca | yatra | tṛptiḥ | ca | tatra | mām | amṛtam | kṛdhi | indrāya | indo iti | pari | srava ||10||

3. ga-karmaṇi na pravartate |
yatrānandāśca modāśca mudaḥ pramuda āsate | kāmasya yatrāptāḥ kāmāstatra māmamṛtaṃ kṛdhīndrāyendo pari srava || 11 || yatra | ā'nandāḥ | ca | modāḥ | ca | mudaḥ | pra'mudaḥ | āsate | kāmasya | yatra | āptāḥ | kāmāḥ | tatra | mām | amṛtam | kṛdhi | indrāya | indo iti | pari | srava || 11 || yatra yasmi~lloke ānandādayaḥ āsate | teṣāmalaṃgaṃ bhedo draṣṭavyaḥ | yatra ca loke kāmasya kāmyamānasya devasya sarve kāmāḥ āptāḥ prāptā bhavanti tatra māmamṛtaṃ kṛdhi | etat tvayā vinā na ghaṭata iti soma tvaṃ pari srava || 27 || ‘ya indoḥ’ iti caturṛcamekādaśaṃ sūktam | ṛṣyādyāḥ pūrvavat | tathā cānukramyate—‘ya indoścatuṣkam’ iti | gataḥ sūktaviniyogaḥ ||
ya indoḥ parvamānasyu dhāmānyakremīt | tamāhuḥ suprajā iti yastai somāvibhranmana indrāyendo pari srava || 1 || yaḥ | indoḥ | parvamānasya | anu | dhāmāni | akremīt || tam | āhuḥ | su'prajāḥ | iti | yaḥ | te | soma | avinat | manaḥ | indrāya | indo iti | pari | srava || 1 || pavamānasya pūyamānasya indoḥ somasya dhāmāni sthānāni droṇakalaśādīni yadvā dhāmāni tejāṃsi yaḥ brāhmaṇaḥ anu akremīt anukrāmati anugacchati taṃ janaṃ suprajāḥ śobhana- prajananaḥ 1 kalyāṇaputrādiprajāyukta ityāhuḥ | ‘nityamasica prajā metrayoḥ’ ityasic samāsāntaḥ | yaḥ somamabhipuṇoti sa putrādiyukto bhavatītyarthaḥ | kiṃca he soma yaḥ manuṣyaḥ te tvadarthaṃ manaḥ avidhat karoti | yadvā | te tvadīyaṃ mano'vidhat paricarati | vidhatiḥ paricaraṇakarmā | tamapi kalyāṇajananamityāhuḥ 2 | tasmāttvaṃ pari srava ||
ṛṣe mantrakṛtāṃ stomaiḥ kaśyapo'rdhayangiraḥ | somaṃ namasya rājānaṃ yo jajñe vīrūdhāṃ patirindrāyendo pari srava || 2 || ṛṣe | mantra'kṛtām | stomaiḥ | kaśyapaḥ | ut'avardhayat | giraḥ | somam | namasya | rājānam | yaḥ | jajñe | vīrūdhām | patiḥ | indrāya | indo iti | pari | srava || 2 || ṛṣiḥ svātmānaṃ pratyāha | he ṛṣe sūktadraṣṭarhe kaśyapa ātman tvaṃ mantrakṛtāṃ ṛṣīṇāṃ stomaiḥ stotraiḥ giraḥ stutirūpā vācaḥ udvardhayan uparyupari vardhayan rājānaṃ sarveṣāṃ svāminaṃ taṃ somaṃ namasya pūjaya 3 | namasaḥ pūjayāṃ ‘namovariva’ iti kyac | yaḥ somaḥ vīrūdhāṃ vanaspatīnāṃ patiḥ pālakaḥ jajñe jātaḥ taṃ namasya | he soma yathātmanā suto bhavasi tathā pari srava ||

2. tara.3-bhaya-janamityāhuḥ; bha1.2-janayinyāhuḥ; bha6-janamityarthaḥ |
3. ta1.2.5.6.7-bha6-mu-manasā pūjaya; bha7-namasā pūjaya |
sapta diśo nānāsūryāḥ sapta hotār ṛtvijaḥ | devā ādityā ye sapta tebhiḥ somābhi rakṣa na indrāyendo pari srava || 3 || sapta | diśaḥ | nānā'sūryāḥ | sapta | hotārḥ | ṛtvijaḥ | devāḥ | ādityāḥ | ye | sapta | tebhiḥ | soma | abhi | rakṣa | naḥ | indrāya | indo iti | pari | srava || 3 ||
sapta diśaḥ |
somo yasyāṃ diśi vartate taddyatiriktāḥ sapta bhavanti |
nānāsūryāḥ nānāvidhaiḥ sūryairadhiṣṭhitā ṛtavo bhavanti |
‘nānāliṅgasvāhatūnāṃ nānāsūryatvam’ (tai. ā. 1.7) ityāmnānāt |
yadvā |
nānāsūryā iti digviśeṣaṇam |
tathā hotārḥ vaṣaṭkartāro hotrādayaḥ sapta ṛtvijaḥ bhavanti |
kiṃca ādityāḥ aditaḥ putrā dhātrādayo mārtāṇḍavarjitāḥ ye sapta devāḥ santi |
etattu ‘aṣṭau putrāso aditaḥ’ (ṛ. saṃ. 10.72.8) ityatra prapañcayiṣyate |
he soma tebhiḥ tairdigā- dibhiḥ sarvaiḥ naḥ asmān abhi rakṣa |
etattvayā vinā na ghaṭata iti tasmāt indrāya indo pari srava ||
upākaraṇotsarjanayormaṇḍalāntahome ‘yatte rājan’ ityeṣā |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘yatte rājañchṛtaṃ haviriti’ drucāḥ samānī va ākṛtirityekā’ (āśva. gṛ. 3. 5. 7-8) iti ||
yattai rājañchṛtaṃ havistenaṃ somābhi rakṣa naḥ | arātīvā mā nastārīnmo ca naḥ kiṃ canāmamadindrāyendo pari srava || 4 || yat | te | rājan | śrutam | haviḥ | tena | soma | abhi | rakṣa | naḥ | arāti'vā | mā | naḥ | tārīt | mā | mo iti | ca | naḥ | kim | cana | āmamat | indrāya | indo iti | pari | srava || 4 ||
he rājan sarveṣāṃ karmasādhanatvena svāmin he soma te tvadarthaṃ śrataṃ pakvaṃ yat haviḥ asti |
‘śrataṃ pāke’ (pā. sū. 6. 1. 27) iti niṣṭhāyāṃ nipātitaḥ |
tena haviṣā naḥ asmān abhi rakṣa |
abhipālaya |
tasmāttvadabhirakṣitānasmān arātīvā arātitvavānśatruḥ naḥ asmān mā tārīt mā vadhīt |
kiṃca naḥ asmākaṃ kiṃ cana kiṃcidapi dhanādikaṃ śatruḥ mo āmamat mā hiṃmīt |
yadetadaitaiḥ sūktairuktaṃ tadyathāsmākaṃ bhavati tathā he indo indrāya pari srava |
evaṃ ‘svādiṣṭhayā’ ityādibhiretadantaiḥ sūktairbahuvidhaṃ somamāhāryamabhyadhāyi tasmādaihikā- muṣmikaphalasiddhaye somayāgaḥ karaṇīya ityuktaṃ bhavati ||
28 ||
iti sāyaṇācāryaviracite mādhavīye vedārthaprakāśe dāśatayyā navame maṇḍale saptamo'nuvākaḥ samāptaṃ ca navamaṃ maṇḍalam ||
